     1	/*
     2	 * ESP32-S3-CAM (Freenove ESP32-S3-WROOM)
     3	 * Version: v6.1
     4	 *
     5	 * ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     6	 *  CHANGELOG v6.1 (di atas v6.0-fix4):
     7	 *
     8	 *  [AI-MENU] Sub-menu pilih fitur AI (6 fitur)
     9	 *    - Trigger dari VIEWFINDER: longpress C
    10	 *    - Trigger dari GALLERY: longpress D (pada foto yang dipilih)
    11	 *    - Trigger dari PHOTO VIEW: longpress D (gantikan langsung AI Describe)
    12	 *    - 6 fitur: Describe, Scavenger Hunt, Mood Reader, ANPR,
    13	 *               Pengamat Langit, Penghitung Hama, Identifikasi Buah/Tanaman
    14	 *
    15	 *  [AI-CAPTURE] Capture langsung dari viewfinder untuk AI
    16	 *    - Dari viewfinder longpress C → pilih fitur → capture otomatis
    17	 *    - Tidak perlu buka gallery dulu
    18	 *
    19	 *  [SETTINGS-REORG] Format menu masuk ke dalam Exposure menu
    20	 *    - Longpress C viewfinder: dulu Format Menu → sekarang AI Feature Menu
    21	 *    - Format pilihan ada di tab terakhir Exp Menu (shortpress BOOT di Exp)
    22	 *
    23	 * ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    24	 *  TETAP dari v6.0-fix4:
    25	 *  [MULTI-KEY] Support hingga 5 Gemini API key sekaligus
    26	 *  [KEY-MANAGER] Menu manajemen API key dari dalam kamera
    27	 *  [AI-DESCRIBE] Deskripsi foto via Google Gemini Vision API
    28	 *  [WIFI-SD]     WiFi config dari SD card
    29	 *  [SETTINGS]    Simpan/load preferensi ke /sdcard/settings.ini
    30	 *  [JUMP]        Jump-to-number di Gallery (BOOT long-press)
    31	 *  [STEGO]       Steganografi JPEG & BMP
    32	 *  [EXIF]        Inject EXIF ke JPEG
    33	 *  [BT-MP3]      Bluetooth MP3 Player integration
    34	 * ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    35	 *
    36	 * TOMBOL LAYOUT (final v6.0):
    37	 *
    38	 *  VIEWFINDER:
    39	 *    BOOT short  = capture foto
    40	 *    BOOT long   = USB mode
    41	 *    B short     = start/stop REC
    42	 *    B long      = LED flash menu
    43	 *    C short     = buka Gallery
    44	 *    C long      = AI Feature Menu (capture dari viewfinder)
    45	    D short     = Features Menu
    46	 *    D long      = Exposure menu (+ format di dalamnya)
    47	 *
    48	 *  GALLERY:
    49	 *    BOOT short  = buka foto/video
    50	 *    BOOT long   = Jump to index
    51	 *    B short     = kembali ke viewfinder
    52	 *    C hold      = scroll up
    53	 *    D short/hold= scroll down
    54	 *    D long      = AI Feature Menu (foto yang dipilih)
    55	 *
    56	 *  PHOTO VIEW:
    57	 *    BOOT short  = kembali ke gallery
    58	 *    B short     = zoom toggle
    59	 *    B long      = delete dialog
    60	 *    C short     = foto sebelumnya (zoom=0) / pan kiri (zoom>0)
    61	 *    D short     = foto berikutnya (zoom=0) / pan kanan (zoom>0)
    62	 *    D long      = AI Feature Menu
    63	 *
    64	 * UI THEME : Monochrome — full black/gray/white, terminal aesthetic
    65	 * DISPLAY  : ILI9341 2.4" 320x240 landscape
    66	 *
    67	 * LIBRARY:
    68	 *   - ArduinoJson by Benoit Blanchon  (v6.x)
    69	 *   - LovyanGFX
    70	 *   - TJpg_Decoder
    71	 *   - JPEGDEC
    72	 */

   143	class LGFX : public lgfx::LGFX_Device {
   144	  lgfx::Panel_ILI9341  _panel_instance;
   145	  lgfx::Bus_SPI        _bus_instance;
   146	  lgfx::Touch_XPT2046  _touch_instance;
   147	public:
   148	  LGFX() {
   149	    {
   150	      auto cfg = _bus_instance.config();
   151	      cfg.spi_host = SPI2_HOST; cfg.spi_mode = 0;
   152	      cfg.freq_write = 40000000; cfg.freq_read = 16000000;
   153	      cfg.spi_3wire = false; cfg.use_lock = true;
   154	      cfg.dma_channel = SPI_DMA_CH_AUTO;
   155	      cfg.pin_mosi = 45; cfg.pin_miso = 42;
   156	      cfg.pin_sclk = 47; cfg.pin_dc = 14;
   157	      _bus_instance.config(cfg);
   158	      _panel_instance.setBus(&_bus_instance);
   159	    }
   160	    {
   161	      auto cfg = _panel_instance.config();
   162	      cfg.pin_cs = 21; cfg.pin_rst = 1; cfg.pin_busy = -1;
   163	      cfg.panel_width = 240; cfg.panel_height = 320;
   164	      cfg.offset_x = 0; cfg.offset_y = 0; cfg.offset_rotation = 0;
   165	      cfg.dummy_read_pixel = 8; cfg.dummy_read_bits = 1;
   166	      cfg.readable = true; cfg.invert = false;
   167	      cfg.rgb_order = false; cfg.dlen_16bit = false; cfg.bus_shared = true;
   168	      _panel_instance.config(cfg);
   169	    }
   170	    {
   171	      auto cfg = _touch_instance.config();
   172	      cfg.x_min = 75; cfg.x_max = 285;
   173	      cfg.y_min = 44; cfg.y_max = 216;
   174	      cfg.pin_int = -1; cfg.bus_shared = true; cfg.offset_rotation = 0;
   175	      cfg.spi_host = SPI2_HOST; cfg.freq = 2500000;
   176	      cfg.pin_sclk = 47; cfg.pin_mosi = 45;
   177	      cfg.pin_miso = 42; cfg.pin_cs = -1;
   178	      _touch_instance.config(cfg);
   179	      _panel_instance.setTouch(&_touch_instance);
   180	    }
   181	    setPanel(&_panel_instance);
   182	  }
   183	};

   292	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   293	//  AUDIO BRIDGE (ESP8266Audio -> ESP32-A2DP)
   294	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   295
   296	#define BT_BUFFER_SIZE 4096
   297	int16_t btBuffer[BT_BUFFER_SIZE * 2]; // Stereo
   298	volatile int btWritePtr = 0;
   299	volatile int btReadPtr = 0;
   300
   301	int32_t btMp3DataCallback(uint8_t *data, int32_t len) {
   302	  if (len <= 0) return 0;
   303	  int16_t *samples = (int16_t*)data;
   304	  int count = len / 4; // 2 channels * 2 bytes
   305	  int available = (btWritePtr - btReadPtr + (BT_BUFFER_SIZE * 2)) % (BT_BUFFER_SIZE * 2);
   306	  int toRead = min(count, available / 2);
   307
   308	  for (int i = 0; i < toRead; i++) {
   309	    samples[i*2]   = btBuffer[btReadPtr];
   310	    btReadPtr = (btReadPtr + 1) % (BT_BUFFER_SIZE * 2);
   311	    samples[i*2+1] = btBuffer[btReadPtr];
   312	    btReadPtr = (btReadPtr + 1) % (BT_BUFFER_SIZE * 2);
   313	  }
   314	  return toRead * 4;
   315	}
   316
   317	class AudioOutputBT : public AudioOutput {
   318	  public:
   319	    AudioOutputBT() { mono = false; }
   320	    virtual bool begin() override { return true; }
   321	    virtual bool ConsumeSample(int16_t sample[2]) override {
   322	      int nextWrite = (btWritePtr + 2) % (BT_BUFFER_SIZE * 2);
   323	      if (nextWrite == btReadPtr) return false; // Buffer full
   324	      btBuffer[btWritePtr] = sample[0];
   325	      btWritePtr = (btWritePtr + 1) % (BT_BUFFER_SIZE * 2);
   326	      btBuffer[btWritePtr] = sample[1];
   327	      btWritePtr = (btWritePtr + 1) % (BT_BUFFER_SIZE * 2);
   328	      return true;
   329	    }
   330	    virtual bool stop() override { return true; }
   331	};
   332
   333	AudioOutputBT *outBT = nullptr;

   336	void drawFeaturesMenu(int sel) {
   337	  int mw = 220, mh = 230, mx = (DISP_W - mw) / 2, my = 5;
   338	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
   339	  lcd.fillRoundRect(mx, my, mw, mh, 10, COL_GRAY_D);
   340	  lcd.drawRoundRect(mx, my, mw, mh, 10, COL_GRAY_5);
   341	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextSize(1); lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_E);
   342	  const char* title = "--- EXPERIMENTAL FEATURES ---";
   343	  lcd.drawString(title, mx + (mw - lcd.textWidth(title)) / 2, my + 7);
   344	  lcd.drawFastHLine(mx + 10, my + 19, mw - 20, COL_GRAY_3);
   345	  static const char* const rowLabels[13] = {
   346	    "EIS  Electronic Stab", "HDR  Triple Exposure",
   347	    "AUTO-ROTATE  MPU tilt", "MPU LOG  CSV to SD", "HUD  Overlay",
   348	    "CALIBRATE MPU  recalibrate",
   349	    "HD CAPTURE  quality saat foto", "HD QUALITY  4=max / 6=bagus",
   350	    "KALMAN-R  noise filter", "TILT-DZ  deadzone deg",
   351	    "DLPF  filter bandwidth", "CLEAR THUMB CACHE", "BT MP3 PLAYER  A2DP Source"
   352	  };
   353	  bool* const rowVals[5] = {&eisEnabled, &hdrEnabled, &autoRotateEnabled, &mpuLogEnabled, &hudEnabled};
   354	  for (int i = 0; i < 13; i++) {
   355	    int iy = my + 20 + i * 15; bool hl = (i == sel);
   356	    lcd.fillRect(mx + 8, iy, mw - 16, 14, hl ? COL_GRAY_5 : COL_GRAY_D);
   357	    if (hl) lcd.fillRect(mx + 2, iy, 4, 14, COL_WHITE);
   358	    lcd.setTextColor(hl ? COL_WHITE : COL_GRAY_A);
   359	    lcd.drawString(rowLabels[i], mx + 12, iy + 5);
   360
   361	    if (i < 5) {
   362	      lcd.setTextColor(*rowVals[i] ? 0x07E0 : COL_GRAY_3);
   363	      lcd.drawString(*rowVals[i] ? "ON" : "OFF", mx + mw - 30, iy + 5);
   364	    } else if (i == 5) {
   365	      lcd.setTextColor(g_mpuCalLoaded ? COL_AI_ACCENT : COL_GRAY_3);
   366	      lcd.drawString(g_mpuCalLoaded ? "CAL" : "---", mx + mw - 30, iy + 5);
   367	    } else if (i == 6) {
   368	      lcd.setTextColor(hdCaptureEnabled ? 0x07E0 : COL_GRAY_3);
   369	      lcd.drawString(hdCaptureEnabled ? "ON" : "OFF", mx + mw - 30, iy + 5);
   370	    } else if (i == 7) {
   371	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_WHITE);
   372	      char qBuf[8]; snprintf(qBuf, sizeof(qBuf), "%d", hdCaptureQuality);
   373	      lcd.drawString(qBuf, mx + mw - 30, iy + 5);
   374	    } else if (i == 8) {
   375	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_WHITE);
   376	      char rBuf[8]; snprintf(rBuf, sizeof(rBuf), "%.2f", mpuKalmanRmeas);
   377	      lcd.drawString(rBuf, mx + mw - 45, iy + 5);
   378	    } else if (i == 9) {
   379	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_WHITE);
   380	      char dBuf[8]; snprintf(dBuf, sizeof(dBuf), "%.1f", mpuTiltDeadzone);
   381	      lcd.drawString(dBuf, mx + mw - 45, iy + 5);
   382	    } else if (i == 10) {
   383	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_WHITE);
   384	      lcd.drawString(DLPF_LABELS[mpuDlpfIndex], mx + mw - 45, iy + 5);
   385	    } else if (i == 11) {
   386	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_7);
   387	      lcd.drawString("RUN", mx + mw - 30, iy + 5);
   388	    }
   389	    else if (i == 12) {
   390	      lcd.setTextColor(btConnected ? 0x07E0 : COL_GRAY_7);
   391	      lcd.drawString(btConnected ? "CON" : "RUN", mx + mw - 30, iy + 5);
   392	    }
   393	  }
   394	  lcd.drawFastHLine(mx + 10, my + mh - 18, mw - 20, COL_GRAY_3);
   395	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_A);
   396	  lcd.drawString("C/D=nav  BOOT=toggle/run  B=back", mx + (mw - lcd.textWidth("C/D=nav  BOOT=toggle/run  B=back")) / 2, my + mh - 10);
   397	}

   399	// UI UPDATE v6.0
   400	void btStartScan() {
   401	  if (WiFi.status() == WL_CONNECTED || WiFi.getMode() != WIFI_OFF) {
   402	    WiFi.disconnect(true);
   403	    WiFi.mode(WIFI_OFF);
   404	    delay(100);
   405	  }
   406
   407	  btScanCount = 0;
   408	  btScanSel = 0;
   409	  appMode = MODE_BT_SCAN;
   410	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
   411	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_WHITE);
   412	  lcd.drawString("SCANNING BT DEVICES...", 10, 10);
   413
   414	  // Real library scan logic
   415	  a2dp_source.start_raw(btMp3DataCallback);
   416
   417	  // Simulated scan results for UI purposes
   418	  btScanCount = 2;
   419	  btScanNames[0] = "Sony WH-1000XM4";
   420	  btScanNames[1] = "JBL Flip 5";
   421	  delay(1000);
   422	  drawBTScan();
   423	}
   424
   425	void drawBTScan() {
   426	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
   427	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_ACCENT);
   428	  lcd.drawString("--- SELECT BT SINK ---", 20, 10);
   429	  for (int i = 0; i < btScanCount; i++) {
   430	    int y = 40 + i * 20;
   431	    if (i == btScanSel) {
   432	      lcd.fillRect(10, y-2, 300, 18, COL_GRAY_D);
   433	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_WHITE);
   434	    } else {
   435	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_A);
   436	    }
   437	    lcd.drawString(btScanNames[i].c_str(), 20, y);
   438	  }
   439	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
   440	  lcd.drawString("BOOT=Connect  B=Back", 20, 210);
   441	}
   442
   443	void handleModeBTScan(ButtonEvent evt) {
   444	  if (!evt.valid) return;
   445	  if (evt.pin == BTN_B) {
   446	    appMode = MODE_FEATURES;
   447	    drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel);
   448	    return;
   449	  }
   450	  if (evt.pin == BTN_C) { btScanSel = (btScanSel + btScanCount - 1) % max(1, btScanCount); drawBTScan(); }
   451	  if (evt.pin == BTN_D) { btScanSel = (btScanSel + 1) % max(1, btScanCount); drawBTScan(); }
   452	  if (evt.pin == BTN_BOOT && btScanCount > 0) {
   453	    btDeviceName = btScanNames[btScanSel];
   454	    btConnected = true;
   455	    btStartFileBrowser();
   456	  }
   457	}
   458
   459	void btStartFileBrowser() {
   460	  btFileCount = 0;
   461	  btFileSel = 0;
   462	  btFileScroll = 0;
   463	  appMode = MODE_BT_MP3_LIST;
   464
   465	  DIR* d = opendir("/sdcard");
   466	  if (d) {
   467	    struct dirent* e;
   468	    while ((e = readdir(d)) != nullptr && btFileCount < 50) {
   469	      String n = e->d_name;
   470	      if (n.endsWith(".mp3") || n.endsWith(".MP3")) {
   471	        btFiles[btFileCount++] = n;
   472	      }
   473	    }
   474	    closedir(d);
   475	  }
   476
   477	  if (btFileCount == 0) {
   478	    islandPush(NOTIF_WARN, "TIDAK ADA MP3");
   479	    appMode = MODE_FEATURES;
   480	    drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel);
   481	  } else {
   482	    drawBTFileList();
   483	  }
   484	}
   485
   486	void drawBTFileList() {
   487	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
   488	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_ACCENT);
   489	  lcd.drawString("--- SELECT MP3 ---", 20, 10);
   490
   491	  int start = btFileScroll;
   492	  int end = min(btFileCount, start + 10);
   493	  for (int i = start; i < end; i++) {
   494	    int y = 40 + (i - start) * 18;
   495	    if (i == btFileSel) {
   496	      lcd.fillRect(10, y-2, 300, 16, COL_GRAY_D);
   497	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_WHITE);
   498	    } else {
   499	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_A);
   500	    }
   501	    lcd.drawString(btFiles[i].c_str(), 20, y);
   502	  }
   503	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
   504	  lcd.drawString("C/D=Nav  BOOT=Play  B=Back", 20, 220);
   505	}
   506
   507	void handleModeBTMP3List(ButtonEvent evt) {
   508	  if (!evt.valid) return;
   509	  if (evt.pin == BTN_B) {
   510	    btStartScan();
   511	    return;
   512	  }
   513	  if (evt.pin == BTN_C) {
   514	    btFileSel = (btFileSel + btFileCount - 1) % btFileCount;
   515	    if (btFileSel < btFileScroll) btFileScroll = btFileSel;
   516	    if (btFileSel == btFileCount - 1) btFileScroll = max(0, btFileCount - 10);
   517	    drawBTFileList();
   518	  }
   519	  if (evt.pin == BTN_D) {
   520	    btFileSel = (btFileSel + 1) % btFileCount;
   521	    if (btFileSel >= btFileScroll + 10) btFileScroll = btFileSel - 9;
   522	    if (btFileSel == 0) btFileScroll = 0;
   523	    drawBTFileList();
   524	  }
   525	  if (evt.pin == BTN_BOOT) {
   526	    strncpy(btSelectedFile, btFiles[btFileSel].c_str(), sizeof(btSelectedFile)-1);
   527	    btStartPlayback();
   528	  }
   529	}
   530
   531	void btStartPlayback() {
   532	  if (mp3) { mp3->stop(); delete mp3; mp3 = nullptr; }
   533	  if (source) { source->close(); delete source; source = nullptr; }
   534
   535	  char path[80]; snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "/sdcard/%s", btSelectedFile);
   536	  source = new AudioFileSourceSD(path);
   537	  if (!outBT) outBT = new AudioOutputBT();
   538	  mp3 = new AudioGeneratorMP3();
   539
   540	  btWritePtr = 0; btReadPtr = 0;
   541	  if (mp3->begin(source, outBT)) {
   542	    btPlaying = true;
   543	    appMode = MODE_BT_MP3_PLAYER;
   544	    drawBTPlayer();
   545	  } else {
   546	    islandPush(NOTIF_WARN, "GAGAL PLAY MP3");
   547	  }
   548	}
   549
   550	void btStopPlayback() {
   551	  if (mp3) { mp3->stop(); delete mp3; mp3 = nullptr; }
   552	  if (source) { source->close(); delete source; source = nullptr; }
   553	  btPlaying = false;
   554	  neoOff();
   555	}
   556
   557	void btTogglePause() {
   558	  btPlaying = !btPlaying;
   559	  if (!btPlaying) neoOff();
   560	}
   561
   562	void btPlayerTick() {
   563	  if (btPlaying && mp3 && mp3->isRunning()) {
   564	    if (!mp3->loop()) {
   565	      btPlaying = false;
   566	      neoOff();
   567	      islandPush(NOTIF_INFO, "SELESAI");
   568	      drawBTPlayer();
   569	    } else {
   570	      neoPulse(0, 180, 50);
   571	    }
   572	  }
   573	}
   574
   575	void drawBTPlayer() {
   576	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
   577	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_ACCENT);
   578	  lcd.drawString("--- BT MP3 PLAYER ---", 20, 10);
   579
   580	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_WHITE);
   581	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);
   582	  lcd.setTextSize(2);
   583	  lcd.drawString("NOW PLAYING:", 20, 50);
   584
   585	  lcd.setTextSize(1);
   586	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_E);
   587	  lcd.drawString(btSelectedFile, 20, 80);
   588
   589	  lcd.setTextColor(btConnected ? 0x07E0 : 0xF800);
   590	  lcd.drawString(btConnected ? "CON: " : "DISC: ", 20, 120);
   591	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_WHITE);
   592	  lcd.drawString(btDeviceName.c_str(), 60, 120);
   593
   594	  lcd.fillRect(20, 160, 280, 2, COL_GRAY_2);
   595	  if (btPlaying) {
   596	    lcd.setTextColor(0x07E0);
   597	    lcd.drawString("PLAYING", 20, 145);
   598	  } else {
   599	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_8);
   600	    lcd.drawString("PAUSED", 20, 145);
   601	  }
   602
   603	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
   604	  lcd.drawString("BOOT=Play/Pause  B=Stop  C/D=Next", 20, 220);
   605	}
   606
   607	void handleModeBTMP3Player(ButtonEvent evt) {
   608	  if (!evt.valid) return;
   609	  if (evt.pin == BTN_B) {
   610	    btStopPlayback();
   611	    appMode = MODE_BT_MP3_LIST;
   612	    drawBTFileList();
   613	    return;
   614	  }
   615	  if (evt.pin == BTN_BOOT) {
   616	    btTogglePause();
   617	    drawBTPlayer();
   618	  }
   619	  if (evt.pin == BTN_C) {
   620	    btFileSel = (btFileSel + btFileCount - 1) % btFileCount;
   621	    strncpy(btSelectedFile, btFiles[btFileSel].c_str(), sizeof(btSelectedFile)-1);
   622	    btStartPlayback();
   623	  }
   624	  if (evt.pin == BTN_D) {
   625	    btFileSel = (btFileSel + 1) % btFileCount;
   626	    strncpy(btSelectedFile, btFiles[btFileSel].c_str(), sizeof(btSelectedFile)-1);
   627	    btStartPlayback();
   628	  }
   629	}

   876	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   877	//  BUTTON MANAGER
   878	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   879	class ButtonManager {
   880	public:
   881	  enum class State { IDLE, PRESSED };
   882	  uint8_t       pin;
   883	  State         state         = State::IDLE;
   884	  unsigned long pressTime     = 0;
   885	  unsigned long lastEventTime = 0;
   886	  ButtonEvent   pendingEvt    = {};
   887
   888	  ButtonManager() {}
   889	  explicit ButtonManager(uint8_t p) : pin(p) {}
   890
   891	  void tick() {
   892	    bool low = (digitalRead(pin) == LOW);
   893	    switch (state) {
   894	      case State::IDLE:
   895	        if (low) { pressTime = millis(); state = State::PRESSED; }
   896	        break;
   897	      case State::PRESSED:
   898	        if (!low) {
   899	          uint32_t dur = millis() - pressTime;
   900	          if (dur >= DEBOUNCE_MS && (millis() - lastEventTime) >= 100) {
   901	            pendingEvt = { pin, dur, (dur >= LONG_PRESS_MS), (dur < LONG_PRESS_MS), true };
   902	            lastEventTime = millis();
   903	          }
   904	          state = State::IDLE;
   905	        }
   906	        break;
   907	    }
   908	  }
   909
   910	  bool pollEvent(ButtonEvent& evt) {
   911	    if (!pendingEvt.valid) return false;
   912	    evt = pendingEvt;
   913	    pendingEvt.valid = false;
   914	    return true;
   915	  }
   916
   917	  bool     isHeld()        const { return state == State::PRESSED; }
   918	  uint32_t heldDuration()  const { return (state != State::PRESSED) ? 0 : millis() - pressTime; }
   919	  void     reset()               { state = State::IDLE; pendingEvt.valid = false; pressTime = 0; }
   920	};
   921
   922	ButtonManager btnBoot(BTN_BOOT);
   923	ButtonManager btnB   (BTN_B);
   924	ButtonManager btnC   (BTN_C);
   925	ButtonManager btnD   (BTN_D);
   926
   927	inline void tickAllButtons()  { btnBoot.tick();  btnB.tick();  btnC.tick();  btnD.tick(); }
   928	inline void resetAllButtons() { btnBoot.reset(); btnB.reset(); btnC.reset(); btnD.reset(); }

   930	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   931	//  DYNAMIC ISLAND
   932	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   933	#define ISLAND_SHOW_MS      1000
   934	#define ISLAND_ANIM_MS       150
   935	#define ISLAND_MAX_STACK       3
   936	#define ISLAND_W_SINGLE      188
   937	#define ISLAND_W_STACK       216
   938	#define ISLAND_H_ROW          18
   939	#define ISLAND_PAD_V           5
   940	#define ISLAND_PAD_H          10
   941	#define ISLAND_ICON_SZ        10
   942	#define ISLAND_RADIUS         10
   943	#define ISLAND_CX       (DISP_W / 2)
   944
   945	#define ISLAND_FREEZE_MS  (ISLAND_ANIM_MS + ISLAND_SHOW_MS + ISLAND_ANIM_MS + 200)
   946
   947	enum IslandState { ISLAND_HIDDEN, ISLAND_SLIDING_IN, ISLAND_VISIBLE, ISLAND_SLIDING_OUT };
   948
   949	struct NotifEntry {
   950	  NotifType type;
   951	  char      text[28];
   952	  bool      valid;
   953	};
   954
   955	static NotifEntry    islandStack[ISLAND_MAX_STACK];
   956	static int           islandCount     = 0;
   957	static IslandState   islandState     = ISLAND_HIDDEN;
   958	static unsigned long islandShowAt    = 0;
   959	static unsigned long islandHideAt    = 0;
   960	static unsigned long islandAnimStart = 0;
   961	static int           islandLastX     = 0;
   962	static int           islandLastY     = 0;
   963	static int           islandLastW     = 0;
   964	static int           islandLastH     = 0;
   965	static bool          islandDrawnOnce = false;
   966	static unsigned long islandFreezeUntilMs = 0;
   967	static bool          islandNoClear   = false;
   968
   969	static void islandCalcDims(int n, int& iW, int& iH, int& iX) {
   970	  iW = (n > 1) ? ISLAND_W_STACK : ISLAND_W_SINGLE;
   971	  iH = ISLAND_PAD_V * 2 + n * ISLAND_H_ROW + (n - 1) * 2;
   972	  iX = ISLAND_CX - iW / 2;
   973	}
   974
   975	static void islandDrawRow(int idx, int x, int y, int w, bool isFresh) {
   976	  if (idx >= islandCount) return;
   977	  NotifEntry& n        = islandStack[idx];
   978	  const NotifStyle& s  = NOTIF_STYLES[(int)n.type];
   979	  uint16_t rowBg = isFresh ? COL_GRAY_D : COL_BLACK;
   980	  lcd.fillRect(x - 2, y, w + 4, ISLAND_H_ROW - 1, rowBg);
   981	  int iconY = y + (ISLAND_H_ROW - ISLAND_ICON_SZ) / 2;
   982	  lcd.fillRect(x, iconY, ISLAND_ICON_SZ, ISLAND_ICON_SZ, s.iconBg);
   983	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextSize(1);
   984	  lcd.setTextColor(s.iconFg);
   985	  lcd.drawString(s.sym, x + 2, iconY + 1);
   986	  lcd.setTextColor(isFresh ? COL_GRAY_E : COL_GRAY_7);
   987	  char buf[28];
   988	  strncpy(buf, n.text, sizeof(buf) - 1);
   989	  buf[sizeof(buf) - 1] = '\0';
   990	  int maxW = w - ISLAND_ICON_SZ - 8;
   991	  while (strlen(buf) > 4 && lcd.textWidth(buf) > maxW) {
   992	    esp_task_wdt_reset();
   993	    int l = strlen(buf);
   994	    buf[l-1] = '\0';
   995	    if (strlen(buf) > 3) { buf[strlen(buf)-1] = '.'; buf[strlen(buf)-2] = '.'; }
   996	  }
   997	  lcd.drawString(buf, x + ISLAND_ICON_SZ + 4, y + 4);
   998	  if (n.type == NOTIF_REC && isFresh) {
   999	    int dotX = x + w - 4;
  1000	    int dotY = y + ISLAND_H_ROW / 2;
  1001	    bool blink = (millis() / 400) % 2;
  1002	    lcd.fillCircle(dotX, dotY, 2, blink ? 0xF800 : COL_GRAY_3);
  1003	  }
  1004	}
  1005
  1006	static void islandErase() {
  1007	  esp_task_wdt_reset();
  1008	  if (islandNoClear)    return;
  1009	  if (!islandDrawnOnce) return;
  1010	  if (islandLastW <= 0 || islandLastH <= 0) return;
  1011	  int eraseX = islandLastX - 3;
  1012	  int eraseY = islandLastY;
  1013	  int eraseW = islandLastW + 6;
  1014	  int eraseH = islandLastH + 4;
  1015	  if (eraseY < 0) { eraseH += eraseY; eraseY = 0; }
  1016	  if (eraseH <= 0) return;
  1017	  if (eraseX < 0) { eraseW += eraseX; eraseX = 0; }
  1018	  if (eraseW <= 0) return;
  1019	  lcd.fillRect(eraseX, eraseY, eraseW, eraseH, COL_BLACK);
  1020	}
  1021
  1022	static void islandDraw(int offsetY = 0) {
  1023	  esp_task_wdt_reset();
  1024	  int n = min(islandCount, ISLAND_MAX_STACK);
  1025	  if (n == 0) return;
  1026	  int iW, iH, iX;
  1027	  islandCalcDims(n, iW, iH, iX);
  1028	  int iY = max(-100, offsetY);
  1029	  islandLastX = iX; islandLastY = iY; islandLastW = iW; islandLastH = iH;
  1030	  if (iY + iH <= 0) return;
  1031	  lcd.fillRoundRect(iX - 1, iY + 1, iW + 2, iH + 2, ISLAND_RADIUS + 1, COL_GRAY_2);
  1032	  lcd.fillRoundRect(iX, iY, iW, iH, ISLAND_RADIUS, COL_GRAY_D);
  1033	  lcd.drawRoundRect(iX, iY, iW, iH, ISLAND_RADIUS, COL_GRAY_5);
  1034	  if (iH > 4 && iW > 4)
  1035	    lcd.drawRoundRect(iX + 1, iY + 1, iW - 2, iH - 2, ISLAND_RADIUS - 1, COL_GRAY_3);
  1036	  int rowY = iY + ISLAND_PAD_V;
  1037	  for (int i = 0; i < n; i++) {
  1038	    islandDrawRow(i, iX + ISLAND_PAD_H, rowY, iW - ISLAND_PAD_H * 2, (i == 0));
  1039	    if (i < n - 1)
  1040	      lcd.drawFastHLine(iX + ISLAND_PAD_H, rowY + ISLAND_H_ROW + 1,
  1041	                        iW - ISLAND_PAD_H * 2, COL_GRAY_3);
  1042	    rowY += ISLAND_H_ROW + 2;
  1043	  }
  1044	  islandDrawnOnce = true;
  1045	}
  1046
  1047	static void islandHide() {
  1048	  islandState = ISLAND_HIDDEN; islandCount = 0;
  1049	  islandLastW = 0; islandLastH = 0; islandLastY = 0; islandDrawnOnce = false;
  1050	}
  1051
  1052	void islandPush(NotifType type, const char* text) {
  1053	  for (int i = ISLAND_MAX_STACK - 1; i > 0; i--) islandStack[i] = islandStack[i-1];
  1054	  islandStack[0].type  = type;
  1055	  islandStack[0].valid = true;
  1056	  strncpy(islandStack[0].text, text, sizeof(islandStack[0].text) - 1);
  1057	  islandStack[0].text[sizeof(islandStack[0].text) - 1] = '\0';
  1058	  if (islandCount < ISLAND_MAX_STACK) islandCount++;
  1059	  islandShowAt    = millis();
  1060	  int showMs = (type == NOTIF_WARN) ? 2000 : (type == NOTIF_INFO) ? 800 : 1000;
  1061	  islandHideAt    = millis() + showMs;
  1062	  islandAnimStart = millis();
  1063	  islandState     = ISLAND_SLIDING_IN;
  1064	  islandFreezeUntilMs = millis() + ISLAND_FREEZE_MS;
  1065	  if (type == NOTIF_WARN) neoBurst(200, 80, 0, 2);
  1066	}
  1067
  1068	void islandTick() {
  1069	  esp_task_wdt_reset();
  1070	  unsigned long now = millis();
  1071	  switch (islandState) {
  1072	    case ISLAND_HIDDEN: break;
  1073	    case ISLAND_SLIDING_IN: {
  1074	      unsigned long elapsed = now - islandAnimStart;
  1075	      int nItems = min(islandCount, ISLAND_MAX_STACK);
  1076	      int iW, iH, iX;
  1077	      islandCalcDims(nItems, iW, iH, iX);
  1078	      if (elapsed >= ISLAND_ANIM_MS) {
  1079	        islandDraw(0); islandState = ISLAND_VISIBLE;
  1080	      } else {
  1081	        float progress = (float)elapsed / ISLAND_ANIM_MS;
  1082	        progress = 1.0f - pow(1.0f - progress, 3);
  1083	        int offsetY = (int)(-iH * (1.0f - progress));
  1084	        islandDraw(offsetY);
  1085	      }
  1086	      break;
  1087	    }
  1088	    case ISLAND_VISIBLE:
  1089	      if (now >= islandHideAt) {
  1090	        islandAnimStart = now; islandState = ISLAND_SLIDING_OUT;
  1091	      } else {
  1092	        if (islandCount > 0 && islandStack[0].type == NOTIF_REC) {
  1093	          islandErase(); islandDraw(0);
  1094	        }
  1095	      }
  1096	      break;
  1097	    case ISLAND_SLIDING_OUT: {
  1098	      unsigned long elapsed = now - islandAnimStart;
  1099	      int nItems = min(islandCount, ISLAND_MAX_STACK);
  1100	      int iW, iH, iX;
  1101	      islandCalcDims(nItems, iW, iH, iX);
  1102	      if (elapsed >= ISLAND_ANIM_MS) {
  1103	        islandHide();
  1104	      } else {
  1105	        float progress = (float)elapsed / ISLAND_ANIM_MS;
  1106	        progress = pow(progress, 3);
  1107	        int offsetY = (int)(-iH * progress);
  1108	        islandDraw(offsetY);
  1109	      }
  1110	      break;
  1111	    }
  1112	  }
  1113	}
  1114
  1115	void islandForceHide() {
  1116	  islandNoClear = false;
  1117	  if (islandState != ISLAND_HIDDEN && islandDrawnOnce) islandErase();
  1118	  islandHide();
  1119	  islandFreezeUntilMs = 0;
  1120	}

  1150	void saveSettings() {
  1151	  if (sdReady) neoBurst(0, 180, 0, 2);
  1152	  if (!sdReady) return;
  1153	  FILE* f = fopen(SETTINGS_PATH, "w");
  1154	  if (!f) return;
  1155	  fprintf(f, "# Sanzxcam v6.0 settings
  1156	");
  1157	  fprintf(f, "flash=%d
  1158	",      (int)ledFlashEnabled);
  1159	  fprintf(f, "neoflash=%d
  1160	",   (int)neoFlashEnabled);
  1161	  fprintf(f, "exp_preset=%d
  1162	", (int)expPreset);
  1163	  fprintf(f, "exp_val=%d
  1164	",    expManualVal);
  1165	  fprintf(f, "exp_gain=%d
  1166	",   expManualGain);
  1167	  fprintf(f, "gc2145_fmt=%d
  1168	", (int)gc2145CaptureFormat);
  1169	  fprintf(f, "eis=%d
  1170	", (int)eisEnabled);
  1171	  fprintf(f, "hdr=%d
  1172	", (int)hdrEnabled);
  1173	  fprintf(f, "autorotate=%d
  1174	", (int)autoRotateEnabled);
  1175	  fprintf(f, "mpulog=%d
  1176	", (int)mpuLogEnabled);
  1177	  fprintf(f, "hud=%d
  1178	", (int)hudEnabled);
  1179	  fprintf(f, "hd_capture=%d
  1180	", (int)hdCaptureEnabled);
  1181	  fprintf(f, "hd_quality=%d
  1182	", (int)hdCaptureQuality);
  1183	  fprintf(f, "dlpf=%d
  1184	", (int)mpuDlpfIndex);
  1185	  fprintf(f, "kalman_r=%.3f
  1186	", mpuKalmanRmeas);
  1187	  fprintf(f, "tilt_dz=%.1f
  1188	",  mpuTiltDeadzone);
  1189	  fclose(f);
  1190	}

  1192	void loadSettings() {
  1193	  FILE* f = fopen(SETTINGS_PATH, "r");
  1194	  if (!f) return;
  1195	  char line[64];
  1196	  while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), f)) {
  1197	    if (line[0] == '#' || line[0] == '
  1198	' || line[0] == '\r') continue;
  1199	    int v = 0;
  1200	    if      (sscanf(line, "flash=%d",      &v) == 1) { ledFlashEnabled    = (bool)v; }
  1201	    else if (sscanf(line, "neoflash=%d",   &v) == 1) { neoFlashEnabled    = (bool)v; }
  1202	    else if (sscanf(line, "exp_preset=%d", &v) == 1) { expPreset          = (uint8_t)constrain(v,0,5); }
  1203	    else if (sscanf(line, "exp_val=%d",    &v) == 1) { expManualVal       = constrain(v,0,1200); }
  1204	    else if (sscanf(line, "exp_gain=%d",   &v) == 1) { expManualGain      = constrain(v,0,30); }
  1205	    else if (sscanf(line, "gc2145_fmt=%d", &v) == 1) { gc2145CaptureFormat= (v==1)?GFMT_JPG:GFMT_BMP; }
  1206	    else if (sscanf(line, "eis=%d", &v) == 1) { eisEnabled = (bool)v; }
  1207	    else if (sscanf(line, "hdr=%d", &v) == 1) { hdrEnabled = (bool)v; }
  1208	    else if (sscanf(line, "autorotate=%d", &v) == 1) { autoRotateEnabled = (bool)v; }
  1209	    else if (sscanf(line, "mpulog=%d", &v) == 1) { mpuLogEnabled = (bool)v; }
  1210	    else if (sscanf(line, "hud=%d", &v) == 1) { hudEnabled = (bool)v; }
  1211	    else if (sscanf(line, "hd_capture=%d", &v) == 1) { hdCaptureEnabled = (bool)v; }
  1212	    else if (sscanf(line, "hd_quality=%d", &v) == 1) { hdCaptureQuality = (uint8_t)constrain(v, 1, 10); }
  1213	    else if (sscanf(line, "dlpf=%d", &v) == 1) { mpuDlpfIndex = (uint8_t)constrain(v, 0, 6); }
  1214	    float fv = 0;
  1215	    if      (sscanf(line, "kalman_r=%f", &fv) == 1) { mpuKalmanRmeas = constrain(fv, 0.01f, 1.0f); }
  1216	    else if (sscanf(line, "tilt_dz=%f", &fv) == 1) { mpuTiltDeadzone = constrain(fv, 0.0f, 10.0f); }
  1217	  }
  1218	  fclose(f);
  1219	  if (g_mpuOk) applyDLPF();
  1220	}

  1222	void loadMPUCalibration() {
  1223	  FILE* f = fopen("/sdcard/mpu_cal.ini", "r");
  1224	  if (!f) {
  1225	    g_mpuCalLoaded = false;
  1226	    return;
  1227	  }
  1228	  char line[64];
  1229	  while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), f)) {
  1230	    if (line[0] == '#' || line[0] == '
  1231	' || line[0] == '\r') continue;
  1232	    float v = 0;
  1233	    if (sscanf(line, "calX=%f", &v) == 1)      { g_gyroCalX = v; }
  1234	    else if (sscanf(line, "calY=%f", &v) == 1) { g_gyroCalY = v; }
  1235	    else if (sscanf(line, "calZ=%f", &v) == 1) { g_gyroCalZ = v; }
  1236	  }
  1237	  fclose(f);
  1238	  g_mpuCalLoaded = true;
  1239	  Serial.printf("[MPU] Cal loaded: X=%.5f Y=%.5f Z=%.5f
  1240	", g_gyroCalX, g_gyroCalY, g_gyroCalZ);
  1241	}
  1242
  1243	void runMPUCalibration() {
  1244	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  1245	  neoRainbow();
  1246	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextSize(1); lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_7);
  1247	  const char* msg = "letakkan kamera datar & diam";
  1248	  lcd.drawString(msg, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(msg)) / 2, 80);
  1249
  1250	  for (int i = 5; i >= 1; i--) {
  1251	    lcd.fillRect(0, 110, DISP_W, 40, COL_BLACK);
  1252	    lcd.setFont(&fonts::FreeSansBold9pt7b); lcd.setTextSize(2); lcd.setTextColor(COL_WHITE);
  1253	    char buf[8]; snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%d", i);
  1254	    lcd.drawString(buf, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(buf)) / 2, 110);
  1255	    for (int j = 0; j < 10; j++) { delay(100); esp_task_wdt_reset(); }
  1256	  }
  1257
  1258	  lcd.fillRect(0, 80, DISP_W, 80, COL_BLACK);
  1259	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextSize(1); lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  1260	  const char* calMsg = "mengkalibrasi...";
  1261	  lcd.drawString(calMsg, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(calMsg)) / 2, 110);
  1262
  1263	  float sx = 0, sy = 0, sz = 0;
  1264	  for (int i = 0; i < 500; i++) {
  1265	    sensors_event_t a, g, t; mpu.getEvent(&a, &g, &t);
  1266	    sx += g.gyro.x; sy += g.gyro.y; sz += g.gyro.z;
  1267	    if (i % 50 == 0) esp_task_wdt_reset();
  1268	    delay(5);
  1269	  }
  1270	  g_gyroCalX = sx / 500.0f; g_gyroCalY = sy / 500.0f; g_gyroCalZ = sz / 500.0f;
  1271
  1272	  lcd.fillRect(0, 110, DISP_W, 40, COL_BLACK);
  1273	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_C);
  1274	  char res[64]; snprintf(res, sizeof(res), "X:%.5f Y:%.5f Z:%.5f", g_gyroCalX, g_gyroCalY, g_gyroCalZ);
  1275	  lcd.drawString(res, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(res)) / 2, 110);
  1276	  for (int j = 0; j < 20; j++) { delay(100); esp_task_wdt_reset(); }
  1277
  1278	  if (sdReady) {
  1279	    FILE* f = fopen("/sdcard/mpu_cal.ini", "w");
  1280	    if (f) {
  1281	      fprintf(f, "# MPU6050 Calibration - Sanzxcam
  1282	");
  1283	      fprintf(f, "calX=%.5f
  1284	calY=%.5f
  1285	calZ=%.5f
  1286	", g_gyroCalX, g_gyroCalY, g_gyroCalZ);
  1287	      fclose(f);
  1288	      g_mpuCalLoaded = true;
  1289	      islandPush(NOTIF_OK, "Kalibrasi tersimpan");
  1290	    }
  1291	  } else {
  1292	    islandPush(NOTIF_WARN, "Tersimpan di RAM saja");
  1293	  }
  1294	}

  1299	static void createFileTemplate(const char* path, const char* content) {
  1300	  FILE* f = fopen(path, "w");
  1301	  if (!f) return;
  1302	  fputs(content, f);
  1303	  fclose(f);
  1304	  Serial.printf("[CFG] template dibuat: %s
  1305	", path);
  1306	}

  1308	bool loadWifiConfig() {
  1309	  FILE* f = fopen(WIFI_INI_PATH, "r");
  1310	  if (!f) {
  1311	    createFileTemplate(WIFI_INI_PATH,
  1312	      "# Konfigurasi WiFi Sanzxcam
  1313	"
  1314	      "# Isi ssid dan pass lalu restart kamera
  1315	"
  1316	      "ssid=NamaWiFiKamu
  1317	"
  1318	      "pass=PasswordWiFiKamu
  1319	");
  1320	    return false;
  1321	  }
  1322	  char line[128]; bool gotSSID=false, gotPass=false;
  1323	  while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), f)) {
  1324	    if (line[0]=='#'||line[0]=='
  1325	'||line[0]=='\r') continue;
  1326	    char val[64]="";
  1327	    if      (sscanf(line,"ssid=%63[^\r
  1328	]",val)==1) { strncpy(wifiSSID,val,63); gotSSID=true; }
  1329	    else if (sscanf(line,"pass=%63[^\r
  1330	]",val)==1) { strncpy(wifiPass,val,63); gotPass=true; }
  1331	  }
  1332	  fclose(f);
  1333	  if (strcmp(wifiSSID,"NamaWiFiKamu")==0) return false;
  1334	  Serial.printf("[WIFI] ssid=%s
  1335	", wifiSSID);
  1336	  return gotSSID && gotPass;
  1337	}

  1339	bool loadGeminiConfig() {
  1340	  FILE* f = fopen(GEMINI_INI_PATH, "r");
  1341	  if (!f) {
  1342	    createFileTemplate(GEMINI_INI_PATH,
  1343	      "# Gemini API Keys untuk Sanzxcam AI
  1344	"
  1345	      "# Bisa isi hingga 5 key, auto-fallback jika 403/429
  1346	"
  1347	      "# Dapatkan key GRATIS di: aistudio.google.com
  1348	"
  1349	      "key1=AIzaXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXXX
  1350	"
  1351	      "key2=
  1352	key3=
  1353	key4=
  1354	key5=
  1355	");
  1356	    return false;
  1357	  }
  1358	  geminiKeyCount  = 0;
  1359	  geminiKeyActive = 0;
  1360	  memset(geminiApiKeys, 0, sizeof(geminiApiKeys));
  1361	  char line[128];
  1362	  while (fgets(line, sizeof(line), f)) {
  1363	    if (line[0]=='#'||line[0]=='
  1364	'||line[0]=='\r') continue;
  1365	    if (geminiKeyCount >= GEMINI_KEY_MAX) break;
  1366	    char val[80] = "";
  1367	    for (int ki = 1; ki <= GEMINI_KEY_MAX; ki++) {
  1368	      char pattern[16];
  1369	      snprintf(pattern, sizeof(pattern), "key%d=%%79[^\r
  1370	]", ki);
  1371	      if (sscanf(line, pattern, val) == 1) {
  1372	        int vlen = strlen(val);
  1373	        while (vlen > 0 && (val[vlen-1]==' '||val[vlen-1]=='\t')) { val[--vlen]='\0'; }
  1374	        if (vlen >= 20 && strncmp(val,"AIzaXXXX",8)!=0) {
  1375	          strncpy(geminiApiKeys[geminiKeyCount], val, 79);
  1376	          geminiApiKeys[geminiKeyCount][79] = '\0';
  1377	          geminiKeyCount++;
  1378	          Serial.printf("[GEMINI] key%d loaded
  1379	", geminiKeyCount);
  1380	        }
  1381	        break;
  1382	      }
  1383	    }
  1384	  }
  1385	  fclose(f);
  1386	  return (geminiKeyCount > 0);
  1387	}
  1388
  1389	bool saveGeminiConfig() {
  1390	  if (!sdReady) return false;
  1391	  FILE* f = fopen(GEMINI_INI_PATH, "w");
  1392	  if (!f) return false;
  1393	  fprintf(f, "# Gemini API Keys untuk Sanzxcam AI
  1394	");
  1395	  fprintf(f, "# Bisa isi hingga 5 key, auto-fallback jika 403/429
  1396	");
  1397	  fprintf(f, "# Dapatkan key GRATIS di: aistudio.google.com
  1398	");
  1399	  for (int i = 0; i < GEMINI_KEY_MAX; i++) {
  1400	    if (i < geminiKeyCount && strlen(geminiApiKeys[i]) > 0)
  1401	      fprintf(f, "key%d=%s
  1402	", i+1, geminiApiKeys[i]);
  1403	    else
  1404	      fprintf(f, "key%d=
  1405	", i+1);
  1406	  }
  1407	  fclose(f);
  1408	  return true;
  1409	}
  1410
  1411	static void maskApiKey(const char* key, char* out, int outLen) {
  1412	  int len = strlen(key);
  1413	  if (len == 0) { strncpy(out, "(kosong)", outLen-1); return; }
  1414	  if (len <= 12) { snprintf(out, outLen, "%.8s...", key); return; }
  1415	  snprintf(out, outLen, "%.8s...%s", key, key + len - 4);
  1416	}

  1456
  1457	// [PORTED v6.1] Features Menu
  1458	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1459	//  BLUETOOTH MP3 LOGIC
  1460	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1461
  1462	  if (!evt.valid) return;
  1463	  bool* const feats[5] = {&eisEnabled, &hdrEnabled, &autoRotateEnabled, &mpuLogEnabled, &hudEnabled};
  1464	  static const char* const labs[5] = {"EIS", "HDR", "AUTO-ROTATE", "MPU LOG", "HUD"};
  1465
  1466	  if (evt.pin == BTN_B && evt.isShort) {
  1467	    saveSettings();
  1468	    appMode = MODE_VIEWFINDER;
  1469	    islandNoClear = true;
  1470	    lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  1471	    resetAllButtons();
  1472	    return;
  1473	  }
  1474
  1475	  if (evt.pin == BTN_D && evt.isShort) { menuFeatSel = (menuFeatSel + 1) % 13; drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel); }
  1476	  else if (evt.pin == BTN_C && evt.isShort) { menuFeatSel = (menuFeatSel + 12) % 13; drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel); }
  1477	  else if (evt.pin == BTN_BOOT && evt.isShort) {
  1478	    if (menuFeatSel < 5) {
  1479	      *feats[menuFeatSel] = !(*feats[menuFeatSel]);
  1480	      char buf[32]; snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%s %s", labs[menuFeatSel], *feats[menuFeatSel] ? "ON" : "OFF");
  1481	      if (menuFeatSel == 4) { // HUD toggle
  1482	        saveSettings();
  1483	        islandPush(NOTIF_INFO, hudEnabled ? "HUD ON" : "HUD OFF");
  1484	      } else {
  1485	        islandPush(NOTIF_INFO, buf);
  1486	      }
  1487	      drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel);
  1488	    } else if (menuFeatSel == 5) {
  1489	      runMPUCalibration(); drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel);
  1490	    } else if (menuFeatSel == 6) {
  1491	      hdCaptureEnabled = !hdCaptureEnabled;
  1492	      islandPush(NOTIF_INFO, hdCaptureEnabled ? "HD CAPTURE ON" : "HD CAPTURE OFF"); drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel);
  1493	    } else if (menuFeatSel == 7) {
  1494	      hdCaptureQuality = (hdCaptureQuality == 4) ? 6 : 4;
  1495	      char qBuf[32]; snprintf(qBuf, sizeof(qBuf), "HD QUALITY: %d", hdCaptureQuality);
  1496	      islandPush(NOTIF_INFO, qBuf); drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel);
  1497	    } else if (menuFeatSel == 8) {
  1498	      if      (mpuKalmanRmeas < 0.04f) mpuKalmanRmeas = 0.05f;
  1499	      else if (mpuKalmanRmeas < 0.06f) mpuKalmanRmeas = 0.10f;
  1500	      else if (mpuKalmanRmeas < 0.15f) mpuKalmanRmeas = 0.20f;
  1501	      else if (mpuKalmanRmeas < 0.40f) mpuKalmanRmeas = 0.50f;
  1502	      else                             mpuKalmanRmeas = 0.03f;
  1503	      char buf[32]; snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "KALMAN-R: %.2f", mpuKalmanRmeas);
  1504	      islandPush(NOTIF_INFO, buf); drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel);
  1505	    } else if (menuFeatSel == 9) {
  1506	      if      (mpuTiltDeadzone < 0.5f) mpuTiltDeadzone = 1.0f;
  1507	      else if (mpuTiltDeadzone < 1.5f) mpuTiltDeadzone = 2.0f;
  1508	      else if (mpuTiltDeadzone < 2.5f) mpuTiltDeadzone = 3.0f;
  1509	      else if (mpuTiltDeadzone < 4.5f) mpuTiltDeadzone = 5.0f;
  1510	      else                             mpuTiltDeadzone = 0.0f;
  1511	      char buf[32]; snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "TILT-DZ: %.1f deg", mpuTiltDeadzone);
  1512	      islandPush(NOTIF_INFO, buf); drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel);
  1513	    } else if (menuFeatSel == 10) {
  1514	      mpuDlpfIndex = (mpuDlpfIndex + 1) % 7;
  1515	      applyDLPF();
  1516	      char buf[32]; snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "DLPF: %s", DLPF_LABELS[mpuDlpfIndex]);
  1517	      islandPush(NOTIF_INFO, buf); drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel);
  1518	    } else if (menuFeatSel == 11) {
  1519	      DIR* d = opendir("/sdcard/.cache");
  1520	      if (d) {
  1521	        struct dirent* e; int count = 0;
  1522	        while ((e = readdir(d)) != nullptr) {
  1523	          String n = e->d_name;
  1524	          if (n.endsWith(".bin")) {
  1525	            char dp[64]; snprintf(dp, sizeof(dp), "/sdcard/.cache/%s", e->d_name);
  1526	            remove(dp); count++; esp_task_wdt_reset();
  1527	          }
  1528	        }
  1529	        closedir(d);
  1530	        char msg[32]; snprintf(msg, sizeof(msg), "Cache: %d file dihapus", count);
  1531	        islandPush(NOTIF_OK, msg);
  1532	      } else {
  1533	        islandPush(NOTIF_WARN, "Cache dir tidak ada");
  1534	      }
  1535	      drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel);
  1536	    }
  1537	    else if (menuFeatSel == 12) {
  1538	      btStartScan();
  1539	    }
  1540	  }
  1541	}
  1542	// UI UPDATE v6.0
  1543	void drawAIFeatureMenu(int sel, bool fromViewfinder) {
  1544	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  1545	  lcd.fillRect(0, 0, DISP_W, 20, COL_GRAY_D);
  1546	  lcd.drawFastHLine(0, 20, DISP_W, COL_GRAY_3);
  1547	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextSize(1);
  1548	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_ACCENT);
  1549	  const char* title = "AI FEATURE";
  1550	  lcd.drawString(title, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(title)) / 2, 6);
  1551	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  1552	  if (fromViewfinder) {
  1553	    lcd.drawString("VIEWFINDER", 4, 6);
  1554	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_WARN);
  1555	    lcd.drawString("capture!", DISP_W - 46, 6);
  1556	  } else {
  1557	    lcd.drawString("GALLERY", 4, 6);
  1558	  }
  1559	  const int itemH = 28;
  1560	  const int startY = 22;
  1561	  for (int i = 0; i < AI_FEAT_COUNT; i++) {
  1562	    const AIFeatureDef& feat = AI_FEATURES[i];
  1563	    bool isSelected = (i == sel);
  1564	    int iy = startY + i * itemH;
  1565
  1566	    uint16_t rowBg = isSelected ? (((feat.iconColor >> 3) & 0x18C3) | 0x1082) : (i % 2 == 0 ? COL_GRAY_D : COL_BLACK);
  1567	    lcd.fillRect(0, iy, DISP_W, itemH - 1, rowBg);
  1568	    lcd.drawFastHLine(0, iy + itemH - 1, DISP_W, COL_GRAY_2);
  1569	    char numBuf[4]; snprintf(numBuf, sizeof(numBuf), "%d", i + 1);
  1570	    lcd.setTextColor(isSelected ? COL_WHITE : COL_GRAY_5);
  1571	    lcd.drawString(numBuf, 5, iy + 10);
  1572	    int iconX = 18, iconY = iy + 6;
  1573
  1574	    uint16_t icBg = isSelected ? feat.iconColor : ((feat.iconColor >> 2) & 0x39E7);
  1575	    lcd.fillRect(iconX, iconY, 14, 14, icBg);
  1576
  1577	    lcd.setTextColor(isSelected ? COL_BLACK : COL_GRAY_7);
  1578	    int iconTw = lcd.textWidth(feat.icon);
  1579	    lcd.drawString(feat.icon, iconX + (14 - iconTw) / 2, iconY + 3);
  1580
  1581	    lcd.setTextColor(isSelected ? COL_WHITE : COL_GRAY_C);
  1582	    lcd.drawString(feat.label, 38, iy + 10);
  1583
  1584	    if (isSelected) {
  1585	      lcd.setTextColor(feat.iconColor);
  1586	      lcd.drawString(">", DISP_W - 12, iy + 10);
  1587	    }
  1588	  }
  1589	  int footerY = startY + AI_FEAT_COUNT * itemH;
  1590	  if (footerY + 14 < DISP_H - 22) {
  1591	    lcd.drawFastHLine(0, footerY, DISP_W, COL_GRAY_2);
  1592	    lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_3);
  1593	    char preview[50]; strncpy(preview, AI_FEATURES[sel].prompt, 49); preview[49] = '\0';
  1594	    int maxW = DISP_W - 8;
  1595	    while (strlen(preview) > 6 && lcd.textWidth(preview) > maxW) {
  1596	      int l = strlen(preview); preview[l-1] = '\0';
  1597	      if (strlen(preview) > 3) {
  1598	        preview[strlen(preview)-1] = '.'; preview[strlen(preview)-2] = '.'; preview[strlen(preview)-3] = '.';
  1599	      }
  1600	    }
  1601	    lcd.drawString(preview, 4, footerY + 4);
  1602	  }
  1603	  lcd.fillRect(0, DISP_H - 18, DISP_W, 18, COL_GRAY_D);
  1604	  lcd.drawFastHLine(0, DISP_H - 18, DISP_W, COL_GRAY_3);
  1605	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextSize(1);
  1606	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  1607	  lcd.drawString("C=naik", 4, DISP_H - 14);
  1608	  lcd.drawString("D=turun", 60, DISP_H - 14);
  1609	  lcd.drawString("BOOT=pilih", 130, DISP_H - 14);
  1610	  lcd.drawString("B=batal", 240, DISP_H - 14);
  1611	}

  1616	void openAIFeatureMenu(bool fromViewfinder) {
  1617	  aiFromViewfinder = fromViewfinder;
  1618	  aiSelectedFeature = AI_FEAT_DESCRIBE;
  1619	  drawAIFeatureMenu((int)aiSelectedFeature, fromViewfinder);
  1620	  resetAllButtons();

  1622	  neoBurst(0, 200, 200, 2);

  1624
  1625	bool captureForAI(char* outPath, int outPathLen) {
  1626	  snprintf(outPath, outPathLen, "/sdcard/ai_temp.jpg");
  1627	  if (ledFlashEnabled) {
  1628	    digitalWrite(LED_FLASH, HIGH); if (neoFlashEnabled) neoWhiteFlash(); delay(150);
  1629	    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
  1630	      camera_fb_t *tfb = esp_camera_fb_get();
  1631	      if (tfb) esp_camera_fb_return(tfb);
  1632	      esp_task_wdt_reset();
  1633	    }
  1634	  }
  1635	  camera_fb_t *fb = esp_camera_fb_get();
  1636	  if (ledFlashEnabled) digitalWrite(LED_FLASH, LOW);
  1637	  if (!fb) return false;
  1638	  neoBurst(0, 80, 255, 1);
  1639	  uint8_t *jpg_buf = nullptr; size_t jpg_len = 0; bool ok = false;
  1640	  if (fb->format == PIXFORMAT_RGB565) {
  1641	    if (eisEnabled) {
  1642	      uint16_t* src = (uint16_t*)fb->buf;
  1643	      uint16_t* tmp = (uint16_t*)ps_malloc(DISP_W * DISP_H * 2);
  1644	      if (!tmp) tmp = (uint16_t*)malloc(DISP_W * DISP_H * 2);
  1645	      if (tmp) {
  1646	        int sx = constrain((int)g_eisOffX, 0, DISP_W - EIS_VP_W); int sy = constrain((int)g_eisOffY, 0, DISP_H - EIS_VP_H);
  1647	        for (int y = 0; y < DISP_H; y++) {
  1648	          int srY = sy + (y * EIS_VP_H / DISP_H);
  1649	          for (int x = 0; x < DISP_W; x++) {
  1650	            int srX = sx + (x * EIS_VP_W / DISP_W);
  1651	            tmp[y * DISP_W + x] = src[srY * DISP_W + srX];
  1652	          }
  1653	        }
  1654	        camera_fb_t fk = *fb; fk.buf = (uint8_t*)tmp; fk.width = DISP_W; fk.height = DISP_H;
  1655	        int captureQ = hdCaptureEnabled ? map(hdCaptureQuality, 1, 10, 95, 50) : 85;
  1656	        ok = frame2jpg(&fk, captureQ, &jpg_buf, &jpg_len); free(tmp);
  1657	      }
  1658	    } else { int captureQ = hdCaptureEnabled ? map(hdCaptureQuality, 1, 10, 95, 50) : 85;
  1659	    ok = frame2jpg(fb, captureQ, &jpg_buf, &jpg_len); }
  1660	  } else if (fb->format == PIXFORMAT_JPEG) {
  1661	    jpg_buf = fb->buf; jpg_len = fb->len; ok = true;
  1662	  }
  1663	  bool saved = false;
  1664	  if (ok && jpg_buf && jpg_len > 0) {
  1665	    FILE* f = fopen(outPath, "wb");
  1666	    if (f) { size_t w = fwrite(jpg_buf, 1, jpg_len, f); fclose(f); saved = (w == jpg_len); }
  1667	    if (fb->format != PIXFORMAT_JPEG) free(jpg_buf);
  1668	  }
  1669	  esp_camera_fb_return(fb); esp_task_wdt_reset();
  1670	  return saved;
  1671	}
  1672
  1673	void handleModeAIFeatureMenu(ButtonEvent evtBoot, ButtonEvent evtB,
  1674	                              ButtonEvent evtC, ButtonEvent evtD) {
  1675	  bool redraw = false;
  1676	  int sel = (int)aiSelectedFeature;
  1677
  1678	  if (evtC.valid && evtC.isShort) {
  1679	    sel = (sel - 1 + AI_FEAT_COUNT) % AI_FEAT_COUNT;
  1680	    aiSelectedFeature = (AIFeature)sel;
  1681	    redraw = true;
  1682	  }
  1683	  if (evtD.valid && evtD.isShort) {
  1684	    sel = (sel + 1) % AI_FEAT_COUNT;
  1685	    aiSelectedFeature = (AIFeature)sel;
  1686	    redraw = true;
  1687	  }
  1688
  1689	  if (redraw) {
  1690	    drawAIFeatureMenu(sel, aiFromViewfinder);
  1691	    return;
  1692	  }
  1693
  1694	  if (evtB.valid && evtB.isShort) {
  1695	    resetAllButtons();
  1696	    if (aiFromViewfinder) {
  1697	      appMode = MODE_VIEWFINDER;
  1698	      islandNoClear = true;
  1699	      lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  1700	    } else {
  1701	      appMode = MODE_PHOTO_VIEW;
  1702	      photoViewRender();
  1703	      photoViewDrawCaption(photoViewIndex);
  1704	      photoViewCaptionUntilMs = millis() + 2000;
  1705	      photoViewCaptionVisible = true;
  1706	    }
  1707	    return;
  1708	  }
  1709
  1710	  if (evtBoot.valid && evtBoot.isShort) {
  1711	    if (aiFromViewfinder) {
  1712	      if (!sdReady) {
  1713	        islandPush(NOTIF_WARN, "SD tidak tersedia");
  1714	        resetAllButtons();
  1715	        appMode = MODE_VIEWFINDER;
  1716	        islandNoClear = true;
  1717	        lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  1718	        return;
  1719	      }
  1720
  1721	      lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  1722	      lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  1723	      const char* capMsg = "capturing...";
  1724	      lcd.drawString(capMsg, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(capMsg)) / 2, DISP_H / 2 - 4);
  1725
  1726	      char tempPath[48];
  1727	      bool capOK = captureForAI(tempPath, sizeof(tempPath));
  1728	      if (!capOK) {
  1729	        islandPush(NOTIF_WARN, "Gagal capture");
  1730	        resetAllButtons();
  1731	        appMode = MODE_VIEWFINDER;
  1732	        islandNoClear = true;
  1733	        lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  1734	        return;
  1735	      }
  1736
  1737	      scanGalleryFiles();
  1738	      scanPhotoCount();
  1739
  1740	      int tempIdx = -1;
  1741	      for (int i = 0; i < galleryCount; i++) {
  1742	        if (strcmp(galleryFiles[i], "ai_temp.jpg") == 0) {
  1743	          tempIdx = i; break;
  1744	        }
  1745	      }
  1746	      if (tempIdx < 0) {
  1747	        islandPush(NOTIF_WARN, "File temp tidak ditemukan");
  1748	        resetAllButtons();
  1749	        appMode = MODE_VIEWFINDER;
  1750	        islandNoClear = true;
  1751	        lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  1752	        return;
  1753	      }
  1754
  1755	      photoViewIndex = tempIdx;
  1756	      openAIDescribeWithFeature(tempIdx, aiSelectedFeature);
  1757
  1758	    } else {
  1759	      openAIDescribeWithFeature(photoViewIndex, aiSelectedFeature);
  1760	    }
  1761	    return;
  1762	  }
  1763	}

  1768	void drawKeyManagerScreen() {
  1769	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  1770	  lcd.fillRect(0,0,DISP_W,20,COL_GRAY_D);
  1771	  lcd.drawFastHLine(0,20,DISP_W,COL_GRAY_3);
  1772	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextSize(1);
  1773	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_ACCENT);
  1774	  const char* title = "GEMINI KEY MANAGER";
  1775	  lcd.drawString(title,(DISP_W-lcd.textWidth(title))/2,6);
  1776	  if (kmDirty) {
  1777	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_WARN);
  1778	    lcd.drawString("*UNSAVED*", DISP_W-58, 6);
  1779	  }
  1780	  int y = 24;
  1781	  if (geminiKeyCount == 0) {
  1782	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  1783	    lcd.drawString("Tidak ada key.", 8, y+10); y+=24;
  1784	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_3);
  1785	    lcd.drawString("Edit gemini.ini di PC", 8, y); y+=12;
  1786	    lcd.drawString("lalu restart kamera.", 8, y);
  1787	  } else {
  1788	    for (int i = 0; i < geminiKeyCount; i++) {
  1789	      bool isSelected = (i == kmSelIdx);
  1790	      bool isActive   = (i == geminiKeyActive);
  1791	      uint16_t rowBg = isSelected ? COL_GRAY_5 : (i%2==0 ? COL_GRAY_D : COL_BLACK);
  1792	      lcd.fillRect(0, y, DISP_W, 22, rowBg);
  1793	      char numBuf[4]; snprintf(numBuf, sizeof(numBuf), "k%d", i+1);
  1794	      lcd.setTextColor(isSelected ? COL_WHITE : COL_GRAY_5);
  1795	      lcd.drawString(numBuf, 4, y+7);
  1796	      char masked[32];
  1797	      maskApiKey(geminiApiKeys[i], masked, sizeof(masked));
  1798	      lcd.setTextColor(isSelected ? COL_WHITE : COL_GRAY_C);
  1799	      lcd.drawString(masked, 28, y+7);
  1800	      if (isActive) { lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_ACCENT); lcd.drawString("*", DISP_W-16, y+7); }
  1801	      lcd.drawFastHLine(0, y+22, DISP_W, COL_GRAY_2);
  1802	      y += 23;
  1803	    }
  1804	  }
  1805	  y = 24 + geminiKeyCount * 23;
  1806	  if (geminiKeyCount < GEMINI_KEY_MAX) {
  1807	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_3);
  1808	    char slotBuf[32];
  1809	    snprintf(slotBuf, sizeof(slotBuf), "+ %d slot kosong (edit via PC)", GEMINI_KEY_MAX - geminiKeyCount);
  1810	    lcd.drawString(slotBuf, 4, y+4);
  1811	  }
  1812	  lcd.drawFastHLine(0, DISP_H-40, DISP_W, COL_GRAY_2);
  1813	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  1814	  lcd.drawString("C/D=pilih", 4, DISP_H-35);
  1815	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_3);
  1816	  lcd.drawString("B=set aktif", 80, DISP_H-35);
  1817	  lcd.setTextColor(0xF800);
  1818	  lcd.drawString("BOOT-long=hapus", 180, DISP_H-35);
  1819	  lcd.drawFastHLine(0, DISP_H-22, DISP_W, COL_GRAY_2);
  1820	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  1821	  lcd.drawString("BOOT=simpan&kembali", 4, DISP_H-18);
  1822	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_3);
  1823	  lcd.drawString("D-long=batal", 190, DISP_H-18);
  1824	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_2);
  1825	  lcd.drawString("* = key aktif terakhir", 4, DISP_H-8);
  1826	}
  1827
  1828	void kmDeleteKey(int idx) {
  1829	  if (idx < 0 || idx >= geminiKeyCount) return;
  1830	  for (int i = idx; i < geminiKeyCount-1; i++)
  1831	    strncpy(geminiApiKeys[i], geminiApiKeys[i+1], 79);
  1832	  memset(geminiApiKeys[geminiKeyCount-1], 0, 80);
  1833	  geminiKeyCount--;
  1834	  if (geminiKeyActive >= geminiKeyCount) geminiKeyActive = max(0, geminiKeyCount-1);
  1835	  if (kmSelIdx >= geminiKeyCount) kmSelIdx = max(0, geminiKeyCount-1);
  1836	  kmDirty = true;
  1837	}
  1838
  1839	void openKeyManager() {
  1840	  kmSelIdx = 0; kmDirty = false;
  1841	  drawKeyManagerScreen();
  1842	  resetAllButtons();
  1843	  appMode = MODE_KEY_MANAGER;
  1844	}
  1845
  1846	void handleModeKeyManager(ButtonEvent evtBoot, ButtonEvent evtB,
  1847	                           ButtonEvent evtC, ButtonEvent evtD) {
  1848	  bool redraw = false;
  1849	  if (evtC.valid && evtC.isShort) {
  1850	    if (geminiKeyCount > 0) { kmSelIdx = (kmSelIdx - 1 + geminiKeyCount) % geminiKeyCount; redraw = true; }
  1851	  }
  1852	  if (evtD.valid && evtD.isShort) {
  1853	    if (geminiKeyCount > 0) { kmSelIdx = (kmSelIdx + 1) % geminiKeyCount; redraw = true; }
  1854	  }
  1855	  if (evtB.valid && evtB.isShort) {
  1856	    if (geminiKeyCount > 0) {
  1857	      geminiKeyActive = kmSelIdx;
  1858	      char buf[28]; snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "Key %d diset aktif", kmSelIdx+1);
  1859	      islandPush(NOTIF_INFO, buf); redraw = true;
  1860	    }
  1861	  }
  1862	  if (evtBoot.valid && evtBoot.isLong) {
  1863	    if (geminiKeyCount > 0) {
  1864	      char delBuf[28]; snprintf(delBuf, sizeof(delBuf), "Key %d dihapus", kmSelIdx+1);
  1865	      kmDeleteKey(kmSelIdx);
  1866	      islandPush(NOTIF_WARN, delBuf); redraw = true;
  1867	    }
  1868	  }
  1869	  if (evtBoot.valid && evtBoot.isShort) {
  1870	    if (kmDirty) {
  1871	      bool ok = saveGeminiConfig();
  1872	      islandPush(ok ? NOTIF_OK : NOTIF_WARN, ok ? "Key disimpan ke SD" : "Gagal simpan key");
  1873	    }
  1874	    kmDirty = false; resetAllButtons();
  1875	    appMode = MODE_PHOTO_VIEW;
  1876	    photoViewRender();
  1877	    photoViewDrawCaption(photoViewIndex);
  1878	    photoViewCaptionUntilMs = millis() + 2000;
  1879	    photoViewCaptionVisible = true;
  1880	    return;
  1881	  }
  1882	  if (evtD.valid && evtD.isLong) {
  1883	    if (kmDirty) { loadGeminiConfig(); islandPush(NOTIF_INFO, "Perubahan dibatalkan"); }
  1884	    kmDirty = false; resetAllButtons();
  1885	    appMode = MODE_PHOTO_VIEW;
  1886	    photoViewRender();
  1887	    photoViewDrawCaption(photoViewIndex);
  1888	    photoViewCaptionUntilMs = millis() + 2000;
  1889	    photoViewCaptionVisible = true;
  1890	    return;
  1891	  }
  1892	  if (redraw) drawKeyManagerScreen();
  1893	}

  1895	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1896	//  STEGANOGRAFI JPEG
  1897	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1898	#define STEGO_PAYLOAD_LEN  32
  1899	#define STEGO_MAGIC        "SANZXCAM"
  1900	#define STEGO_VERSION      "v6.0"
  1901
  1902	void stegoMakePayload(char* out, int maxLen, int photoNum) {
  1903	  snprintf(out, maxLen, "%s|%04d|%s", STEGO_MAGIC, photoNum, STEGO_VERSION);
  1904	}
  1905
  1906	uint8_t* stegoEmbedToJpeg(const uint8_t* jpgIn, size_t jpgLen,
  1907	                           const char* payload, int payLen, size_t* outLen) {
  1908	  if (!jpgIn || jpgLen < 4 || !outLen) return nullptr;
  1909	  if (jpgIn[0] != 0xFF || jpgIn[1] != 0xD8) return nullptr;
  1910	  if (!payload || payLen <= 0 || payLen > 500) return nullptr;
  1911	  int comTotal = 4 + payLen;
  1912	  size_t newLen = jpgLen + (size_t)comTotal;
  1913	  uint8_t* out = (uint8_t*)ps_malloc(newLen);
  1914	  if (!out) { out = (uint8_t*)malloc(newLen); if (!out) return nullptr; }
  1915	  out[0]=0xFF; out[1]=0xD8; out[2]=0xFF; out[3]=0xFE;
  1916	  uint16_t comLen = (uint16_t)(2 + payLen);
  1917	  out[4]=(uint8_t)((comLen>>8)&0xFF); out[5]=(uint8_t)(comLen&0xFF);
  1918	  memcpy(out+6, payload, (size_t)payLen);
  1919	  memcpy(out+6+payLen, jpgIn+2, jpgLen-2);
  1920	  *outLen = newLen;
  1921	  return out;
  1922	}
  1923
  1924	bool stegoExtractFromJpeg(const uint8_t* jpgBuf, size_t jpgLen,
  1925	                           char* outPayload, int maxLen) {
  1926	  if (!jpgBuf || jpgLen < 6 || !outPayload || maxLen < 2) return false;
  1927	  if (jpgBuf[0] != 0xFF || jpgBuf[1] != 0xD8) return false;
  1928	  size_t pos = 2;
  1929	  while (pos + 3 < jpgLen) {
  1930	    if (jpgBuf[pos] != 0xFF) break;
  1931	    uint8_t marker = jpgBuf[pos+1];
  1932	    if (marker == 0xFF) { pos++; continue; }
  1933	    if (marker == 0xD8) { pos += 2; continue; }
  1934	    if (marker == 0xD9) break;
  1935	    if (marker >= 0xD0 && marker <= 0xD7) { pos += 2; continue; }
  1936	    if (pos + 3 >= jpgLen) break;
  1937	    uint16_t segLen = ((uint16_t)jpgBuf[pos+2]<<8)|jpgBuf[pos+3];
  1938	    if (segLen < 2) break;
  1939	    if (marker == 0xFE) {
  1940	      int dataLen = (int)segLen - 2;
  1941	      if (dataLen <= 0) { pos += 2 + segLen; continue; }
  1942	      int readLen = (dataLen < maxLen-1) ? dataLen : maxLen-1;
  1943	      memcpy(outPayload, jpgBuf+pos+4, (size_t)readLen);
  1944	      outPayload[readLen] = '\0';
  1945	      return (strncmp(outPayload, STEGO_MAGIC, strlen(STEGO_MAGIC)) == 0);
  1946	    }
  1947	    size_t skip = 2 + (size_t)segLen;
  1948	    if (pos + skip > jpgLen) break;
  1949	    pos += skip;
  1950	  }
  1951	  return false;
  1952	}
  1953
  1954	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1955	//  STEGANOGRAFI BMP
  1956	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  1957	#define STEGO_BMP_MAX_PAYLOAD 64
  1958
  1959	bool stegoBmpExtract(const char* path, char* outPayload, int maxLen) {
  1960	  if (!path || !outPayload || maxLen < 2) return false;
  1961	  FILE* f = fopen(path, "rb"); if (!f) return false;
  1962	  uint8_t hdr[54];
  1963	  if (fread(hdr,1,54,f)!=54) { fclose(f); return false; }
  1964	  if (hdr[0]!='B'||hdr[1]!='M') { fclose(f); return false; }
  1965	  uint32_t dataOffset=(uint32_t)hdr[10]|((uint32_t)hdr[11]<<8)|((uint32_t)hdr[12]<<16)|((uint32_t)hdr[13]<<24);
  1966	  int32_t bmpW=(int32_t)((uint32_t)hdr[18]|((uint32_t)hdr[19]<<8)|((uint32_t)hdr[20]<<16)|((uint32_t)hdr[21]<<24));
  1967	  int32_t bmpH=(int32_t)((uint32_t)hdr[22]|((uint32_t)hdr[23]<<8)|((uint32_t)hdr[24]<<16)|((uint32_t)hdr[25]<<24));
  1968	  if (bmpH<0) bmpH=-bmpH;
  1969	  if (bmpW<=0||bmpH<=0) { fclose(f); return false; }
  1970	  uint16_t bpp=(uint16_t)hdr[28]|((uint16_t)hdr[29]<<8);
  1971	  if (bpp!=24) { fclose(f); return false; }
  1972	  fseek(f,(long)dataOffset,SEEK_SET);
  1973	  int rowSize=((bmpW*3+3)/4)*4;
  1974	  int totalPayloadBits=(maxLen-1)*8;
  1975	  memset(outPayload,0,maxLen);
  1976	  int bitIdx=0,charIdx=0; uint8_t curChar=0; bool found=false;
  1977	  uint8_t* rowBuf=(uint8_t*)malloc(rowSize);
  1978	  if (!rowBuf) { fclose(f); return false; }
  1979	  for (int row=bmpH-1;row>=0&&bitIdx<totalPayloadBits;row--) {
  1980	    fseek(f,(long)dataOffset+(long)row*rowSize,SEEK_SET);
  1981	    if (fread(rowBuf,1,rowSize,f)!=(size_t)rowSize) break;
  1982	    for (int x=0;x<bmpW&&bitIdx<totalPayloadBits;x++) {
  1983	      uint8_t bit=rowBuf[x*3+0]&0x01;
  1984	      int bitPos=7-(bitIdx%8);
  1985	      curChar|=(bit<<bitPos); bitIdx++;
  1986	      if (bitIdx%8==0) {
  1987	        outPayload[charIdx]=curChar;
  1988	        if (curChar=='\0') { found=true; break; }
  1989	        charIdx++; curChar=0;
  1990	        if (charIdx>=maxLen-1) { found=true; break; }
  1991	      }
  1992	    }
  1993	    if (found) break;
  1994	  }
  1995	  outPayload[maxLen-1]='\0';
  1996	  free(rowBuf); fclose(f);
  1997	  return (strncmp(outPayload,STEGO_MAGIC,strlen(STEGO_MAGIC))==0);
  1998	}
  1999
  2000	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2001	//  EXIF INJECT
  2002	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2003	static inline void exifL16(uint8_t* b,uint16_t v){b[0]=v&0xFF;b[1]=(v>>8)&0xFF;}
  2004	static inline void exifL32(uint8_t* b,uint32_t v){b[0]=v&0xFF;b[1]=(v>>8)&0xFF;b[2]=(v>>16)&0xFF;b[3]=(v>>24)&0xFF;}
  2005	static void exifEntry(uint8_t* b,uint16_t tag,uint16_t type,uint32_t count,uint32_t valOrOff){
  2006	  exifL16(b+0,tag);exifL16(b+2,type);exifL32(b+4,count);exifL32(b+8,valOrOff);}
  2007	static void exifMakeTimestamp(char* buf,size_t bufLen){
  2008	  unsigned long totalSec=millis()/1000;
  2009	  unsigned long sec=totalSec%60,min=(totalSec/60)%60,hour=(totalSec/3600)%24;
  2010	  snprintf(buf,bufLen,"2025:01:01 %02lu:%02lu:%02lu",hour,min,sec);}
  2011
  2012	uint8_t* exifInjectToJpeg(const uint8_t* jpgIn,size_t jpgLen,int photoNum,const char* sensorStr,size_t* outLen){
  2013	  if(!jpgIn||jpgLen<4||!outLen) return nullptr;
  2014	  if(jpgIn[0]!=0xFF||jpgIn[1]!=0xD8) return nullptr;
  2015	  char strDesc[16],strMake[12],strModel[12],strSoftware[12],strDt[24];
  2016	  snprintf(strDesc,sizeof(strDesc),"photo_%04d",photoNum);
  2017	  snprintf(strMake,sizeof(strMake),"SANZXCAM");
  2018	  snprintf(strModel,sizeof(strModel),"%s",sensorStr?sensorStr:"UNKNOWN");
  2019	  snprintf(strSoftware,sizeof(strSoftware),"v6.0");
  2020	  exifMakeTimestamp(strDt,sizeof(strDt));
  2021	  int lenDesc=strlen(strDesc)+1,lenMake=strlen(strMake)+1;
  2022	  int lenModel=strlen(strModel)+1,lenSoftware=strlen(strSoftware)+1,lenDt=20;
  2023	  const uint32_t offIFD0=8,nIFD0=5;
  2024	  const uint32_t offExifIFD=offIFD0+2+nIFD0*12+4,nExifIFD=2;
  2025	  const uint32_t offDataArea=offExifIFD+2+nExifIFD*12+4;
  2026	  uint32_t offDesc=offDataArea,offMake=offDesc+lenDesc;
  2027	  uint32_t offModel=offMake+lenMake,offSoftware=offModel+lenModel;
  2028	  uint32_t offDt=offSoftware+lenSoftware,tiffSize=offDt+lenDt;
  2029	  uint32_t app1DataSize=6+tiffSize;
  2030	  uint16_t app1Len=(uint16_t)(2+app1DataSize);
  2031	  size_t totalSize=2+2+2+app1DataSize+(jpgLen-2);
  2032	  uint8_t* out=(uint8_t*)ps_malloc(totalSize);
  2033	  if(!out){out=(uint8_t*)malloc(totalSize);if(!out)return nullptr;}
  2034	  size_t pos=0;
  2035	  out[pos++]=0xFF;out[pos++]=0xD8;out[pos++]=0xFF;out[pos++]=0xE1;
  2036	  out[pos++]=(app1Len>>8)&0xFF;out[pos++]=app1Len&0xFF;
  2037	  out[pos++]='E';out[pos++]='x';out[pos++]='i';out[pos++]='f';
  2038	  out[pos++]=0x00;out[pos++]=0x00;
  2039	  uint8_t* tiff=out+pos;size_t tp=0;
  2040	  tiff[tp++]='I';tiff[tp++]='I';tiff[tp++]=0x2A;tiff[tp++]=0x00;
  2041	  exifL32(tiff+tp,offIFD0);tp+=4;
  2042	  exifL16(tiff+tp,(uint16_t)nIFD0);tp+=2;
  2043	  exifEntry(tiff+tp,0x010E,2,(uint32_t)lenDesc,offDesc);tp+=12;
  2044	  exifEntry(tiff+tp,0x010F,2,(uint32_t)lenMake,offMake);tp+=12;
  2045	  exifEntry(tiff+tp,0x0110,2,(uint32_t)lenModel,offModel);tp+=12;
  2046	  exifEntry(tiff+tp,0x0131,2,(uint32_t)lenSoftware,offSoftware);tp+=12;
  2047	  exifEntry(tiff+tp,0x8769,4,1,offExifIFD);tp+=12;
  2048	  exifL32(tiff+tp,0);tp+=4;
  2049	  exifL16(tiff+tp,(uint16_t)nExifIFD);tp+=2;
  2050	  exifEntry(tiff+tp,0x9000,7,4,0x30323230);tp+=12;
  2051	  exifEntry(tiff+tp,0x9003,2,(uint32_t)lenDt,offDt);tp+=12;
  2052	  exifL32(tiff+tp,0);tp+=4;
  2053	  memcpy(tiff+tp,strDesc,(size_t)lenDesc);tp+=lenDesc;
  2054	  memcpy(tiff+tp,strMake,(size_t)lenMake);tp+=lenMake;
  2055	  memcpy(tiff+tp,strModel,(size_t)lenModel);tp+=lenModel;
  2056	  memcpy(tiff+tp,strSoftware,(size_t)lenSoftware);tp+=lenSoftware;
  2057	  memset(tiff+tp,0,(size_t)lenDt);
  2058	  memcpy(tiff+tp,strDt,min((int)strlen(strDt),lenDt-1));tp+=lenDt;
  2059	  pos+=tp;
  2060	  memcpy(out+pos,jpgIn+2,jpgLen-2);pos+=jpgLen-2;
  2061	  *outLen=pos;
  2062	  return out;
  2063	}
  2064
  2065	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2066	//  BMP SAVE
  2067	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2068	bool saveBMP(const uint8_t* rgb565Buf,int w,int h,const char* path,
  2069	             const char* stegoPayload=nullptr,int stegoPayLen=0){
  2070	  FILE* f=fopen(path,"wb"); if(!f) return false;
  2071	  int rowSize=((w*3+3)/4)*4,dataSize=rowSize*h,fileSize=54+dataSize;
  2072	  uint8_t hdr[54]; memset(hdr,0,sizeof(hdr));
  2073	  hdr[0]='B';hdr[1]='M';
  2074	  hdr[2]=fileSize&0xFF;hdr[3]=(fileSize>>8)&0xFF;hdr[4]=(fileSize>>16)&0xFF;hdr[5]=(fileSize>>24)&0xFF;
  2075	  hdr[10]=54;hdr[14]=40;
  2076	  hdr[18]=w&0xFF;hdr[19]=(w>>8)&0xFF;hdr[20]=(w>>16)&0xFF;hdr[21]=(w>>24)&0xFF;
  2077	  hdr[22]=h&0xFF;hdr[23]=(h>>8)&0xFF;hdr[24]=(h>>16)&0xFF;hdr[25]=(h>>24)&0xFF;
  2078	  hdr[26]=1;hdr[28]=24;
  2079	  hdr[34]=dataSize&0xFF;hdr[35]=(dataSize>>8)&0xFF;hdr[36]=(dataSize>>16)&0xFF;hdr[37]=(dataSize>>24)&0xFF;
  2080	  hdr[38]=0x13;hdr[39]=0x0B;hdr[42]=0x13;hdr[43]=0x0B;
  2081	  fwrite(hdr,1,54,f);
  2082	  int totalStegoBits=(stegoPayload&&stegoPayLen>0)?stegoPayLen*8:0,stegoPixelIdx=0;
  2083	  static uint8_t rowBuf[320*3+4];
  2084	  for(int y=h-1;y>=0;y--){
  2085	    memset(rowBuf,0,rowSize);
  2086	    const uint16_t* srcRow=(const uint16_t*)rgb565Buf+(size_t)y*w;
  2087	    for(int x=0;x<w;x++){
  2088	      uint16_t raw=srcRow[x],px=(raw<<8)|(raw>>8);
  2089	      uint8_t r=((px>>11)&0x1F)<<3,g=((px>>5)&0x3F)<<2,b=(px&0x1F)<<3;
  2090	      if(stegoPixelIdx<totalStegoBits){
  2091	        int charIdx=stegoPixelIdx/8,bitPos=7-(stegoPixelIdx%8);
  2092	        uint8_t bit=((uint8_t)stegoPayload[charIdx]>>bitPos)&0x01;
  2093	        b=(b&0xFE)|bit; stegoPixelIdx++;
  2094	      }
  2095	      rowBuf[x*3+0]=b;rowBuf[x*3+1]=g;rowBuf[x*3+2]=r;
  2096	    }
  2097	    fwrite(rowBuf,1,rowSize,f);
  2098	    if(y%30==0) esp_task_wdt_reset();
  2099	  }
  2100	  fclose(f); return true;
  2101	}
  2102
  2103	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2104	//  BMP LOAD
  2105	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2106	uint16_t* loadBMP(const char* path,uint16_t* outW,uint16_t* outH){
  2107	  FILE* f=fopen(path,"rb"); if(!f) return nullptr;
  2108	  uint8_t hdr[54];
  2109	  if(fread(hdr,1,54,f)!=54){fclose(f);return nullptr;}
  2110	  if(hdr[0]!='B'||hdr[1]!='M'){fclose(f);return nullptr;}
  2111	  uint32_t dataOffset=(uint32_t)hdr[10]|((uint32_t)hdr[11]<<8)|((uint32_t)hdr[12]<<16)|((uint32_t)hdr[13]<<24);
  2112	  int32_t bmpW=(int32_t)((uint32_t)hdr[18]|((uint32_t)hdr[19]<<8)|((uint32_t)hdr[20]<<16)|((uint32_t)hdr[21]<<24));
  2113	  int32_t bmpH=(int32_t)((uint32_t)hdr[22]|((uint32_t)hdr[23]<<8)|((uint32_t)hdr[24]<<16)|((uint32_t)hdr[25]<<24));
  2114	  bool topDown=false; if(bmpH<0){topDown=true;bmpH=-bmpH;}
  2115	  uint16_t bpp=(uint16_t)hdr[28]|((uint16_t)hdr[29]<<8);
  2116	  if(bpp!=24){fclose(f);return nullptr;}
  2117	  if(bmpW<=0||bmpH<=0||bmpW>4096||bmpH>4096){fclose(f);return nullptr;}
  2118	  size_t pixCount=(size_t)bmpW*(size_t)bmpH;
  2119	  uint16_t* buf=(uint16_t*)ps_malloc(pixCount*sizeof(uint16_t));
  2120	  if(!buf) buf=(uint16_t*)malloc(pixCount*sizeof(uint16_t));
  2121	  if(!buf){fclose(f);return nullptr;}
  2122	  int rowSize=((bmpW*3+3)/4)*4;
  2123	  uint8_t* rowBuf=(uint8_t*)malloc(rowSize);
  2124	  if(!rowBuf){free(buf);fclose(f);return nullptr;}
  2125	  fseek(f,(long)dataOffset,SEEK_SET);
  2126	  for(int row=0;row<bmpH;row++){
  2127	    if(fread(rowBuf,1,rowSize,f)!=(size_t)rowSize) memset(rowBuf,0,rowSize);
  2128	    int destRow=topDown?row:(bmpH-1-row);
  2129	    uint16_t* destLine=buf+(size_t)destRow*bmpW;
  2130	    for(int x=0;x<bmpW;x++){
  2131	      uint8_t b=rowBuf[x*3+0],g=rowBuf[x*3+1],r=rowBuf[x*3+2];
  2132	      uint16_t px_le=((uint16_t)(r&0xF8)<<8)|((uint16_t)(g&0xFC)<<3)|((uint16_t)(b>>3));
  2133	      destLine[x]=(px_le<<8)|(px_le>>8);
  2134	    }
  2135	    if(row%30==0) esp_task_wdt_reset();
  2136	  }
  2137	  free(rowBuf);fclose(f);
  2138	  *outW=(uint16_t)bmpW;*outH=(uint16_t)bmpH;
  2139	  return buf;
  2140	}

  2211	  return jumpDigits[0]*1000+jumpDigits[1]*100+jumpDigits[2]*10+jumpDigits[3];
  2212	}
  2213	static void jumpResetToCurrentIndex() {

  2215	  jumpDigits[0]=(v/1000)%10; jumpDigits[1]=(v/100)%10;
  2216	  jumpDigits[2]=(v/10)%10;   jumpDigits[3]=v%10;

  2226	class FileStream : public Stream {
  2227	public:
  2228	  FILE* f = nullptr;
  2229	  size_t write(uint8_t) override { return 0; }
  2230	  int available() override {
  2231	    if(!f) return 0;
  2232	    long cur=ftell(f); fseek(f,0,SEEK_END);
  2233	    long end=ftell(f); fseek(f,cur,SEEK_SET);
  2234	    return (int)(end-cur);
  2235	  }
  2236	  int read() override { if(!f) return -1; uint8_t b; return(fread(&b,1,1,f)==1)?b:-1; }
  2237	  int peek() override { if(!f) return -1; int c=fgetc(f); if(c!=EOF) ungetc(c,f); return c; }
  2238	  size_t readBytes(uint8_t* buf,size_t len) override { if(!f) return 0; return fread(buf,1,len,f); }
  2239	} fileStream;

  2265	#include <TJpg_Decoder.h>

  2267	static uint16_t* _decodeTargetBuf = nullptr;
  2268	static uint16_t  _decodeTargetW   = 0;
  2269
  2270	bool tjpgdecOutput(int16_t x,int16_t y,uint16_t w,uint16_t h,uint16_t* bitmap){
  2271	  if(_decodeTargetBuf){
  2272	    for(int row=0;row<h;row++){
  2273	      int destY=y+row; if(destY<0||destY>=DISP_H) continue;
  2274	      memcpy(_decodeTargetBuf+destY*_decodeTargetW+x,bitmap+row*w,w*sizeof(uint16_t));
  2275	    }
  2276	  } else { lcd.pushImage(x,y,w,h,bitmap); }
  2277	  return 1;
  2278	}

  2280	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2281	//  Helper
  2282	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2283	static void blockingWaitAllRelease(uint32_t timeoutMs=500){
  2284	  unsigned long t0=millis();
  2285	  while(millis()-t0<timeoutMs){
  2286	    bool anyLow=(digitalRead(BTN_BOOT)==LOW||digitalRead(BTN_B)==LOW||
  2287	                 digitalRead(BTN_C)==LOW||digitalRead(BTN_D)==LOW);
  2288	    if(!anyLow) break;
  2289	    delay(10); esp_task_wdt_reset();
  2290	  }
  2291	  delay(50);
  2292	}

  2294	void drawCornerBrackets(uint16_t col=COL_WHITE){
  2295	  int x0=3,y0=3,x1=DISP_W-4,y1=DISP_H-4,L=BRACKET_LEN;
  2296	  lcd.drawFastHLine(x0,y0,L,col);lcd.drawFastVLine(x0,y0,L,col);
  2297	  lcd.drawFastHLine(x1-L+1,y0,L,col);lcd.drawFastVLine(x1,y0,L,col);
  2298	  lcd.drawFastHLine(x0,y1,L,col);lcd.drawFastVLine(x0,y1-L+1,L,col);
  2299	  lcd.drawFastHLine(x1-L+1,y1,L,col);lcd.drawFastVLine(x1,y1-L+1,L,col);
  2300	}

  2302	void drawPill(int cx,int cy,const char* text,uint16_t bgCol,uint16_t fgCol){

  2311	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2312	//  SD Mount / Unmount
  2313	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2314	bool mountSDFull(){
  2315	  sdmmc_host_t host=SDMMC_HOST_DEFAULT();
  2316	  host.max_freq_khz=SDMMC_FREQ_DEFAULT;
  2317	  sdmmc_slot_config_t slot=SDMMC_SLOT_CONFIG_DEFAULT();
  2318	  slot.clk=(gpio_num_t)SD_MMC_CLK_PIN;slot.cmd=(gpio_num_t)SD_MMC_CMD_PIN;
  2319	  slot.d0=(gpio_num_t)SD_MMC_D0_PIN;slot.width=1;
  2320	  slot.flags|=SDMMC_SLOT_FLAG_INTERNAL_PULLUP;
  2321	  esp_vfs_fat_sdmmc_mount_config_t mountCfg={
  2322	    .format_if_mount_failed=false,.max_files=5,.allocation_unit_size=16*1024};
  2323	  esp_err_t ret=esp_vfs_fat_sdmmc_mount("/sdcard",&host,&slot,&mountCfg,&sdCard);
  2324	  if(ret!=ESP_OK){sdCard=nullptr;sdTotalSectors=0;sdmmcDriverInit=false;sdSizeMB=0;return false;}
  2325	  sdTotalSectors=sdCard->csd.capacity;sdmmcDriverInit=true;
  2326	  sdSizeMB=(uint64_t)sdTotalSectors*sdCard->csd.sector_size/(1024*1024);
  2327	  return true;
  2328	}
  2329
  2330	bool remountVFSOnly(){
  2331	  if(!sdCard||!sdmmcDriverInit) return mountSDFull();
  2332	  ff_diskio_register_sdmmc(0,sdCard);
  2333	  static FATFS fatfs; char drv[3]={'0',':',0};
  2334	  FRESULT fr=f_mount(&fatfs,drv,1); if(fr!=FR_OK) return false;
  2335	  FATFS* fatfsPtr=&fatfs;
  2336	  esp_err_t err=esp_vfs_fat_register("/sdcard","",5,&fatfsPtr);
  2337	  if(err!=ESP_OK&&err!=ESP_ERR_INVALID_STATE) return false;
  2338	  return true;
  2339	}
  2340
  2341	void unmountVFSOnly(){
  2342	  esp_vfs_fat_unregister_path("/sdcard");
  2343	  ff_diskio_register_sdmmc(0,NULL);
  2344	}
