  2346	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2347	//  scanPhotoCount / scanVideoCount / scanGalleryFiles
  2348	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2349	void scanPhotoCount(){
  2350	  photoCount=0;
  2351	  DIR* dir=opendir("/sdcard"); if(!dir) return;
  2352	  struct dirent* entry;
  2353	  while((entry=readdir(dir))!=nullptr){
  2354	    String name=entry->d_name;
  2355	    bool isJpg=name.startsWith("photo_")&&name.endsWith(".jpg");
  2356	    bool isBmp=name.startsWith("photo_")&&name.endsWith(".bmp");
  2357	    if(isJpg||isBmp){
  2358	      int num=name.substring(6,name.length()-4).toInt();
  2359	      if(num>photoCount) photoCount=num;
  2360	    }
  2361	  }
  2362	  closedir(dir);
  2363	}
  2364
  2365	void scanVideoCount(){
  2366	  recVideoCount=0;
  2367	  DIR* dir=opendir("/sdcard"); if(!dir) return;
  2368	  struct dirent* entry;
  2369	  while((entry=readdir(dir))!=nullptr){
  2370	    String name=entry->d_name;
  2371	    if(name.startsWith("video_")&&name.endsWith(".mjpeg")){
  2372	      int num=name.substring(6,name.length()-6).toInt();
  2373	      if(num>recVideoCount) recVideoCount=num;
  2374	    }
  2375	  }
  2376	  closedir(dir);
  2377	}
  2378
  2379	void scanGalleryFiles(){
  2380	  multiDeleteMode = false; memset(multiDeleteSelected, 0, sizeof(multiDeleteSelected)); multiDeleteCount = 0;
  2381	  galleryCount=0;galleryScroll=0;
  2382	  DIR* dir=opendir("/sdcard"); if(!dir) return;
  2383	  struct dirent* entry;
  2384	  while((entry=readdir(dir))!=nullptr&&galleryCount<GALLERY_MAX_FILES){
  2385	    if (strncmp(entry->d_name, ".", 1) == 0) continue;
  2386	    String name=entry->d_name;
  2387	    bool isJpg=name.endsWith(".jpg")||name.endsWith(".JPG");
  2388	    bool isBmp=name.endsWith(".bmp")||name.endsWith(".BMP");
  2389	    bool isMjpeg=name.endsWith(".mjpeg")||name.endsWith(".MJPEG");
  2390	    if(isJpg||isBmp||isMjpeg){
  2391	      strncpy(galleryFiles[galleryCount],entry->d_name,31);
  2392	      galleryFiles[galleryCount][31]='\0';
  2393	      if(isMjpeg)    galleryFileType[galleryCount]=GFILE_VIDEO;
  2394	      else if(isBmp) galleryFileType[galleryCount]=GFILE_BMP;
  2395	      else           galleryFileType[galleryCount]=GFILE_JPG;
  2396	      galleryCount++;
  2397	    }
  2398	  }
  2399	  if (sdReady) thumbCacheEnsureDir();
  2400	  closedir(dir);
  2401	  for(int i=1;i<galleryCount;i++){
  2402	    char tmpN[32]; strncpy(tmpN,galleryFiles[i],31); tmpN[31]='\0';
  2403	    GalleryFileType tmpT=galleryFileType[i];
  2404	    int j=i-1;
  2405	    while(j>=0&&strcmp(galleryFiles[j],tmpN)>0){
  2406	      strncpy(galleryFiles[j+1],galleryFiles[j],31);
  2407	      galleryFileType[j+1]=galleryFileType[j]; j--;
  2408	      if(j%20==0) esp_task_wdt_reset();
  2409	    }
  2410	    strncpy(galleryFiles[j+1],tmpN,31); galleryFileType[j+1]=tmpT;
  2411	  }
  2412	}
  2413
  2414	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2415	// UI UPDATE v6.0
  2416
  2417	void thumbCacheEnsureDir() {
  2418	  struct stat st;
  2419	  if (stat("/sdcard/.cache", &st) != 0) {
  2420	    mkdir("/sdcard/.cache", 0755);
  2421	  }
  2422	}
  2423
  2424	void thumbCachePath(const char* galleryFilename, char* outPath, int outLen) {
  2425	  char base[32];
  2426	  strncpy(base, galleryFilename, sizeof(base)-1);
  2427	  base[sizeof(base)-1] = '\0';
  2428	  char* dot = strrchr(base, '.');
  2429	  if (dot) *dot = '\0';
  2430	  snprintf(outPath, outLen, "/sdcard/.cache/%s.bin", base);
  2431	}
  2432
  2433	bool thumbCacheLoad(const char* galleryFilename, uint16_t* outBuf) {
  2434	  char path[64];
  2435	  thumbCachePath(galleryFilename, path, sizeof(path));
  2436	  FILE* f = fopen(path, "rb");
  2437	  if (!f) return false;
  2438	  size_t bytesRead = fread(outBuf, 1, 72*42*2, f);
  2439	  fclose(f);
  2440	  return (bytesRead == 72*42*2);
  2441	}
  2442
  2443	void thumbCacheSave(const char* galleryFilename, const uint16_t* buf) {
  2444	  char path[64];
  2445	  thumbCachePath(galleryFilename, path, sizeof(path));
  2446	  FILE* f = fopen(path, "wb");
  2447	  if (!f) return;
  2448	  fwrite(buf, 1, 72*42*2, f);
  2449	  fclose(f);
  2450	}
  2451
  2452	bool thumbDecodeJpeg(const char* galleryFilename, uint16_t* outBuf) {
  2453	  char path[64];
  2454	  snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "/sdcard/%s", galleryFilename);
  2455	  FILE* f = fopen(path, "rb");
  2456	  if (!f) return false;
  2457	  fseek(f, 0, SEEK_END);
  2458	  size_t fsize = ftell(f);
  2459	  fseek(f, 0, SEEK_SET);
  2460	  if (fsize == 0 || fsize > 250000) { fclose(f); return false; }
  2461	  uint8_t* buf = (uint8_t*)ps_malloc(fsize);
  2462	  if (!buf) buf = (uint8_t*)malloc(fsize);
  2463	  if (!buf) { fclose(f); return false; }
  2464	  if (fread(buf, 1, fsize, f) != fsize) { free(buf); fclose(f); return false; }
  2465	  fclose(f);
  2466
  2467	  uint16_t* tmpBuf = (uint16_t*)ps_malloc(80*60*2);
  2468	  if (!tmpBuf) tmpBuf = (uint16_t*)malloc(80*60*2);
  2469	  if (!tmpBuf) { free(buf); return false; }
  2470	  memset(tmpBuf, 0, 80*60*2);
  2471
  2472	  TJpgDec.setJpgScale(4);
  2473	  TJpgDec.setSwapBytes(true);
  2474	  TJpgDec.setCallback(tjpgdecOutput);
  2475	  _decodeTargetBuf = tmpBuf;
  2476	  _decodeTargetW   = 80;
  2477	  TJpgDec.drawJpg(0, 0, buf, fsize);
  2478	  _decodeTargetBuf = nullptr;
  2479	  free(buf);
  2480
  2481	  for (int dy = 0; dy < 42; dy++) {
  2482	    int sy = dy * 60 / 42;
  2483	    for (int dx = 0; dx < 72; dx++) {
  2484	      int sx = dx * 80 / 72;
  2485	      outBuf[dy * 72 + dx] = tmpBuf[sy * 80 + sx];
  2486	    }
  2487	  }
  2488	  free(tmpBuf);
  2489	  esp_task_wdt_reset();
  2490	  return true;
  2491	}

  2493	void drawGalleryGrid() {
  2494	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  2495	  lcd.fillRect(0, 0, DISP_W, 20, COL_GRAY_D);
  2496	  lcd.drawFastHLine(0, 20, DISP_W, COL_GRAY_3);
  2497	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);
  2498
  2499	  if (multiDeleteMode) {
  2500	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_WARN);
  2501	    char hdr[32]; snprintf(hdr, sizeof(hdr), "MULTI-DEL  [%d] dipilih", multiDeleteCount);
  2502	    lcd.drawString(hdr, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(hdr)) / 2, 6);
  2503	  } else {
  2504	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_E);
  2505	    int currentPage = galleryScroll / 16 + 1;
  2506	    int totalPage = max(1, (galleryCount + 15) / 16);
  2507	    char hdr[32]; snprintf(hdr, sizeof(hdr), "GALLERY  %d item", galleryCount);
  2508	    lcd.drawString(hdr, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(hdr)) / 2, 6);
  2509	    char pageInfo[8]; snprintf(pageInfo, sizeof(pageInfo), "%d/%d", currentPage, totalPage);
  2510	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_3); lcd.drawString(pageInfo, 4, 6);
  2511	  }
  2512	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5); lcd.drawString("B=BACK", DISP_W - 46, 6);
  2513
  2514	  if (galleryCount == 0) {
  2515	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  2516	    const char* msg = "SD kosong / tidak ada file";
  2517	    lcd.drawString(msg, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(msg)) / 2, DISP_H / 2); return;
  2518	  }
  2519
  2520	  static uint16_t thumbBuf[72 * 42];
  2521	  int startIdx = (galleryScroll / 16) * 16;
  2522	  for (int i = 0; i < 16; i++) {
  2523	    int idx = startIdx + i;
  2524	    if (idx >= galleryCount) break;
  2525	    int row = i / 4, col = i % 4;
  2526	    int x = 4 + col * (76 + 2);
  2527	    int y = 22 + row * (56 + 2);
  2528
  2529	    bool isSelected = (idx == gallerySelIdx);
  2530	    lcd.fillRect(x, y, 76, 56, isSelected ? COL_GRAY_5 : COL_GRAY_D);
  2531	    lcd.drawRect(x, y, 76, 56, isSelected ? COL_WHITE : COL_GRAY_3);
  2532
  2533	    GalleryFileType ft = galleryFileType[idx];
  2534	    if (ft == GFILE_JPG) {
  2535	      if (thumbCacheLoad(galleryFiles[idx], thumbBuf)) {
  2536	        lcd.setClipRect(x + 2, y + 2, 72, 42);
  2537	        lcd.pushImage(x + 2, y + 2, 72, 42, thumbBuf);
  2538	        lcd.clearClipRect();
  2539	      } else if (thumbDecodeJpeg(galleryFiles[idx], thumbBuf)) {
  2540	        thumbCacheSave(galleryFiles[idx], thumbBuf);
  2541	        lcd.setClipRect(x + 2, y + 2, 72, 42);
  2542	        lcd.pushImage(x + 2, y + 2, 72, 42, thumbBuf);
  2543	        lcd.clearClipRect();
  2544	      } else {
  2545	        lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_7);
  2546	        lcd.drawString("JPG", x + 28, y + 20);
  2547	      }
  2548	    } else if (ft == GFILE_BMP) {
  2549	      for (int r = 0; r < 42; r++) {
  2550	        uint16_t gCol = lcd.color565(0, 0, 20 + r / 2);
  2551	        lcd.drawFastHLine(x + 2, y + 2 + r, 72, gCol);
  2552	      }
  2553	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_BMP_ACCENT);
  2554	      lcd.drawString("BMP", x + (76 - lcd.textWidth("BMP")) / 2, y + 18);
  2555	    } else if (ft == GFILE_VIDEO) {
  2556	      lcd.fillRect(x + 2, y + 2, 72, 42, COL_GRAY_2);
  2557	      int cx = x + 38, cy = y + 21;
  2558	      lcd.fillTriangle(cx - 8, cy - 8, cx - 8, cy + 8, cx + 10, cy, COL_GRAY_7);
  2559	      char vidNum[12] = ""; int vn = 0;
  2560	      for (int j = 0; galleryFiles[idx][j] && vn < 11; j++)
  2561	        if (isdigit(galleryFiles[idx][j])) vidNum[vn++] = galleryFiles[idx][j];
  2562	      vidNum[vn] = '\0';
  2563	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_VID_ACCENT);
  2564	      lcd.drawString(vidNum, x + (76 - lcd.textWidth(vidNum)) / 2, y + 34);
  2565	    }
  2566
  2567	    if (multiDeleteMode && multiDeleteSelected[idx]) {
  2568	      lcd.fillRect(x + 2, y + 2, 10, 10, COL_AI_WARN);
  2569	      lcd.drawRect(x + 2, y + 2, 10, 10, COL_WHITE);
  2570	    }
  2571	  }
  2572
  2573	  if (galleryCount > 16) {
  2574	    int barH = DISP_H - 22, indH = max(10, barH * 16 / galleryCount);
  2575	    int indY = 22 + (barH - indH) * (galleryScroll / 16) / max(1, (galleryCount - 1) / 16);
  2576	    lcd.fillRect(DISP_W - 4, 22, 4, barH, COL_GRAY_2);
  2577	    lcd.fillRect(DISP_W - 4, indY, 4, indH, COL_GRAY_7);
  2578	  }
  2579
  2580	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_2);
  2581	  const char* footer = multiDeleteMode ? "BOOT=pilih  B-long=konfirmasi  B=batal" : "B-long=list  BOOT=buka  D-long=AI";
  2582	  lcd.drawString(footer, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(footer)) / 2, DISP_H - 10);
  2583	}

  2594	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_WARN);
  2595	    char hdr[32]; snprintf(hdr, sizeof(hdr), "MULTI-DEL  [%d] dipilih", multiDeleteCount);
  2596	    lcd.drawString(hdr, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(hdr)) / 2, 6);
  2597	  } else {
  2598	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_E);
  2599	    int currentPage=galleryScroll/GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE+1;
  2600	    int totalPage=max(1,(galleryCount+GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE-1)/GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE);

  2605	  }
  2606	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5); lcd.drawString("B=BACK",DISP_W-46,6);
  2607
  2608	  if(galleryCount==0){
  2609	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  2610	    const char* msg="SD kosong / tidak ada file";
  2611	    lcd.drawString(msg,(DISP_W-lcd.textWidth(msg))/2,DISP_H/2); return;
  2612	  }
  2613	  int visibleEnd=min(galleryScroll+GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE,galleryCount);
  2614	  for(int i=galleryScroll;i<visibleEnd;i++){
  2615	    int rowY=24+(i-galleryScroll)*GALLERY_ITEM_H;
  2616	    uint16_t rowBg=(i%2==0)?COL_GRAY_D:COL_BLACK;
  2617	    lcd.fillRect(0,rowY,DISP_W,GALLERY_ITEM_H-1,rowBg);
  2618	    lcd.drawFastHLine(0,rowY+GALLERY_ITEM_H-1,DISP_W,COL_GRAY_2);
  2619	    lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);
  2620
  2621	    if (multiDeleteMode) {
  2622	      if (multiDeleteSelected[i]) {
  2623	        lcd.fillRect(6, rowY + 6, 8, 8, COL_AI_WARN);
  2624	        lcd.drawRect(6, rowY + 6, 8, 8, COL_WHITE);
  2625	      } else {
  2626	        lcd.drawRect(6, rowY + 6, 8, 8, COL_GRAY_5);
  2627	      }
  2628	    } else {
  2629	      lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  2630	      char num[6]; snprintf(num,sizeof(num),"%3d",i+1); lcd.drawString(num,6,rowY+8);
  2631	    }
  2632
  2633	    GalleryFileType ft=galleryFileType[i];
  2634	    if(ft==GFILE_VIDEO){ lcd.setTextColor(COL_VID_ACCENT); lcd.drawString("\x10",26,rowY+8); }
  2635	    else if(ft==GFILE_BMP){ lcd.drawRect(26,rowY+5,9,9,COL_BMP_ACCENT); lcd.setTextColor(COL_BMP_ACCENT); lcd.drawString("B",28,rowY+6); }
  2636	    else { lcd.drawRect(26,rowY+6,7,7,COL_GRAY_5); }
  2637	    uint16_t nameCol=(ft==GFILE_VIDEO)?COL_GRAY_A:COL_GRAY_C;
  2638	    lcd.setTextColor(nameCol); lcd.drawString(galleryFiles[i],38,rowY+8);
  2639	    const char* typeLabel=(ft==GFILE_VIDEO)?"VID":(ft==GFILE_BMP)?"BMP":"JPG";
  2640	    uint16_t typeCol=(ft==GFILE_VIDEO)?COL_VID_ACCENT:(ft==GFILE_BMP)?COL_BMP_ACCENT:COL_JPG_ACCENT;
  2641	    lcd.setTextColor(typeCol); lcd.drawString(typeLabel,DISP_W-28,rowY+8);
  2642	  }
  2643	  int selY=24+(gallerySelIdx-galleryScroll)*GALLERY_ITEM_H;
  2644	  lcd.drawRect(0,selY,DISP_W-4,GALLERY_ITEM_H-1,COL_GRAY_5);
  2645	  if(galleryCount>GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE){
  2646	    int barH=DISP_H-22,indH=max(10,barH*GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE/galleryCount);
  2647	    int indY=22+(barH-indH)*galleryScroll/max(1,galleryCount-GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE);
  2648	    lcd.fillRect(DISP_W-4,22,4,barH,COL_GRAY_2); lcd.fillRect(DISP_W-4,indY,4,indH,COL_GRAY_7);
  2649	  }
  2650	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_2);
  2651	  const char* footer = multiDeleteMode ? "BOOT=pilih  B-long=konfirmasi  B=batal" : "B-long=grid  D-long=AI";
  2652	  lcd.drawString(footer, (DISP_W - lcd.textWidth(footer)) / 2, DISP_H - 10);
  2653	}

  2655	void galleryUpdateHighlight(int oldSel,int newSel){
  2656	  int oldRow=oldSel-galleryScroll;
  2657	  if(oldRow>=0&&oldRow<GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE){
  2658	    int y=24+oldRow*GALLERY_ITEM_H;
  2659	    lcd.drawRect(0,y,DISP_W-4,GALLERY_ITEM_H-1,(oldSel%2==0)?COL_GRAY_D:COL_BLACK);
  2660	  }
  2661	  int newRow=newSel-galleryScroll;
  2662	  if(newRow>=0&&newRow<GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE){
  2663	    int y=24+newRow*GALLERY_ITEM_H;
  2664	    lcd.drawRect(0,y,DISP_W-4,GALLERY_ITEM_H-1,COL_GRAY_5);
  2665	  }
  2666	}
  2667
  2668	void galleryGridUpdateHighlight(int oldSel, int newSel) {
  2669	  int startIdx = (galleryScroll / 16) * 16;
  2670
  2671	  // erase old highlight
  2672	  int oldI = oldSel - startIdx;
  2673	  if (oldI >= 0 && oldI < 16) {
  2674	    int row = oldI / 4, col = oldI % 4;
  2675	    int x = 4 + col * 78;
  2676	    int y = 22 + row * 58;
  2677	    lcd.drawRect(x, y, 76, 56, COL_GRAY_3);
  2678	  }
  2679
  2680	  // draw new highlight
  2681	  int newI = newSel - startIdx;
  2682	  if (newI >= 0 && newI < 16) {
  2683	    int row = newI / 4, col = newI % 4;
  2684	    int x = 4 + col * 78;
  2685	    int y = 22 + row * 58;
  2686	    lcd.drawRect(x, y, 76, 56, COL_WHITE);
  2687	  }
  2688	}
  2689
  2690	void galleryStep(int delta){
  2691	  if(galleryCount<=0) return;
  2692	  int oldSel=gallerySelIdx;
  2693	  gallerySelIdx=(gallerySelIdx+delta%galleryCount+galleryCount)%galleryCount;
  2694	  if(gallerySelIdx==oldSel) return;
  2695
  2696	  int pageSize = galleryGridMode ? 16 : GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE;
  2697	  bool needRedraw = false;
  2698
  2699	  if(gallerySelIdx < galleryScroll) {
  2700	    galleryScroll = (gallerySelIdx / pageSize) * pageSize;
  2701	    needRedraw = true;
  2702	  } else if(gallerySelIdx >= galleryScroll + pageSize) {
  2703	    galleryScroll = (gallerySelIdx / pageSize) * pageSize;
  2704	    needRedraw = true;
  2705	  }
  2706
  2707	  if(needRedraw) {
  2708	    if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid();
  2709	    else drawGallery();
  2710	  } else {
  2711	    if(galleryGridMode) galleryGridUpdateHighlight(oldSel, gallerySelIdx);
  2712	    else galleryUpdateHighlight(oldSel, gallerySelIdx);
  2713	  }
  2714	}
  2715
  2716	bool photoLoadPixelBuf(int idx){
  2717	  if(idx<0||idx>=galleryCount||gIsVideo(idx)) return false;
  2718	  if(photoPixelBuf){free(photoPixelBuf);photoPixelBuf=nullptr;}
  2719	  photoBufW=0;photoBufH=0;
  2720	  char path[56]; snprintf(path,sizeof(path),"/sdcard/%s",galleryFiles[idx]);
  2721	  if(gIsBmp(idx)){
  2722	    uint16_t bw=0,bh=0; uint16_t* buf=loadBMP(path,&bw,&bh);
  2723	    if(!buf||bw==0||bh==0) return false;
  2724	    photoPixelBuf=buf;photoBufW=bw;photoBufH=bh; return true;
  2725	  } else {
  2726	    FILE* f=fopen(path,"rb"); if(!f) return false;
  2727	    fseek(f,0,SEEK_END);size_t fsize=ftell(f);fseek(f,0,SEEK_SET);
  2728	    uint8_t* jpgBuf=(uint8_t*)ps_malloc(fsize);
  2729	    if(!jpgBuf){fclose(f);return false;}
  2730	    fread(jpgBuf,1,fsize,f);fclose(f);
  2731	    TJpgDec.setJpgScale(1);TJpgDec.setSwapBytes(true);TJpgDec.setCallback(tjpgdecOutput);
  2732	    uint16_t iw=0,ih=0; TJpgDec.getJpgSize(&iw,&ih,jpgBuf,fsize);
  2733	    if(iw==0||ih==0){free(jpgBuf);return false;}
  2734	    uint16_t* buf=(uint16_t*)ps_malloc((size_t)iw*ih*sizeof(uint16_t));
  2735	    if(!buf){free(jpgBuf);return false;}
  2736	    memset(buf,0,(size_t)iw*ih*sizeof(uint16_t));
  2737	    _decodeTargetBuf=buf;_decodeTargetW=iw;
  2738	    TJpgDec.drawJpg(0,0,jpgBuf,fsize);
  2739	    _decodeTargetBuf=nullptr; free(jpgBuf);
  2740	    photoPixelBuf=buf;photoBufW=iw;photoBufH=ih; return true;
  2741	  }
  2742	}
  2743
  2744	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2745	//  Photo View
  2746	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2747	void photoViewRender(){
  2748	  if(!photoPixelBuf) return;
  2749	  float zf=photoZoomFactors[photoZoomLevel];
  2750	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  2751	  if(zf<=1.0f){
  2752	    int ox=(DISP_W-min((int)photoBufW,DISP_W))/2;
  2753	    int oy=(DISP_H-min((int)photoBufH,DISP_H))/2;
  2754	    int renderW=min((int)photoBufW,DISP_W),renderH=min((int)photoBufH,DISP_H);
  2755	    for(int row=0;row<renderH;row++){
  2756	      lcd.pushImage(ox,oy+row,renderW,1,photoPixelBuf+row*photoBufW);
  2757	      if(row%20==0) esp_task_wdt_reset();
  2758	    }
  2759	  } else {
  2760	    int vpW=(int)(DISP_W/zf),vpH=(int)(DISP_H/zf);
  2761	    int maxOffX=max(0,(int)photoBufW-vpW),maxOffY=max(0,(int)photoBufH-vpH);
  2762	    photoZoomOffX=constrain(photoZoomOffX,0,maxOffX);
  2763	    photoZoomOffY=constrain(photoZoomOffY,0,maxOffY);
  2764	    static uint16_t lineBuf[DISP_W];
  2765	    for(int sy=0;sy<DISP_H;sy++){
  2766	      int srcY=constrain(photoZoomOffY+(int)(sy/zf),0,(int)photoBufH-1);
  2767	      for(int sx=0;sx<DISP_W;sx++){
  2768	        int srcX=constrain(photoZoomOffX+(int)(sx/zf),0,(int)photoBufW-1);
  2769	        lineBuf[sx]=photoPixelBuf[srcY*photoBufW+srcX];
  2770	      }
  2771	      lcd.pushImage(0,sy,DISP_W,1,lineBuf);
  2772	      if(sy%20==0) esp_task_wdt_reset();
  2773	    }
  2774	  }
  2775	  if(photoZoomLevel>0){
  2776	    char zBuf[8]; snprintf(zBuf,sizeof(zBuf),"%.0f\xd7",(double)zf);
  2777	    drawPill(DISP_W/2,DISP_H-10,zBuf,COL_PILL_BG,COL_GRAY_A);
  2778	  }
  2779	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);lcd.setTextSize(1);lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_3);
  2780	  lcd.drawString("B=zoom",2,2);
  2781	  lcd.drawString("BOOT=back",DISP_W-58,2);
  2782	  if(photoZoomLevel==0){
  2783	    lcd.drawString("C=prev",2,DISP_H-10);
  2784	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_ACCENT);
  2785	    lcd.drawString("Dlong=AI",DISP_W-52,DISP_H-10);
  2786	  } else {
  2787	    lcd.drawString("C/D=pan",2,DISP_H-10);
  2788	    lcd.drawString("Blong=del",DISP_W-58,DISP_H-10);
  2789	  }
  2790	}

  2792	void photoViewDrawCaption(int idx){
  2793	  char stegoInfo[48]="";
  2794	  char path[56]; snprintf(path,sizeof(path),"/sdcard/%s",galleryFiles[idx]);
  2795	  if(gIsJpg(idx)){
  2796	    FILE* f=fopen(path,"rb");
  2797	    if(f){
  2798	      fseek(f,0,SEEK_END);long fsize_l=ftell(f);fseek(f,0,SEEK_SET);
  2799	      if(fsize_l>0){
  2800	        size_t fsize=(size_t)fsize_l,readSize=(fsize<8192)?fsize:8192;
  2801	        uint8_t* buf=(uint8_t*)ps_malloc(readSize);
  2802	        if(!buf) buf=(uint8_t*)malloc(readSize);
  2803	        if(buf){
  2804	          size_t readN=fread(buf,1,readSize,f);
  2805	          char payload[STEGO_PAYLOAD_LEN+1];
  2806	          if(stegoExtractFromJpeg(buf,readN,payload,sizeof(payload))){
  2807	            char* p1=strchr(payload,'|'),*p2=p1?strchr(p1+1,'|'):nullptr;
  2808	            if(p1&&p2){
  2809	              char num[8],ver[12]; int numLen=p2-p1-1;
  2810	              strncpy(num,p1+1,numLen);num[numLen]='\0';
  2811	              strncpy(ver,p2+1,sizeof(ver)-1);ver[sizeof(ver)-1]='\0';
  2812	              snprintf(stegoInfo,sizeof(stegoInfo)," [#%s %s]",num,ver);
  2813	            }
  2814	          }
  2815	          free(buf);
  2816	        }
  2817	      }
  2818	      fclose(f);
  2819	    }
  2820	  } else if(gIsBmp(idx)){
  2821	    char payload[STEGO_BMP_MAX_PAYLOAD+1];
  2822	    if(stegoBmpExtract(path,payload,sizeof(payload))){
  2823	      char* p1=strchr(payload,'|'),*p2=p1?strchr(p1+1,'|'):nullptr;
  2824	      if(p1&&p2){
  2825	        char num[8],ver[12]; int numLen=p2-p1-1;
  2826	        strncpy(num,p1+1,numLen);num[numLen]='\0';
  2827	        strncpy(ver,p2+1,sizeof(ver)-1);ver[sizeof(ver)-1]='\0';
  2828	        snprintf(stegoInfo,sizeof(stegoInfo)," [#%s %s]",num,ver);
  2829	      }
  2830	    }
  2831	  }
  2832	  int photoSeq=0,photoTotal=0;
  2833	  for(int i=0;i<galleryCount;i++){
  2834	    if(gIsPhoto(i)){photoTotal++;if(i<=idx)photoSeq=photoTotal;}
  2835	  }
  2836	  const char* typeTag=gIsBmp(idx)?" [BMP]":"";
  2837	  lcd.fillRect(0,DISP_H-16,DISP_W,16,COL_GRAY_D);
  2838	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);
  2839	  char bar[56],barFull[88];
  2840	  snprintf(bar,sizeof(bar),"< %d / %d >%s%s",photoSeq,photoTotal,typeTag,stegoInfo);
  2841	  snprintf(barFull,sizeof(barFull),"< %d / %d >  %s%s%s",photoSeq,photoTotal,galleryFiles[idx],typeTag,stegoInfo);
  2842	  const char* barStr=(lcd.textWidth(barFull)<DISP_W-4)?barFull:bar;
  2843	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_A);
  2844	  lcd.drawString(barStr,(DISP_W-lcd.textWidth(barStr))/2,DISP_H-13);
  2845	}

  2924	void showPhotoView(int idx){
  2925	  if(idx<0||idx>=galleryCount||gIsVideo(idx)) return;
  2926	  photoViewIndex=idx;

  2928	  photoZoomLevel=0;photoZoomOffX=0;photoZoomOffY=0;

  2930	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  2931	  const char* loadMsg=gIsBmp(idx)?"loading BMP...":"loading...";
  2932	  lcd.drawString(loadMsg,DISP_W/2-30,DISP_H/2-4);
  2933	  if(!photoLoadPixelBuf(idx)){
  2934	    lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);

  2936	    delay(1500);lcd.setRotation(3); resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_GALLERY; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();return;
  2937	  }
  2938	  photoViewRender();
  2939	  photoViewDrawCaption(idx);
  2940	  photoViewCaptionUntilMs=millis()+2000;photoViewCaptionVisible=true;
  2941	  resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_PHOTO_VIEW;
  2942	}

  2960	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2961	//  Menu LED
  2962	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  2963	void drawLedMenu(int sel){
  2964	  int mw=220,mh=114,mx=(DISP_W-mw)/2,my=(DISP_H-mh)/2;
  2965	  lcd.fillRoundRect(mx,my,mw,mh,10,COL_GRAY_D);
  2966	  lcd.drawRoundRect(mx,my,mw,mh,10,COL_GRAY_5);
  2967	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);lcd.setTextSize(1);
  2968	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_E);
  2969	  const char* title="LED FLASH";
  2970	  lcd.drawString(title,mx+(mw-lcd.textWidth(title))/2,my+7);
  2971	  lcd.drawFastHLine(mx+10,my+19,mw-20,COL_GRAY_3);
  2972	  for(int i=0;i<3;i++){
  2973	    int iy=my+24+i*24;
  2974	    bool isChecked=false;
  2975	    if(i==0) isChecked = (!ledFlashEnabled && !neoFlashEnabled);
  2976	    else if(i==1) isChecked = (ledFlashEnabled && !neoFlashEnabled);
  2977	    else if(i==2) isChecked = (ledFlashEnabled && neoFlashEnabled);
  2978	    bool isHighlight=(i==sel);
  2979	    lcd.fillRect(mx+8,iy,mw-16,18,isHighlight?COL_GRAY_5:COL_GRAY_D);
  2980	    lcd.setTextColor(isHighlight?COL_WHITE:(isChecked?COL_GRAY_E:COL_GRAY_7));
  2981	    lcd.drawRect(mx+12,iy+5,8,8,isHighlight?COL_WHITE:COL_GRAY_5);
  2982	    if(isChecked){lcd.drawFastHLine(mx+14,iy+9,4,isHighlight?COL_WHITE:COL_GRAY_E);lcd.drawFastVLine(mx+14,iy+7,4,isHighlight?COL_WHITE:COL_GRAY_E);}
  2983	    if(i==0)      lcd.drawString("LED OFF   - tanpa flash",mx+24,iy+5);
  2984	    else if(i==1) lcd.drawString("LED ON    - flash LED saja",mx+24,iy+5);
  2985	    else if(i==2) lcd.drawString("LED+NEO   - flash LED & NeoPixel putih",mx+24,iy+5);
  2986	  }
  2987	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_3);

  3138	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3139	//  MJPEG Player
  3140	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3141	bool mjpegOpen(const char* filename){
  3142	  char path[48]; snprintf(path,sizeof(path),"/sdcard/%s",filename);
  3143	  if(mjpegFile){fclose(mjpegFile);mjpegFile=nullptr;}
  3144	  if(mjpegBuf){free(mjpegBuf);mjpegBuf=nullptr;}
  3145	  mjpegBuf=(uint8_t*)malloc(MJPEG_BUF_SIZE);
  3146	  if(!mjpegBuf) return false;
  3147	  mjpegFile=fopen(path,"rb");
  3148	  if(!mjpegFile){free(mjpegBuf);mjpegBuf=nullptr;return false;}
  3149
  3150	  mjpegActualFps = 15.0f;
  3151	  char iniPath[48]; strncpy(iniPath, path, sizeof(iniPath)-1);
  3152	  char* dot = strrchr(iniPath, '.');
  3153	  if(dot) strcpy(dot, ".ini");
  3154	  FILE* fIni = fopen(iniPath, "r");
  3155	  if(fIni){
  3156	    char line[48];
  3157	    while(fgets(line, sizeof(line), fIni)){
  3158	      sscanf(line, "fps=%f", &mjpegActualFps);
  3159	    }
  3160	    fclose(fIni);
  3161	  }
  3162
  3163	  fileStream.f=mjpegFile;
  3164	  mjpeg.setup(&fileStream,mjpegBuf,jpegDrawCallback,true,0,0,DISP_W,DISP_H);
  3165	  strncpy(mjpegPath,filename,sizeof(mjpegPath)-1);
  3166	  strncpy(mjpegPathSaved,filename,sizeof(mjpegPathSaved)-1);
  3167	  mjpegFrame=0;mjpegPlaying=true;mjpegPaused=false;mjpegNotifUntilMs=0;
  3168	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK); return true;
  3169	}
  3170	void mjpegClose(){
  3171	  if(mjpegFile){fclose(mjpegFile);mjpegFile=nullptr;}
  3172	  if(mjpegBuf){free(mjpegBuf);mjpegBuf=nullptr;}
  3173	  fileStream.f=nullptr;
  3174	  mjpegPlaying=false;mjpegPaused=false;mjpegFrame=0;mjpegNotifUntilMs=0;
  3175	}

  3200
  3201	void loopMjpegPlayer(){
  3202	  if(!mjpegPlaying) return;
  3203	  if(!mjpegPaused){
  3204	    bool ok=mjpeg.readMjpegBuf();
  3205	    if(!ok){
  3206	      if(mjpegLoop){
  3207	        mjpegClose();
  3208	        if(!mjpegOpen(mjpegPathSaved)){lcd.setRotation(3); resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_GALLERY; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();}
  3209	        return;
  3210	      }
  3211	      mjpegClose();lcd.setRotation(3); resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_GALLERY; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();return;
  3212	    }
  3213	    mjpeg.drawJpg();mjpegFrame++;mjpegDrawHUD();
  3214	    if(mjpegNotifUntilMs>0&&millis()>mjpegNotifUntilMs) mjpegClearNotif();
  3215	    float speed = mjpegSpeeds[mjpegSpeedIdx];
  3216	    uint32_t frameIntervalMs = (uint32_t)(1000.0f / (mjpegActualFps * speed));
  3217	    frameIntervalMs = constrain(frameIntervalMs, 16, 500); // clamp between 2fps and 62fps
  3218	    vTaskDelay(pdMS_TO_TICKS(frameIntervalMs));
  3219	  } else {
  3220	    if(mjpegNotifUntilMs>0&&millis()>mjpegNotifUntilMs) mjpegClearNotif();
  3221	    delay(16);
  3222	  }
  3223	  esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3224	}
  3225	void openMjpegPlayer(const char* filename){
  3226	  mjpegLoop=false;mjpegSpeedIdx=1;
  3227	  if(!mjpegOpen(filename)){
  3228	    lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  3229	    lcd.drawString("gagal buka file",20,DISP_H/2);
  3230	    delay(1500);resetAllButtons();drawGallery();appMode=MODE_GALLERY;return;
  3231	  }
  3232	  resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_MJPEG_PLAYER;
  3233	}

  3250
  3251	void startRecording() {
  3252	  if (!sdReady || recActive) return;
  3253	  neoSolid(180, 0, 0);
  3254	  if (!sdReady || recActive) return;
  3255	  recVideoCount++;
  3256	  char path[48]; snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "/sdcard/video_%04d.mjpeg", recVideoCount);
  3257	  recFile = fopen(path, "wb");
  3258	  if (!recFile) { recVideoCount--; return; }
  3259	  g_eisOffX = EIS_CROP_X; g_eisOffY = EIS_CROP_Y;
  3260	  g_eisBiasX = 0; g_eisBiasY = 0;
  3261	  recFrameCount = 0; recStartMs = millis(); recActive = true;
  3262	  char buf[20]; snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "REC #%04d", recVideoCount);
  3263	  islandPush(NOTIF_REC, buf);
  3264	}
  3265
  3266	void stopRecording(){
  3267	  if(!recActive||!recFile) return;
  3268	  fclose(recFile);recFile=nullptr;recActive=false;
  3269	  float actualFps = (float)recFrameCount / ((millis() - recStartMs) / 1000.0f);
  3270	  char iniPath[48]; snprintf(iniPath, sizeof(iniPath), "/sdcard/video_%04d.ini", recVideoCount);
  3271	  FILE* fIni = fopen(iniPath, "w");
  3272	  if(fIni){
  3273	    fprintf(fIni, "fps=%.2f
  3274	", actualFps);
  3275	    fprintf(fIni, "frames=%d
  3276	", recFrameCount);
  3277	    fclose(fIni);
  3278	  }
  3279	  unsigned long dur=(millis()-recStartMs)/1000;
  3280	  char buf[28]; snprintf(buf,sizeof(buf),"%df  %02lu:%02lu",recFrameCount,dur/60,dur%60);
  3281	  islandPush(NOTIF_OK,buf);
  3282	  neoBurst(0, 180, 0, 3); neoOff();
  3283	  fpsLastTime=millis();fpsFrameCount=0;
  3284	}
  3285	void recordFrame() {
  3286	  if (!recActive || !recFile) return;
  3287	  camera_fb_t* fb = esp_camera_fb_get();
  3288	  if (!fb) { esp_task_wdt_reset(); return; }
  3289	  if (eisEnabled && fb->format == PIXFORMAT_RGB565) {
  3290	    uint16_t* src = (uint16_t*)fb->buf;
  3291	    uint16_t* tmp = (uint16_t*)ps_malloc(DISP_W * DISP_H * 2);
  3292	    if (!tmp) tmp = (uint16_t*)malloc(DISP_W * DISP_H * 2);
  3293	    if (tmp) {
  3294	      int sx = constrain((int)g_eisOffX, 0, DISP_W - EIS_VP_W); int sy = constrain((int)g_eisOffY, 0, DISP_H - EIS_VP_H);
  3295	      for (int y = 0; y < DISP_H; y++) {
  3296	        int srY = sy + (y * EIS_VP_H / DISP_H);
  3297	        for (int x = 0; x < DISP_W; x++) {
  3298	          int srX = sx + (x * EIS_VP_W / DISP_W);
  3299	          tmp[y * DISP_W + x] = src[srY * DISP_W + srX];
  3300	        }
  3301	      }
  3302	      uint8_t* out = nullptr; size_t oLen = 0;
  3303	      camera_fb_t fk = *fb; fk.buf = (uint8_t*)tmp; fk.width = DISP_W; fk.height = DISP_H;
  3304	      int recQ = hdCaptureEnabled ? map(hdCaptureQuality, 1, 10, 95, 50) : 85;
  3305	      if (frame2jpg(&fk, recQ, &out, &oLen)) { fwrite(out, 1, oLen, recFile); recFrameCount++; free(out); }
  3306	      if (recFrameCount % 3 == 0) {
  3307	        int dw = min((int)DISP_W, (int)lcd.width());
  3308	        int dh = min((int)DISP_H, (int)lcd.height());
  3309	        lcd.pushImage(0, 0, dw, dh, tmp);
  3310	      }
  3311	      free(tmp);
  3312	    }
  3313	    esp_camera_fb_return(fb); return;
  3314	  }
  3315	  uint8_t* jpg = nullptr; size_t jLen = 0; bool ok = false;
  3316	  if (fb->format == PIXFORMAT_JPEG) { jpg = fb->buf; jLen = fb->len; ok = true; }
  3317	  else { int recQ = hdCaptureEnabled ? map(hdCaptureQuality, 1, 10, 95, 50) : 85;
  3318	  ok = frame2jpg(fb, recQ, &jpg, &jLen); }
  3319	  if (ok && jpg) {
  3320	    fwrite(jpg, 1, jLen, recFile); recFrameCount++;
  3321	    if (fb->format != PIXFORMAT_JPEG) free(jpg);
  3322	  }
  3323	  if (recFrameCount % 3 == 0 && fb->format == PIXFORMAT_RGB565 && fb->width == DISP_W) {
  3324	    int dw = min((int)fb->width, (int)lcd.width());
  3325	    int dh = min((int)fb->height, (int)lcd.height());
  3326	    lcd.pushImage(0, 0, dw, dh, (uint16_t*)fb->buf);
  3327	  }
  3328	  esp_camera_fb_return(fb); esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3329	}

  3331	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3332	//  USB MSC
  3333	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3334	static int32_t onRead(uint32_t lba,uint32_t offset,void* buffer,uint32_t bufsize){
  3335	  (void)offset;
  3336	  if(!sdCard||!sdmmcDriverInit) return -1;
  3337	  return(sdmmc_read_sectors(sdCard,buffer,lba,bufsize/512)==ESP_OK)?(int32_t)bufsize:-1;
  3338	}
  3339	static int32_t onWrite(uint32_t lba,uint32_t offset,uint8_t* buffer,uint32_t bufsize){
  3340	  (void)offset;
  3341	  if(!sdCard||!sdmmcDriverInit) return -1;
  3342	  return(sdmmc_write_sectors(sdCard,buffer,lba,bufsize/512)==ESP_OK)?(int32_t)bufsize:-1;
  3343	}

  3345	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3346	//  FPS
  3347	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3348	void updateFPS(){
  3349	  fpsFrameCount++;
  3350	  unsigned long elapsed=millis()-fpsLastTime;
  3351	  if(elapsed>=500){
  3352	    float newFps=fpsFrameCount*1000.0f/elapsed;
  3353	    fpsValue=fpsValue*0.7f+newFps*0.3f;
  3354	    fpsFrameCount=0;fpsLastTime=millis();
  3355	  }
  3356	}
  3357
  3358	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3359	//  Exposure
  3360	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3361
  3362	static uint8_t mpuCalcOrientation(float pitch, float roll) {
  3363	  if (fabsf(roll) < 45.0f && fabsf(pitch) < 45.0f) return 0;
  3364	  if (roll > 45.0f) return 1;
  3365	  if (roll < -45.0f) return 3;
  3366	  if (pitch > 45.0f) return 2;
  3367	  return 0;
  3368	}
  3369
  3370	struct KalmanAngle {
  3371	  float angle   = 0.0f;
  3372	  float bias    = 0.0f;
  3373	  float P[2][2] = {{1,0},{0,1}};
  3374	  float Q_angle = 0.001f;
  3375	  float Q_bias  = 0.003f;
  3376
  3377	  float update(float newAngle, float newRate, float dt) {
  3378	    float rate = newRate - bias;
  3379	    angle += dt * rate;
  3380	    P[0][0] += dt * (dt * P[1][1] - P[0][1] - P[1][0] + Q_angle);
  3381	    P[0][1] -= dt * P[1][1];
  3382	    P[1][0] -= dt * P[1][1];
  3383	    P[1][1] += Q_bias * dt;
  3384	    float S = P[0][0] + mpuKalmanRmeas;
  3385	    float K[2] = { P[0][0] / S, P[1][0] / S };
  3386	    float y = newAngle - angle;
  3387	    angle += K[0] * y;
  3388	    bias  += K[1] * y;
  3389	    P[0][0] -= K[0] * P[0][0];
  3390	    P[0][1] -= K[0] * P[0][1];
  3391	    P[1][0] -= K[1] * P[0][0];
  3392	    P[1][1] -= K[1] * P[0][1];
  3393	    return angle;
  3394	  }
  3395	};
  3396
  3397	static KalmanAngle kalmanX, kalmanY;
  3398	static uint32_t kalmanLastMs = 0;
  3399
  3400	void mpuTick() {
  3401	  esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3402	  if (!g_mpuOk) return;
  3403	  uint32_t now = millis();
  3404	  if (now - g_mpuLastMs < MPU_READ_MS) return;
  3405	  g_mpuLastMs = now;
  3406
  3407	  sensors_event_t a, g_ev, t;
  3408	  mpu.getEvent(&a, &g_ev, &t);
  3409
  3410	  g_accX = a.acceleration.x;
  3411	  g_accY = a.acceleration.y;
  3412	  g_accZ = a.acceleration.z;
  3413
  3414	  // Pure accelerometer + offset correction
  3415	  float ax = a.acceleration.x - g_accOffX;
  3416	  float ay = a.acceleration.y - g_accOffY;
  3417	  float az = a.acceleration.z - g_accOffZ;
  3418
  3419	  float rawTiltX = atan2f(ay, az) * RAD_TO_DEG;
  3420	  float rawTiltY = atan2f(-ax, sqrtf(ay * ay + az * az)) * RAD_TO_DEG;
  3421
  3422	  float dt = (kalmanLastMs == 0) ? 0.08f : (now - kalmanLastMs) / 1000.0f;
  3423	  kalmanLastMs = now;
  3424	  dt = constrain(dt, 0.01f, 0.2f);
  3425
  3426	  g_tiltX = kalmanX.update(rawTiltX, g_gyroX * RAD_TO_DEG, dt);
  3427	  g_tiltY = kalmanY.update(rawTiltY, g_gyroY * RAD_TO_DEG, dt);
  3428
  3429	  // Keep gyro lines unchanged (radians for EIS consistency)
  3430	  g_gyroX = g_ev.gyro.x - g_gyroCalX;
  3431	  g_gyroY = g_ev.gyro.y - g_gyroCalY;
  3432	  g_gyroZ = g_ev.gyro.z - g_gyroCalZ;
  3433
  3434	  g_tilted = (fabsf(g_tiltX) > MPU_TILT_DEG || fabsf(g_tiltY) > MPU_TILT_DEG);
  3435
  3436	  float mag = sqrtf(ax * ax + ay * ay + az * az);
  3437	  static float magFiltered = 9.8f;
  3438	  static int shakeCount = 0;
  3439	  magFiltered = magFiltered * 0.92f + mag * 0.08f;
  3440	  bool rawShake = (fabsf(magFiltered - 9.8f) > 3.0f);
  3441	  if (rawShake) shakeCount = min(shakeCount + 1, 5);
  3442	  else          shakeCount = max(shakeCount - 1, 0);
  3443	  g_shake = (shakeCount >= 3);
  3444	  if (g_shake) neoSpin(200, 60, 0);
  3445
  3446	  g_orientation = mpuCalcOrientation(g_tiltY, g_tiltX);
  3447
  3448	  if (autoRotateEnabled && appMode == MODE_VIEWFINDER) {
  3449	    uint8_t r = 3;
  3450	    if (g_tiltX > 45.0f) r = 2;
  3451	    else if (g_tiltX < -45.0f) r = 0;
  3452	    if (lcd.getRotation() != r) lcd.setRotation(r);
  3453	  }
  3454
  3455	  if (eisEnabled) {
  3456	    float gmag = sqrtf(g_gyroX * g_gyroX + g_gyroY * g_gyroY);
  3457	    if (gmag < 0.05f) {
  3458	      g_eisBiasX = g_eisBiasX * (1.0f - EIS_ALPHA) + g_gyroX * EIS_ALPHA;
  3459	      g_eisBiasY = g_eisBiasY * (1.0f - EIS_ALPHA) + g_gyroY * EIS_ALPHA;
  3460	    }
  3461	    float gdx = g_gyroX - g_eisBiasX, gdy = g_gyroY - g_eisBiasY;
  3462	    float tx = g_eisOffX + (gdx * 0.08f * 306.0f);
  3463	    float ty = g_eisOffY + (gdy * 0.08f * 306.0f);
  3464	    tx = tx * (1.0f - EIS_ALPHA) + (float)EIS_CROP_X * EIS_ALPHA;
  3465	    ty = ty * (1.0f - EIS_ALPHA) + (float)EIS_CROP_Y * EIS_ALPHA;
  3466	    tx = constrain(tx, 0.0f, (float)EIS_CROP_X * 2.0f);
  3467	    ty = constrain(ty, 0.0f, (float)EIS_CROP_Y * 2.0f);
  3468	    g_eisOffX = g_eisOffX * 0.85f + tx * 0.15f;
  3469	    g_eisOffY = g_eisOffY * 0.85f + ty * 0.15f;
  3470	  }
  3471
  3472	  if (sdReady && (now - g_mpuLogMs) >= MPU_LOG_MS && mpuLogEnabled) {
  3473	    g_mpuLogMs = now;
  3474	    FILE* lf = fopen("/sdcard/mpu_log.csv", "a");
  3475	    if (lf) {
  3476	      if (ftell(lf) == 0) fprintf(lf, "millis,tiltX,tiltY,gyroX,gyroY,gyroZ,orient
  3477	");
  3478	      fprintf(lf, "%lu,%.2f,%.2f,%.3f,%.3f,%.3f,%d
  3479	",
  3480	              (unsigned long)now, g_tiltX, g_tiltY, g_gyroX, g_gyroY, g_gyroZ, (int)g_orientation);
  3481	      fclose(lf);
  3482	    }
  3483	    esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3484	  }
  3485	}
  3486
  3487	void mpuDrawIndicator() {
  3488	  if (!g_mpuOk) return;
  3489	  char buf[16]; uint16_t col;
  3490	  bool showTilt = g_tilted && (fabsf(g_tiltX) > mpuTiltDeadzone || fabsf(g_tiltY) > mpuTiltDeadzone);
  3491	  if (g_shake) { strncpy(buf, "SHAKE!", sizeof(buf)); col=0xFD20; neoSpin(200, 60, 0); } // COL_WARN
  3492	  else if (showTilt) {
  3493	    if (fabsf(g_tiltX) >= fabsf(g_tiltY))
  3494	      snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%.0f\xb0 %s", fabsf(g_tiltX), g_tiltX > 0 ? "R" : "L");
  3495	    else
  3496	      snprintf(buf, sizeof(buf), "%.0f\xb0 %s", fabsf(g_tiltY), g_tiltY > 0 ? "F" : "B");
  3497	    col=0xFD20; // COL_WARN
  3498	    float tilt = sqrtf(g_tiltX * g_tiltX + g_tiltY * g_tiltY);
  3499	    if (tilt > 30.0f) neoSolid(200, 80, 0); else neoSolid(180, 150, 0);
  3500	  } else { strncpy(buf, "LEVEL", sizeof(buf)); col=0x7BCF; neoSolid(0, 40, 0); } // COL_GRAY_7
  3501	  drawPill(DISP_W - 36, DISP_H - 22, buf, 0x18C3, col); // COL_PILL_BG = 0x18C3
  3502	}
  3503
  3504	// Write one register to GC2145 via SCCB
  3505	static void gc2145WriteReg(uint8_t reg, uint8_t val) {
  3506	  sensor_t* s = esp_camera_sensor_get();
  3507	  if (!s) return;
  3508	  SCCB_Write(s->slv_addr, reg, val);
  3509	}
  3510
  3511	// Read one register from GC2145 via SCCB
  3512	static uint8_t gc2145ReadReg(uint8_t reg) {
  3513	  sensor_t* s = esp_camera_sensor_get();
  3514	  if (!s) return 0;
  3515	  return (uint8_t)SCCB_Read(s->slv_addr, reg);
  3516	}
  3517
  3518	// Enable or disable AEC (auto exposure control)
  3519	// reg 0xb6 bit[0]: 1=AEC on, 0=manual
  3520	static void gc2145SetAEC(bool enable) {
  3521	  gc2145WriteReg(0xfe, 0x00); // select page 0
  3522	  uint8_t val = gc2145ReadReg(0xb6);
  3523	  if (enable) val |=  0x01;
  3524	  else        val &= ~0x01;
  3525	  gc2145WriteReg(0xb6, val);
  3526	}
  3527
  3528	// Set manual shutter value (0–1200 scale mapped to 13-bit GC2145 register)
  3529	// GC2145 exposure = 13-bit: reg 0x03[4:0] high, reg 0x04[7:0] low
  3530	static void gc2145SetExposure(uint16_t expVal) {
  3531	  uint16_t mapped = (uint16_t)((uint32_t)expVal * 0x1FFF / 1200);
  3532	  mapped = constrain(mapped, 0, 0x1FFF);
  3533	  gc2145WriteReg(0xfe, 0x00); // page 0
  3534	  gc2145WriteReg(0x03, (mapped >> 8) & 0x1F);
  3535	  gc2145WriteReg(0x04, mapped & 0xFF);
  3536	}
  3537
  3538	// Set gain ceiling via page 1 reg 0x08 (0x10=lowest, 0xFF=highest)
  3539	static void gc2145SetGain(uint8_t gain) {
  3540	  gc2145WriteReg(0xfe, 0x01); // page 1
  3541	  gc2145WriteReg(0x08, gain);
  3542	  gc2145WriteReg(0xfe, 0x00); // back to page 0
  3543	}
  3544
  3545	void applyExpPreset(uint8_t preset){
  3546	  sensor_t *s=esp_camera_sensor_get(); if(!s) return;
  3547	  expPreset=preset;
  3548	  const ExpPresetCfg& c=expPresets[preset];
  3549	  if (detectedSensor == PID_GC2145) {
  3550	    // All set_* functions are set_dummy on GC2145 — use direct register access
  3551	    int aecVal  = (preset == 5) ? expManualVal  : c.aec_val;
  3552	    int gainVal = (preset == 5) ? expManualGain : c.agc_gain;
  3553	    gc2145SetAEC(c.aec_on);
  3554	    if (!c.aec_on) {
  3555	      gc2145SetExposure((uint16_t)constrain(aecVal, 0, 1200));
  3556	      // map agc_gain 0-30 to gain register 0x10-0xFF
  3557	      uint8_t gainReg = (uint8_t)map(gainVal, 0, 30, 0x10, 0xFF);
  3558	      gc2145SetGain(gainReg);
  3559	    }
  3560	    // special_effect is the ONE set_* that actually works on GC2145
  3561	    s->set_special_effect(s, c.special_effect);
  3562	    s->set_hmirror(s, HMIRROR_GC2145);
  3563	    s->set_vflip(s, VFLIP_GC2145);
  3564	    delay(150); esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3565	    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
  3566	      camera_fb_t *fb = esp_camera_fb_get();
  3567	      if (fb) esp_camera_fb_return(fb);
  3568	      esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3569	    }
  3570	    return; // skip the OV3660/generic set_* calls below
  3571	  }
  3572	  int aecVal=(preset==5)?expManualVal:c.aec_val;
  3573	  int gainVal=(preset==5)?expManualGain:c.agc_gain;
  3574	  s->set_exposure_ctrl(s,c.aec_on?1:0);
  3575	  s->set_aec2(s,c.aec2_on?1:0);
  3576	  if(!c.aec_on) s->set_aec_value(s,aecVal);
  3577	  s->set_gain_ctrl(s,c.agc_on?1:0);
  3578	  if(!c.agc_on) s->set_agc_gain(s,gainVal);
  3579	  s->set_gainceiling(s,(gainceiling_t)c.gainceiling);
  3580	  s->set_ae_level(s,c.ae_level);
  3581	  s->set_special_effect(s,c.special_effect);
  3582	  if(detectedSensor==PID_GC2145){s->set_hmirror(s,HMIRROR_GC2145);s->set_vflip(s,VFLIP_GC2145);}
  3583	  else if(detectedSensor==PID_OV3660){s->set_hmirror(s,HMIRROR_OV3660);s->set_vflip(s,VFLIP_OV3660);}
  3584	  delay(150);esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3585	  for(int i=0;i<2;i++){
  3586	    camera_fb_t *fb=esp_camera_fb_get();
  3587	    if(fb) esp_camera_fb_return(fb);
  3588	    esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3589	  }
  3590	}
  3591
  3592	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3593	//  AI — BASE64 ENCODER
  3594	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3595	static void base64Encode(const uint8_t* in,size_t inLen,String& out){
  3596	  static const char b64[]="ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
  3597	  out=""; out.reserve(((inLen+2)/3)*4+4);
  3598	  uint32_t val=0; int bits=-6;
  3599	  for(size_t i=0;i<inLen;i++){
  3600	    val=(val<<8)+in[i];bits+=8;
  3601	    while(bits>=0){out+=b64[(val>>bits)&0x3F];bits-=6;}
  3602	  }
  3603	  if(bits>-6) out+=b64[((val<<8)>>(bits+8))&0x3F];
  3604	  while(out.length()%4) out+='=';
  3605	}

  3607	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3608	//  AI — WORD WRAP
  3609	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3610	void aiWrapText(const char* text){
  3611	  aiTotalLines=0;aiScrollLine=0;
  3612	  memset(aiLines,0,sizeof(aiLines));
  3613	  const int maxW=DISP_W-12;
  3614	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);lcd.setTextSize(1);
  3615	  char word[48];int wi=0;
  3616	  char lineBuf[AI_LINE_W];lineBuf[0]='\0';
  3617
  3618	  auto flushLine=[&](){
  3619	    if(aiTotalLines<AI_LINE_MAX&&lineBuf[0]){
  3620	      strncpy(aiLines[aiTotalLines],lineBuf,AI_LINE_W-1);
  3621	      aiLines[aiTotalLines][AI_LINE_W-1]='\0';
  3622	      aiTotalLines++;lineBuf[0]='\0';
  3623	    }
  3624	  };
  3625
  3626	  const char* p=text;
  3627	  while(*p){
  3628	    if(*p=='
  3629	'){
  3630	      if(wi>0){word[wi]='\0';wi=0;
  3631	        char test[AI_LINE_W];
  3632	        snprintf(test,sizeof(test),"%s%s%s",lineBuf,lineBuf[0]?" ":"",word);
  3633	        if(lcd.textWidth(test)>maxW){flushLine();strncpy(lineBuf,word,AI_LINE_W-1);}
  3634	        else strncpy(lineBuf,test,AI_LINE_W-1);
  3635	      }
  3636	      flushLine();p++;continue;
  3637	    }
  3638	    if(*p==' '||wi>=47){
  3639	      word[wi]='\0';wi=0;
  3640	      if(word[0]){
  3641	        char test[AI_LINE_W];
  3642	        snprintf(test,sizeof(test),"%s%s%s",lineBuf,lineBuf[0]?" ":"",word);
  3643	        if(lcd.textWidth(test)>maxW){flushLine();strncpy(lineBuf,word,AI_LINE_W-1);}
  3644	        else strncpy(lineBuf,test,AI_LINE_W-1);
  3645	      }
  3646	      if(*p==' ') p++;continue;
  3647	    }
  3648	    word[wi++]=*p++;
  3649	  }
  3650	  if(wi>0){
  3651	    word[wi]='\0';
  3652	    char test[AI_LINE_W];
  3653	    snprintf(test,sizeof(test),"%s%s%s",lineBuf,lineBuf[0]?" ":"",word);
  3654	    if(lcd.textWidth(test)>maxW){flushLine();strncpy(lineBuf,word,AI_LINE_W-1);}
  3655	    else strncpy(lineBuf,test,AI_LINE_W-1);
  3656	  }
  3657	  flushLine();
  3658	}

  3741	  int y=26;
  3742	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_7);
  3743	  lcd.drawString("File config sudah dibuat di SD card.",4,y);y+=12;
  3744	  lcd.drawString("Cabut SD, isi file berikut di PC:",4,y);y+=14;
  3745	  if(missingWifi){

  3751	  }
  3752	  if(missingGemini){
  3753	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_ACCENT);
  3754	    lcd.drawString("/sdcard/gemini.ini",4,y);y+=11;
  3755	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  3756	    lcd.drawString("  key1=AIzaXXXXXXXXXXXX",4,y);y+=11;
  3757	    lcd.drawString("  key2=AIzaXXXX  (opsional)",4,y);y+=11;
  3758	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_3);
  3759	    lcd.drawString("  (dari aistudio.google.com)",4,y);y+=13;
  3760	  }
  3761	  lcd.drawFastHLine(0,y,DISP_W,COL_GRAY_2);y+=4;
  3762	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_3);
  3763	  lcd.drawString("Setelah diisi, pasang SD & restart.",4,y);y+=12;
  3764	  if(missingGemini&&sdReady){
  3765	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_AI_ACCENT);
  3766	    lcd.drawString("D-long = kelola key di kamera",4,y);
  3767	  }
  3768	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  3769	  lcd.drawString("B=kembali",DISP_W-52,DISP_H-10);
  3770	}
  3771
  3772	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3773	//  AI — Baca foto dari SD sebagai JPEG
  3774	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3775	static uint8_t* aiLoadPhotoAsJpeg(int idx,size_t* outLen){
  3776	  char path[56]; snprintf(path,sizeof(path),"/sdcard/%s",galleryFiles[idx]);
  3777	  *outLen=0;
  3778	  if(gIsJpg(idx)){
  3779	    FILE* f=fopen(path,"rb"); if(!f) return nullptr;
  3780	    fseek(f,0,SEEK_END);size_t sz=ftell(f);fseek(f,0,SEEK_SET);
  3781	    if(sz==0||sz>300000){fclose(f);return nullptr;}
  3782	    uint8_t* buf=(uint8_t*)ps_malloc(sz);
  3783	    if(!buf) buf=(uint8_t*)malloc(sz);
  3784	    if(!buf){fclose(f);return nullptr;}
  3785	    fread(buf,1,sz,f);fclose(f);*outLen=sz; return buf;
  3786	  }
  3787	  if(gIsBmp(idx)){
  3788	    uint16_t bw=0,bh=0;
  3789	    uint16_t* pixels=loadBMP(path,&bw,&bh);
  3790	    if(!pixels||bw==0||bh==0) return nullptr;
  3791	    uint8_t* jpgOut=nullptr;size_t jpgLen=0;
  3792	    bool ok=fmt2jpg((uint8_t*)pixels,(size_t)bw*bh*2,bw,bh,PIXFORMAT_RGB565,80,&jpgOut,&jpgLen);
  3793	    free(pixels);
  3794	    if(!ok||!jpgOut) return nullptr;
  3795	    *outLen=jpgLen; return jpgOut;
  3796	  }
  3797	  return nullptr;
  3798	}

  3800	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3801	//  AI — Core HTTP call dengan multi-key fallback
  3802	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3803	bool doAICall(int idx, const char* customPrompt, const String* prebuiltBody = nullptr) {
  3804	  bool wifiOK = (wifiSSID[0]!='\0'&&strcmp(wifiSSID,"NamaWiFiKamu")!=0);
  3805	  bool geminiOK = (geminiKeyCount>0);
  3806	  if(!wifiOK) wifiOK = loadWifiConfig();
  3807	  if(!geminiOK) geminiOK = loadGeminiConfig();
  3808	  if(!wifiOK||!geminiOK){ resetAllButtons(); drawAINoConfigScreen(!wifiOK,!geminiOK); appMode=MODE_AI_NO_CONFIG; return false; }
  3809	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK); drawAIStatus("CONNECTING","menghubungkan WiFi...");
  3810	  if(WiFi.status()!=WL_CONNECTED){
  3811	    WiFi.begin(wifiSSID,wifiPass); neoSpin(0, 80, 255); unsigned long t0=millis();
  3812	    while(WiFi.status()!=WL_CONNECTED&&millis()-t0<15000){delay(300);esp_task_wdt_reset();}
  3813	    if(WiFi.status()!=WL_CONNECTED){ drawAIStatus("WiFi gagal","periksa wifi.ini"); neoBurst(180, 0, 0, 5); delay(2500);return false; }
  3814	    neoOff();
  3815	  }
  3816	  char featLabel[24]; strncpy(featLabel,AI_FEATURES[(int)aiSelectedFeature].label,sizeof(featLabel)-1);
  3817	  char statusHdr[32]; snprintf(statusHdr,sizeof(statusHdr),"AI: %s",featLabel);
  3818	  String body;
  3819	  if (prebuiltBody) { body = *prebuiltBody; }
  3820	  else {
  3821	    drawAIStatus(statusHdr,"membaca foto..."); esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3822	    size_t jl=0; uint8_t* jb=aiLoadPhotoAsJpeg(idx,&jl); if(!jb||jl==0){drawAIStatus("gagal baca foto","");delay(2000);return false;}
  3823	    drawAIStatus(statusHdr,"encode base64..."); esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3824	    String b64; base64Encode(jb,jl,b64); free(jb);
  3825	    body = "{\"contents\":[{\"parts\":[{\"inline_data\":{\"mime_type\":\"image/jpeg\",\"data\":\"" + b64 + "\"}},{\"text\":\"";
  3826	    for(const char* p=customPrompt;*p;p++){
  3827	      if(*p=='\"') body+="\\\""; else if(*p=='
  3828	') body+="\
  3829	"; else body+=*p;
  3830	    }
  3831	    body += "\"}]}]}";
  3832	  }
  3833	  int hCode=0; String resp="";
  3834	  for(int ki=0;ki<geminiKeyCount;ki++){
  3835	    int kIdx=(geminiKeyActive+ki)%geminiKeyCount;
  3836	    drawAIStatus(statusHdr,geminiKeyCount>1?("kirim... (key "+String(kIdx+1)+")").c_str():"mengirim ke Gemini...");
  3837	    neoBreath(0, 200, 200); esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3838	    WiFiClientSecure client; client.setInsecure(); HTTPClient http;
  3839	    if(http.begin(client,String(GEMINI_URL_BASE)+geminiApiKeys[kIdx])){
  3840	      http.addHeader("Content-Type","application/json"); hCode=http.POST(body);
  3841	      if(hCode==200){ resp=http.getString(); geminiKeyActive=kIdx; http.end(); break; }
  3842	      http.end();
  3843	    }
  3844	  }
  3845	  if(hCode!=200){ drawAIStatus("Gagal",("Error: "+String(hCode)).c_str()); neoBurst(180, 0, 0, 3); delay(2500); return false; }
  3846	  DynamicJsonDocument doc(8192); DeserializationError error = deserializeJson(doc, resp);
  3847	  if(error){ drawAIStatus("Parse Error","JSON invalid"); delay(2000); return false; }
  3848	  const char* text = doc["candidates"][0]["content"]["parts"][0]["text"];
  3849	  if(!text){ drawAIStatus("AI Kosong","tidak ada jawaban"); delay(2000); return false; }
  3850	  strncpy(aiResult,text,AI_RESULT_MAX); aiResult[AI_RESULT_MAX]='\0'; return true;
  3851	}

  3856	void openAIDescribeWithFeature(int idx, AIFeature feature) {

  3863	  aiDescribeBusy = false;
  3864
  3865	  if(ok){
  3866	    aiWrapText(aiResult);
  3867	    drawAIDescribeScreen();
  3868	    resetAllButtons();
  3869	    appMode = MODE_AI_DESCRIBE;
  3870	    char notifBuf[32];
  3871	    snprintf(notifBuf,sizeof(notifBuf),"%s selesai",AI_FEATURES[(int)feature].label);
  3872	    islandPush(NOTIF_INFO,notifBuf);
  3873	  } else {
  3874	    if(appMode==MODE_AI_NO_CONFIG) return;
  3875	    resetAllButtons();
  3876	    appMode = MODE_PHOTO_VIEW;
  3877	    photoViewRender();
  3878	    photoViewDrawCaption(photoViewIndex);
  3879	    char warnBuf[32];
  3880	    snprintf(warnBuf,sizeof(warnBuf),"%s gagal",AI_FEATURES[(int)feature].label);
  3881	    islandPush(NOTIF_WARN,warnBuf);
  3882	  }
  3883	}

  3888	void handleModeAIDescribe(ButtonEvent evtB,ButtonEvent evtC,ButtonEvent evtD){
  3889	  if(aiDescribeBusy) return;
  3890	  bool redraw=false;
  3891	  const int visLines=(DISP_H-34-14)/13;
  3892	  if(evtC.valid&&evtC.isShort){ if(aiScrollLine>0){aiScrollLine--;redraw=true;} }
  3893	  if(evtD.valid&&evtD.isShort){ if(aiScrollLine<aiTotalLines-visLines){aiScrollLine++;redraw=true;} }
  3894	  if(evtC.valid&&evtC.isLong){ aiScrollLine=max(0,aiScrollLine-5);redraw=true; }
  3895	  if(evtD.valid&&evtD.isLong){ aiScrollLine=min(max(0,aiTotalLines-visLines),aiScrollLine+5);redraw=true; }
  3896	  if(redraw) drawAIDescribeScreen();
  3897	  if(evtB.valid&&evtB.isShort){
  3898	    resetAllButtons();
  3899	    appMode=MODE_PHOTO_VIEW;
  3900	    photoViewRender();
  3901	    photoViewDrawCaption(photoViewIndex);
  3902	    photoViewCaptionUntilMs=millis()+2000;
  3903	    photoViewCaptionVisible=true;
  3904	  }
  3905	}

  3982	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3983	//  captureAndPreview
  3984	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  3985
  3986	// [PORTED v6.1] HDR Triple Capture
  3987	bool captureHDRFrame(const char* path, int aecVal) {
  3988	  sensor_t* s = esp_camera_sensor_get(); if (!s) return false;
  3989	  if (detectedSensor == PID_GC2145) {
  3990	    gc2145SetAEC(false);
  3991	    gc2145SetExposure((uint16_t)constrain(aecVal, 0, 1200));
  3992	  } else {
  3993	    s->set_exposure_ctrl(s, 0); s->set_aec_value(s, aecVal);
  3994	  }
  3995	  delay(250); esp_task_wdt_reset();
  3996	  for (int i = 0; i < 3; i++) { camera_fb_t* t = esp_camera_fb_get(); if (t) esp_camera_fb_return(t); esp_task_wdt_reset(); }
  3997	  camera_fb_t* fb = esp_camera_fb_get(); if (!fb) return false;
  3998	  uint8_t* jpg = nullptr; size_t jLen = 0; bool ok = false;
  3999	  if (fb->format == PIXFORMAT_RGB565) {
  4000	    int captureQ = hdCaptureEnabled ? map(hdCaptureQuality, 1, 10, 95, 50) : 85;
  4001	    ok = frame2jpg(fb, captureQ, &jpg, &jLen);
  4002	  }
  4003	  else if (fb->format == PIXFORMAT_JPEG) { jpg = fb->buf; jLen = fb->len; ok = true; }
  4004	  if (ok && jpg) {
  4005	    char payload[32]; stegoMakePayload(payload, sizeof(payload), photoCount);
  4006	    size_t sLen = 0;
  4007	    uint8_t* sBuf = stegoEmbedToJpeg(jpg, jLen, payload, (int)strlen(payload), &sLen);
  4008	    if (!sBuf) { sBuf = jpg; sLen = jLen; }
  4009	    size_t fLen = 0;
  4010	    uint8_t* fBuf = exifInjectToJpeg(sBuf, sLen, photoCount, sensorName, &fLen);
  4011	    if (!fBuf) { fBuf = sBuf; fLen = sLen; }
  4012	    FILE* f = fopen(path, "wb");
  4013	    if (f) { fwrite(fBuf, 1, fLen, f); fclose(f); }
  4014	    if (fBuf != sBuf) free(fBuf);
  4015	    if (sBuf != jpg) free(sBuf);
  4016	    if (fb->format != PIXFORMAT_JPEG) free(jpg);
  4017	  }
  4018	  esp_camera_fb_return(fb); return ok;
  4019	}
  4020
  4021	void runHDRFlow() {
  4022	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  4023	  int cx = DISP_W / 2, cy = DISP_H / 2;
  4024	  uint32_t start = millis(); bool failed = false;
  4025	  int origVal = expManualVal;
  4026	  neoFade(180,0,0, 0,180,0, 3000);
  4027	  while (millis() - start < HDR_WAIT_MS) {
  4028	    esp_task_wdt_reset(); // [PORTED v6.1] MPU tick
  4029	  mpuTick();
  4030	    float gmag = sqrtf(g_gyroX * g_gyroX + g_gyroY * g_gyroY + g_gyroZ * g_gyroZ);
  4031	    if (gmag > HDR_STABLE_THRESH) { failed = true; break; }
  4032	    lcd.setFont(&fonts::FreeSansBold9pt7b); lcd.setTextColor(0x07E0); // COL_ACCENT
  4033	    lcd.drawString("HDR CAPTURE", cx - lcd.textWidth("HDR CAPTURE") / 2, cy - 40);
  4034	    lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0); lcd.setTextColor(0x528A); // COL_GRAY_5
  4035	    lcd.drawString("tahan kamera diam...", cx - lcd.textWidth("tahan kamera diam...") / 2, cy - 15);
  4036	    int barW = 200;
  4037	    lcd.drawRect(cx - barW / 2, cy + 25, barW, 8, 0x3186); // COL_GRAY_3
  4038	    int prog = (int)(barW * (millis() - start) / (float)HDR_WAIT_MS);
  4039	    lcd.fillRect(cx - barW / 2, cy + 25, prog, 8, 0x07E0); // COL_ACCENT
  4040	    delay(30);
  4041	  }
  4042	  if (failed) {
  4043	    lcd.setFont(&fonts::FreeSansBold9pt7b); lcd.setTextColor(0xFD20); // COL_WARN
  4044	    lcd.drawString("GAGAL - tekan lagi", cx - lcd.textWidth("GAGAL - tekan lagi") / 2, cy);
  4045	    delay(1500);
  4046	  } else {
  4047	    photoCount++;
  4048	    char p0[48], p1[48], p2[48];
  4049	    snprintf(p0, sizeof(p0), "/sdcard/photo_%04d_hdr0.jpg", photoCount);
  4050	    snprintf(p1, sizeof(p1), "/sdcard/photo_%04d_hdr1.jpg", photoCount);
  4051	    snprintf(p2, sizeof(p2), "/sdcard/photo_%04d_hdr2.jpg", photoCount);
  4052	    captureHDRFrame(p0, 50); captureHDRFrame(p1, origVal); captureHDRFrame(p2, 800);
  4053	    applyExpPreset(expPreset);
  4054	    char msg[32]; snprintf(msg, sizeof(msg), "HDR OK #%04d x3", photoCount);
  4055	    islandPush(NOTIF_OK, msg);
  4056	    neoBurst(200, 150, 0, 3);
  4057	  }
  4058	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  4059	  blockingWaitAllRelease(300);
  4060	}
  4061
  4062	void captureAndPreview() {
  4063	  if (ledFlashEnabled) {
  4064	    digitalWrite(LED_FLASH, HIGH); if (neoFlashEnabled) neoWhiteFlash(); delay(150);
  4065	    for (int i = 0; i < 2; i++) {
  4066	      camera_fb_t *tfb = esp_camera_fb_get();
  4067	      if (tfb) esp_camera_fb_return(tfb); esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4068	    }
  4069	  }
  4070	  camera_fb_t* fb = esp_camera_fb_get();
  4071	  if (ledFlashEnabled) digitalWrite(LED_FLASH, LOW);
  4072	  if (!fb) { neoBurst(180, 0, 0, 5); return; }
  4073	  neoBurst(0, 80, 255, 1);
  4074	  if (fb->format == PIXFORMAT_RGB565 && fb->width == DISP_W) {
  4075	    int dw = min((int)fb->width, (int)lcd.width());
  4076	    int dh = min((int)fb->height, (int)lcd.height());
  4077	    lcd.pushImage(0, 0, dw, dh, (uint16_t*)fb->buf);
  4078	  }
  4079	  drawCornerBrackets(COL_GRAY_E);
  4080	  bool saved = false;
  4081	  if (sdReady) {
  4082	    photoCount++;
  4083	    bool isGCrgb = (detectedSensor == PID_GC2145 && fb->format == PIXFORMAT_RGB565 && fb->width == DISP_W && fb->height == DISP_H);
  4084	    if (isGCrgb && gc2145CaptureFormat == GFMT_BMP) {
  4085	      char path[48]; snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "/sdcard/photo_%04d.bmp", photoCount);
  4086	      char payload[32]; stegoMakePayload(payload, sizeof(payload), photoCount);
  4087	      saved = saveBMP(fb->buf, fb->width, fb->height, path, payload, (int)strlen(payload));
  4088	    } else {
  4089	      uint8_t* jpg = nullptr; size_t jLen = 0; bool ok = false;
  4090	      if (fb->format == PIXFORMAT_RGB565) {
  4091	        if (eisEnabled) {
  4092	          uint16_t* src = (uint16_t*)fb->buf;
  4093	          uint16_t* tmp = (uint16_t*)ps_malloc(DISP_W * DISP_H * 2);
  4094	          if (!tmp) tmp = (uint16_t*)malloc(DISP_W * DISP_H * 2);
  4095	          if (tmp) {
  4096	            int sx = constrain((int)g_eisOffX, 0, DISP_W - EIS_VP_W); int sy = constrain((int)g_eisOffY, 0, DISP_H - EIS_VP_H);
  4097	            for (int y = 0; y < DISP_H; y++) {
  4098	              int srY = sy + (y * EIS_VP_H / DISP_H);
  4099	              for (int x = 0; x < DISP_W; x++) {
  4100	                int srX = sx + (x * EIS_VP_W / DISP_W);
  4101	                tmp[y * DISP_W + x] = src[srY * DISP_W + srX];
  4102	              }
  4103	            }
  4104	            camera_fb_t fk = *fb; fk.buf = (uint8_t*)tmp; fk.width = DISP_W; fk.height = DISP_H;
  4105	            int captureQ = hdCaptureEnabled ? map(hdCaptureQuality, 1, 10, 95, 50) : 85;
  4106	            ok = frame2jpg(&fk, captureQ, &jpg, &jLen); free(tmp);
  4107	          }
  4108	        } else { int captureQ = hdCaptureEnabled ? map(hdCaptureQuality, 1, 10, 95, 50) : 85;
  4109	        ok = frame2jpg(fb, captureQ, &jpg, &jLen); }
  4110	      } else if (fb->format == PIXFORMAT_JPEG) { jpg = fb->buf; jLen = fb->len; ok = true; }
  4111	      if (ok && jpg && jLen > 0) {
  4112	        char payload[32]; stegoMakePayload(payload, sizeof(payload), photoCount);
  4113	        size_t sLen = 0;
  4114	        uint8_t* sBuf = stegoEmbedToJpeg(jpg, jLen, payload, (int)strlen(payload), &sLen);
  4115	        if (!sBuf) { sBuf = jpg; sLen = jLen; }
  4116	        size_t fLen = 0;
  4117	        uint8_t* fBuf = exifInjectToJpeg(sBuf, sLen, photoCount, sensorName, &fLen);
  4118	        if (!fBuf) { fBuf = sBuf; fLen = sLen; }
  4119	        char path[48]; snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "/sdcard/photo_%04d.jpg", photoCount);
  4120	        FILE* f = fopen(path, "wb");
  4121	        if (f) { size_t w = fwrite(fBuf, 1, fLen, f); fclose(f); saved = (w == fLen); }
  4122	        if (fBuf != sBuf) free(fBuf);
  4123	        if (sBuf != jpg) free(sBuf);
  4124	        if (fb->format != PIXFORMAT_JPEG) free(jpg);
  4125	      }
  4126	    }
  4127	    if (!saved) photoCount--;
  4128	  }
  4129	  esp_camera_fb_return(fb);
  4130	  if (saved) {
  4131	    char msg[28];
  4132	    bool isBmp = (detectedSensor == PID_GC2145 && gc2145CaptureFormat == GFMT_BMP);
  4133	    snprintf(msg, sizeof(msg), "SAVED %s #%04d", isBmp ? "BMP" : "JPG", photoCount);
  4134	    islandPush(NOTIF_OK, msg);
  4135	    neoBurst(200, 150, 0, 3); if (isBmp) neoBurst(0, 80, 200, 2); else neoBurst(0, 180, 0, 2);
  4136	  } else {
  4137	    islandPush(NOTIF_WARN, sdReady ? "WRITE ERR" : "NO SD CARD"); neoBurst(180, 0, 0, 5);
  4138	  }
  4139	  fpsLastTime = millis(); fpsFrameCount = 0;
  4140	  resetAllButtons();
  4141	}

  4143	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  4144	//  Sensor settings + Camera init
  4145	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  4146	void applySensorSettings(sensor_t *s,uint16_t pid){
  4147	  s->set_brightness(s,0);s->set_contrast(s,0);s->set_saturation(s,0);
  4148	  s->set_special_effect(s,0);s->set_whitebal(s,1);s->set_awb_gain(s,1);
  4149	  s->set_wb_mode(s,0);s->set_exposure_ctrl(s,1);s->set_aec2(s,1);
  4150	  s->set_ae_level(s,0);s->set_gain_ctrl(s,1);s->set_agc_gain(s,0);
  4151	  if(pid==PID_GC2145){
  4152	    s->set_aec_value(s,300);s->set_gainceiling(s,GAINCEILING_4X);
  4153	    s->set_hmirror(s,HMIRROR_GC2145);s->set_vflip(s,VFLIP_GC2145);
  4154	  } else if(pid==PID_OV3660){
  4155	    s->set_aec_value(s,400);s->set_gainceiling(s,GAINCEILING_4X);
  4156	    s->set_hmirror(s,HMIRROR_OV3660);s->set_vflip(s,VFLIP_OV3660);
  4157	  } else {
  4158	    s->set_aec_value(s,300);s->set_gainceiling(s,GAINCEILING_2X);
  4159	    s->set_hmirror(s,0);s->set_vflip(s,0);
  4160	  }
  4161	}
  4162
  4163	bool initCamera(){
  4164	  camera_config_t cfg;
  4165	  cfg.ledc_channel=LEDC_CHANNEL_0;cfg.ledc_timer=LEDC_TIMER_0;
  4166	  cfg.pin_d0=Y2_GPIO_NUM;cfg.pin_d1=Y3_GPIO_NUM;cfg.pin_d2=Y4_GPIO_NUM;cfg.pin_d3=Y5_GPIO_NUM;
  4167	  cfg.pin_d4=Y6_GPIO_NUM;cfg.pin_d5=Y7_GPIO_NUM;cfg.pin_d6=Y8_GPIO_NUM;cfg.pin_d7=Y9_GPIO_NUM;
  4168	  cfg.pin_xclk=XCLK_GPIO_NUM;cfg.pin_pclk=PCLK_GPIO_NUM;
  4169	  cfg.pin_vsync=VSYNC_GPIO_NUM;cfg.pin_href=HREF_GPIO_NUM;
  4170	  cfg.pin_sccb_sda=SIOD_GPIO_NUM;cfg.pin_sccb_scl=SIOC_GPIO_NUM;
  4171	  cfg.pin_pwdn=PWDN_GPIO_NUM;cfg.pin_reset=RESET_GPIO_NUM;
  4172	  cfg.pixel_format=PIXFORMAT_RGB565;cfg.frame_size=FRAMESIZE_QVGA;
  4173	  cfg.grab_mode=CAMERA_GRAB_LATEST;
  4174	  if(psramFound()){cfg.jpeg_quality=7;cfg.fb_count=2;cfg.fb_location=CAMERA_FB_IN_PSRAM;}
  4175	  else{cfg.jpeg_quality=12;cfg.fb_count=1;cfg.fb_location=CAMERA_FB_IN_DRAM;}
  4176	  cfg.xclk_freq_hz=20000000;
  4177	  if(esp_camera_init(&cfg)!=ESP_OK) return false;
  4178	  sensor_t *s=esp_camera_sensor_get();if(!s) return false;
  4179	  detectedSensor=s->id.PID;
  4180	  if(detectedSensor==PID_OV3660){
  4181	    esp_camera_deinit();delay(100);cfg.xclk_freq_hz=24000000;
  4182	    if(esp_camera_init(&cfg)!=ESP_OK){
  4183	      cfg.xclk_freq_hz=20000000;
  4184	      if(esp_camera_init(&cfg)!=ESP_OK) return false;
  4185	    }
  4186	    s=esp_camera_sensor_get();if(!s) return false;
  4187	  }
  4188	  switch(detectedSensor){
  4189	    case PID_GC2145: strncpy(sensorName,"GC2145",sizeof(sensorName));break;
  4190	    case PID_OV3660: strncpy(sensorName,"OV3660",sizeof(sensorName));break;
  4191	    default: snprintf(sensorName,sizeof(sensorName),"0x%04X",detectedSensor);break;
  4192	  }
  4193	  applySensorSettings(s,detectedSensor);
  4194	  return true;
  4195	}

  4200	void drawJumpDialog(){
  4201	  const int dw=240,dh=100,dx=(DISP_W-dw)/2,dy=(DISP_H-dh)/2;
  4202	  lcd.fillRoundRect(dx,dy,dw,dh,10,COL_GRAY_D);
  4203	  lcd.drawRoundRect(dx,dy,dw,dh,10,COL_GRAY_5);
  4204	  lcd.drawRoundRect(dx+1,dy+1,dw-2,dh-2,9,COL_GRAY_3);
  4205	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);lcd.setTextSize(1);
  4206	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_E);
  4207	  const char* title="GO TO INDEX";
  4208	  lcd.drawString(title,dx+(dw-lcd.textWidth(title))/2,dy+7);
  4209	  lcd.drawFastHLine(dx+10,dy+19,dw-20,COL_GRAY_3);
  4210	  const int boxW=26,boxH=28,boxGap=6;
  4211	  const int totalBoxW=JUMP_DIGIT_COUNT*boxW+(JUMP_DIGIT_COUNT-1)*boxGap;
  4212	  int boxStartX=dx+(dw-totalBoxW)/2;
  4213	  const int boxY=dy+24;
  4214	  for(int i=0;i<JUMP_DIGIT_COUNT;i++){
  4215	    int bx=boxStartX+i*(boxW+boxGap);
  4216	    bool isCursor=(i==jumpCursorPos);
  4217	    lcd.fillRoundRect(bx,boxY,boxW,boxH,4,isCursor?COL_GRAY_5:COL_GRAY_2);
  4218	    lcd.drawRoundRect(bx,boxY,boxW,boxH,4,isCursor?COL_WHITE:COL_GRAY_3);
  4219	    char dBuf[2]; snprintf(dBuf,sizeof(dBuf),"%d",jumpDigits[i]);
  4220	    lcd.setFont(&fonts::FreeSansBold9pt7b);
  4221	    int tw=lcd.textWidth(dBuf);
  4222	    lcd.setTextColor(isCursor?COL_WHITE:COL_GRAY_A);
  4223	    lcd.drawString(dBuf,bx+(boxW-tw)/2,boxY+6);
  4224	    if(isCursor){
  4225	      int cx2=bx+boxW/2,cy2=boxY+boxH+3;
  4226	      lcd.fillTriangle(cx2-4,cy2,cx2+4,cy2,cx2,cy2+5,COL_GRAY_A);
  4227	    }
  4228	  }
  4229	  lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);lcd.setTextSize(1);
  4230	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  4231	  char totalBuf[12]; snprintf(totalBuf,sizeof(totalBuf),"/ %d",galleryCount);
  4232	  lcd.drawString(totalBuf,boxStartX+totalBoxW+8,boxY+10);
  4233	  lcd.drawFastHLine(dx+10,dy+dh-22,dw-20,COL_GRAY_2);
  4234	  lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  4235	  const char* hint="C=- D=+  B=kursor  BOOT=ok";
  4236	  lcd.drawString(hint,dx+(dw-lcd.textWidth(hint))/2,dy+dh-18);
  4237	  unsigned long elapsed=millis()-jumpOpenMs;
  4238	  int barW=dw-20,prog=barW-(int)((long)barW*elapsed/JUMP_TIMEOUT_MS);
  4239	  prog=constrain(prog,0,barW);
  4240	  lcd.fillRect(dx+10,dy+dh-6,barW,3,COL_GRAY_2);
  4241	  lcd.fillRect(dx+10,dy+dh-6,prog,3,COL_GRAY_5);
  4242	}

  4249	void handleModeJumpInput(ButtonEvent evtBoot,ButtonEvent evtB,
  4250	                          ButtonEvent evtC,ButtonEvent evtD){
  4251	  if(millis()-jumpOpenMs>=JUMP_TIMEOUT_MS){
  4252	    jumpActive=false;lcd.setRotation(3); resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_GALLERY; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();
  4253	    islandPush(NOTIF_INFO,"JUMP BATAL (timeout)");return;
  4254	  }
  4255	  bool redraw=false;
  4256	  if(evtD.valid&&evtD.isShort){jumpDigits[jumpCursorPos]=(jumpDigits[jumpCursorPos]+1)%10;redraw=true;}
  4257	  if(evtC.valid&&evtC.isShort){jumpDigits[jumpCursorPos]=(jumpDigits[jumpCursorPos]+9)%10;redraw=true;}
  4258	  if(evtB.valid&&evtB.isShort){jumpCursorPos=(jumpCursorPos+1)%JUMP_DIGIT_COUNT;redraw=true;}
  4259	  if(evtB.valid&&evtB.isLong){jumpCursorPos=(jumpCursorPos+JUMP_DIGIT_COUNT-1)%JUMP_DIGIT_COUNT;redraw=true;}
  4260	  if(evtBoot.valid&&evtBoot.isShort){
  4261	    int target=jumpGetValue();jumpActive=false;
  4262	    if(target<1||target>galleryCount){
  4263	      lcd.setRotation(3); resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_GALLERY; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();
  4264	      char warnBuf[32]; snprintf(warnBuf,sizeof(warnBuf),"INDEX %d diluar range",target);
  4265	      islandPush(NOTIF_WARN,warnBuf);
  4266	    } else {
  4267	      int newIdx=constrain(target-1,0,galleryCount-1);
  4268	      gallerySelIdx=newIdx;
  4269	      galleryScroll=(newIdx/GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE)*GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE;
  4270	      galleryHoldDir=0;
  4271	      lcd.setRotation(3); resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_GALLERY; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();
  4272	      char infoBuf[24]; snprintf(infoBuf,sizeof(infoBuf),"JUMP #%d",target);
  4273	      islandPush(NOTIF_INFO,infoBuf);
  4274	    }
  4275	    return;
  4276	  }
  4277	  if(evtBoot.valid&&evtBoot.isLong){
  4278	    jumpActive=false;lcd.setRotation(3); resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_GALLERY; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();
  4279	    islandPush(NOTIF_INFO,"JUMP BATAL");return;
  4280	  }
  4281	  if(redraw) drawJumpDialog();
  4282	  unsigned long elapsed=millis()-jumpOpenMs;
  4283	  int barW=240-20,prog=barW-(int)((long)barW*elapsed/JUMP_TIMEOUT_MS);
  4284	  prog=constrain(prog,0,barW);
  4285	  int dx=(DISP_W-240)/2,dy=(DISP_H-100)/2;
  4286	  lcd.fillRect(dx+10,dy+100-6,barW,3,COL_GRAY_2);
  4287	  lcd.fillRect(dx+10,dy+100-6,prog,3,COL_GRAY_5);
  4288	  esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4289	}

  4294	static const char GLITCH_CHARS[]="ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ0123456789#@$%&*!?";
  4295	static const int  GLITCH_CHAR_COUNT=sizeof(GLITCH_CHARS)-1;
  4296	char glitchRandChar(){return GLITCH_CHARS[random(GLITCH_CHAR_COUNT)];}
  4297
  4298	void glitchScrambleText(const char* target,int cx,int cy,uint16_t col,int steps,int stepDelayMs){
  4299	  int len=strlen(target);
  4300	  char buf[16];memset(buf,0,sizeof(buf));
  4301	  for(int step=0;step<=len;step++){
  4302	    for(int i=0;i<len;i++) buf[i]=(i<step)?target[i]:glitchRandChar();
  4303	    buf[len]='\0';
  4304	    lcd.fillRect(cx-70,cy-8,140,16,COL_BLACK);
  4305	    lcd.setFont(&fonts::FreeSansBold9pt7b);
  4306	    int tw=lcd.textWidth(buf);
  4307	    lcd.setTextColor(col); lcd.drawString(buf,cx-tw/2,cy-7);
  4308	    for(int r=0;r<stepDelayMs/10;r++){delay(10);esp_task_wdt_reset();}
  4309	  }
  4310	}
  4311
  4312	void glitchBarsFlash(int count,int delayMs){
  4313	  for(int i=0;i<count;i++){
  4314	    int barY=random(DISP_H),barX=random(DISP_W-60);
  4315	    int barW=random(30,120),barH=(random(10)>7)?5:2;
  4316	    uint16_t barCol=(random(3)==0)?COL_GRAY_5:COL_GRAY_3;
  4317	    lcd.fillRect(barX,barY,barW,barH,barCol);
  4318	    delay(delayMs);esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4319	    lcd.fillRect(barX,barY,barW,barH,COL_BLACK);
  4320	  }
  4321	}

  4416	void enterUSBMode(){
  4417	  neoBreath(0, 80, 255);
  4418	  if(!sdReady) return;
  4419	  if(photoPixelBuf){free(photoPixelBuf);photoPixelBuf=nullptr;}
  4420	  islandForceHide();
  4421	  unmountVFSOnly();sdReady=false;
  4422	  WiFi.disconnect(true);
  4423	  esp_task_wdt_reset();msc.mediaPresent(true);esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4424	  drawUSBModeScreen();usbModeActive=true;
  4425	  resetAllButtons();
  4426	}

  4428	void exitUSBMode(){
  4429	  usbModeActive=false;msc.mediaPresent(false);esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4430	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  4431	  lcd.drawString("reconnecting sd...",(DISP_W-lcd.textWidth("reconnecting sd..."))/2,DISP_H/2-6);
  4432	  sdReady=remountVFSOnly();
  4433	  if(sdReady){
  4434	    scanPhotoCount();loadSettings();loadGeminiConfig();applyExpPreset(expPreset);
  4435	    lcd.fillRect(0,DISP_H/2-10,DISP_W,20,COL_BLACK);
  4436	    char buf2[32]; snprintf(buf2,sizeof(buf2),"sd ok  next #%04d",photoCount+1);
  4437	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_C);lcd.drawString(buf2,(DISP_W-lcd.textWidth(buf2))/2,DISP_H/2-6);
  4438	  } else {
  4439	    lcd.fillRect(0,DISP_H/2-10,DISP_W,20,COL_BLACK);
  4440	    lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  4441	    lcd.drawString("sd mount failed",(DISP_W-lcd.textWidth("sd mount failed"))/2,DISP_H/2-6);
  4442	  }
  4443	  neoOff();
  4444	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);resetAllButtons();
  4445	  appMode=MODE_VIEWFINDER;
  4446	  fpsLastTime=millis();fpsFrameCount=0;fpsValue=0.0f;
  4447	}

  4453	void neoSetup() {
  4454	  strip.begin();
  4455	  strip.setBrightness(80);
  4456	  for(int i=0; i<NEO_NUM; i++) strip.setPixelColor(i, 0, 0, 0);
  4457	  strip.show();
  4458	}
  4459	void neoSolid(uint8_t r, uint8_t g, uint8_t b) {
  4460	  g_neoMode = NEO_MODE_SOLID; g_neoR = r; g_neoG = g; g_neoB = b;
  4461	  strip.setPixelColor(0, r, g, b); strip.show();
  4462	}
  4463	void neoOff() {
  4464	  g_neoMode = NEO_MODE_OFF; strip.setPixelColor(0, 0, 0, 0); strip.show();
  4465	}
  4466	void neoBurst(uint8_t r, uint8_t g, uint8_t b, int times) {
  4467	  g_neoMode = NEO_MODE_OFF;
  4468	  for (int i = 0; i < times; i++) {
  4469	    strip.setPixelColor(0, r, g, b); strip.show(); delay(150);
  4470	    strip.setPixelColor(0, 0, 0, 0); strip.show(); if(i<times-1) delay(100);
  4471	    esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4472	  }
  4473	}
  4474
  4475	void neoWhiteFlash() {
  4476	  strip.setPixelColor(0, 255, 255, 255); strip.show();
  4477	  delay(150);
  4478	  strip.setPixelColor(0, 0, 0, 0); strip.show();
  4479	  g_neoMode = NEO_MODE_OFF;
  4480	}
  4481	void neoPulse(uint8_t r, uint8_t g, uint8_t b) {
  4482	  g_neoMode = NEO_MODE_PULSE; g_neoR = r; g_neoG = g; g_neoB = b;
  4483	}
  4484	void neoBreath(uint8_t r, uint8_t g, uint8_t b) {
  4485	  g_neoMode = NEO_MODE_BREATH; g_neoR = r; g_neoG = g; g_neoB = b;
  4486	}
  4487	void neoSpin(uint8_t r, uint8_t g, uint8_t b) {
  4488	  g_neoMode = NEO_MODE_SPIN; g_neoR = r; g_neoG = g; g_neoB = b;
  4489	}
  4490	void neoFade(uint8_t r1, uint8_t g1, uint8_t b1, uint8_t r2, uint8_t g2, uint8_t b2, uint32_t durationMs) {
  4491	  g_neoMode = NEO_MODE_OFF;
  4492	  uint32_t start = millis();
  4493	  while (millis() - start < durationMs) {
  4494	    float p = (float)(millis() - start) / (float)durationMs;
  4495	    strip.setPixelColor(0, r1+(r2-r1)*p, g1+(g2-g1)*p, b1+(b2-b1)*p);
  4496	    strip.show(); esp_task_wdt_reset(); delay(10);
  4497	  }
  4498	  strip.setPixelColor(0, r2, g2, b2); strip.show();
  4499	}
  4500	void neoRainbow() {
  4501	  g_neoMode = NEO_MODE_OFF;
  4502	  for (int i=0; i<255; i++) {
  4503	    uint32_t c = strip.ColorHSV((uint16_t)i * 257, 255, 255);
  4504	    strip.setPixelColor(0, strip.gamma32(c));
  4505	    strip.show(); delay(5); esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4506	  }
  4507	}
  4508	void neoTick() {
  4509	  uint32_t now = millis();
  4510	  if (recActive) {
  4511	    if ((now / 1000) % 2 == 0) { strip.setPixelColor(0, 180, 0, 0); }
  4512	    else { strip.setPixelColor(0, 60, 0, 0); }
  4513	    strip.show(); return;
  4514	  }
  4515	  if (g_neoMode == NEO_MODE_OFF || g_neoMode == NEO_MODE_SOLID) return;
  4516	  if (g_neoMode == NEO_MODE_BREATH) {
  4517	    float v = (exp(sin(now/1500.0*PI)) - 0.36787944) * 0.42545906;
  4518	    strip.setPixelColor(0, g_neoR*v, g_neoG*v, g_neoB*v); strip.show();
  4519	  } else if (g_neoMode == NEO_MODE_PULSE) {
  4520	    float v = (sin(now/1000.0*PI) + 1.0) / 2.0;
  4521	    strip.setPixelColor(0, g_neoR*v, g_neoG*v, g_neoB*v); strip.show();
  4522	  } else if (g_neoMode == NEO_MODE_SPIN) {
  4523	    if (now - g_neoLastMs > 100) {
  4524	      g_neoLastMs = now; g_neoState = !g_neoState;
  4525	      if (g_neoState) strip.setPixelColor(0, g_neoR, g_neoG, g_neoB);
  4526	      else strip.setPixelColor(0, 0, 0, 0);
  4527	      strip.show();
  4528	    }
  4529	  }
  4530	}

  4532	void setup(){
  4533	  Serial.begin(115200);
  4534	  Serial.println("
  4535	=== Sanzxcam v6.0 ===");
  4536	  Serial.println("[AI-MENU] 7 fitur: Describe/Scavenger/Mood/ANPR/Sky/Pest/Produce");
  4537	  Serial.println("[TRIGGER] Clong=viewfinder  Dlong=gallery  Dlong=photo view");
  4538
  4539	  galleryFiles   =(char(*)[32])     ps_malloc(GALLERY_MAX_FILES*32);
  4540	  galleryFileType=(GalleryFileType*)ps_malloc(GALLERY_MAX_FILES*sizeof(GalleryFileType));
  4541	  if(!galleryFiles||!galleryFileType){Serial.println("PSRAM alloc failed!");ESP.restart();}
  4542
  4543	  neoSetup();
  4544	  pinMode(LED_FLASH,OUTPUT);digitalWrite(LED_FLASH,LOW);
  4545	  pinMode(BTN_BOOT, INPUT_PULLUP);
  4546	  pinMode(BTN_B,    INPUT_PULLUP);
  4547	  pinMode(BTN_C,    INPUT_PULLUP);
  4548	  pinMode(BTN_D,    INPUT_PULLUP);
  4549
  4550	  setCpuFrequencyMhz(240);
  4551	  lcd.init();lcd.setRotation(3);lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  4552
  4553	  static uint16_t touchCalData[8]={3851,3630,673,3277,3965,160,772,136};
  4554	  lcd.setTouchCalibrate(touchCalData);
  4555
  4556	  TJpgDec.setJpgScale(1);TJpgDec.setSwapBytes(true);TJpgDec.setCallback(tjpgdecOutput);
  4557
  4558	  sdReady=mountSDFull();
  4559	  if(sdReady) { neoSolid(0, 80, 0); delay(3000); neoOff(); }
  4560	  else neoPulse(200, 80, 0);
  4561	  if(sdReady && sdTotalSectors > 0 && sdTotalSectors < 1000) neoPulse(180, 0, 0);
  4562	  if(sdReady){
  4563	    scanPhotoCount();
  4564	    scanVideoCount();
  4565	    loadSettings();
  4566	    loadMPUCalibration();
  4567	    loadWifiConfig();
  4568	    loadGeminiConfig();
  4569	  }
  4570
  4571	  msc.vendorID("ESP32S3");msc.productID("SD Card");msc.productRevision("1.0");
  4572	  msc.onRead(onRead);msc.onWrite(onWrite);
  4573	  msc.begin(sdTotalSectors>0?sdTotalSectors:0,512);
  4574	  msc.mediaPresent(false);USB.begin();
  4575
  4576	  bool camOK=initCamera();
  4577	  // [PORTED v6.1] MPU init
  4578	  Wire.begin(PIN_MPU_SDA, PIN_MPU_SCL);
  4579	  g_mpuOk = mpu.begin(MPU_I2C_ADDR, &Wire);
  4580	  if (g_mpuOk) {
  4581	    mpu.setAccelerometerRange(MPU6050_RANGE_4_G);
  4582	    mpu.setGyroRange(MPU6050_RANGE_500_DEG);
  4583	    applyDLPF();
  4584
  4585	    // Calibrate accelerometer offset
  4586	    g_accOffX = 0.0f;
  4587	    g_accOffY = 0.0f;
  4588	    g_accOffZ = 0.0f;
  4589
  4590	    if (g_mpuCalLoaded) {
  4591	      Serial.printf("[MPU] Skip gyro calibration, using SD: X=%.5f Y=%.5f Z=%.5f
  4592	", g_gyroCalX, g_gyroCalY, g_gyroCalZ);
  4593	    } else {
  4594	      g_gyroCalX = 0.0f; g_gyroCalY = 0.0f; g_gyroCalZ = 0.0f;
  4595	      Serial.println("[MPU] No cal file found. Gyro bias set to 0. Use CALIBRATE menu.");
  4596	    }
  4597	  } esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4598	  Serial.printf("[MPU] %s
  4599	", g_mpuOk ? "OK" : "FAIL");
  4600	  g_eisOffX = EIS_CROP_X; g_eisOffY = EIS_CROP_Y;
  4601	  bool pidOK=(detectedSensor==PID_GC2145||detectedSensor==PID_OV3660);
  4602	  uint32_t xclkHz=(detectedSensor==PID_OV3660)?24000000:20000000;
  4603
  4604	  if(camOK&&sdReady) applyExpPreset(expPreset);
  4605
  4606	  runBootSequence(sdReady,sdSizeMB,pidOK,detectedSensor,camOK,xclkHz);
  4607
  4608	  if(!camOK){
  4609	    lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);lcd.setFont(&fonts::Font0);lcd.setTextColor(COL_GRAY_5);
  4610	    lcd.drawString("camera init failed",(DISP_W-lcd.textWidth("camera init failed"))/2,110);
  4611	    while(true){neoPulse(180, 0, 0); neoTick(); delay(10); esp_task_wdt_reset();}
  4612	  }
  4613
  4614	  lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);
  4615	  fpsLastTime=millis();fpsFrameCount=0;
  4616	  neoOff();
  4617	  blockingWaitAllRelease(600);
  4618	  Serial.println("[BOOT] Fixes applied: gyro-cal, no-autokal, hd-quality-map");
  4619	  resetAllButtons();
  4620	}
