  4625	void handleModeViewfinder(ButtonEvent evt){
  4626	  galleryGridActive=false;
  4627	  if(!evt.valid){renderViewfinder();return;}
  4628	  if(evt.pin==BTN_BOOT){
  4629	    if(evt.isLong){if(sdReady) enterUSBMode();}
  4630	    else {
  4631	      if (hdrEnabled) {
  4632	        if (!g_hdrWaiting) {
  4633	          g_hdrWaiting = true; g_hdrFirstMs = millis();
  4634	        } else if (millis() - g_hdrFirstMs < HDR_DOUBLE_TAP_MS) {
  4635	          g_hdrWaiting = false; captureAndPreview();
  4636	        }
  4637	      } else {
  4638	        captureAndPreview();
  4639	      }
  4640	    }
  4641	  }
  4642	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_B){
  4643	    if(evt.isShort){if(recActive) stopRecording();else startRecording();}
  4644	    else           {openLedMenu();}
  4645	  }
  4646	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_C){
  4647	    if(evt.isShort){
  4648	      if(sdReady){
  4649	        scanGalleryFiles();
  4650	        gallerySelIdx=0;galleryScroll=0;galleryHoldDir=0;
  4651	        islandForceHide();islandNoClear=false;
  4652	        lcd.setRotation(3); resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_GALLERY; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();
  4653	      }
  4654	    }
  4655	    else{
  4656	      if(!sdReady){islandPush(NOTIF_WARN,"SD tidak tersedia");return;}
  4657	      islandForceHide();
  4658	      openAIFeatureMenu(true);
  4659	    }
  4660	  }
  4661	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_D){
  4662	    if(evt.isShort){ Serial.println("[UI] Opening Features Menu"); lcd.setRotation(3); menuFeatSel=0; drawFeaturesMenu(menuFeatSel); resetAllButtons(); appMode=MODE_FEATURES; }
  4663	    else           {openExpMenu();}
  4664	  }
  4665	}

  4691	void openMultiDeleteConfirmDialog() {
  4692	  drawMultiDeleteConfirmDialog(multiDeleteCount);
  4693	  deleteDialogOpenMs = millis();
  4694	  blockingWaitAllRelease(600); resetAllButtons();

  4698	void executeMultiDelete() {
  4699	  int deletedCount = 0;
  4700	  for (int i = 0; i < galleryCount; i++) {
  4701	    if (multiDeleteSelected[i]) {
  4702	      char path[56]; snprintf(path, sizeof(path), "/sdcard/%s", galleryFiles[i]);
  4703	      if (remove(path) == 0) {
  4704	        if (gIsJpg(i)) {
  4705	          char cachePath[64]; thumbCachePath(galleryFiles[i], cachePath, sizeof(cachePath));
  4706	          remove(cachePath);
  4707	        }
  4708	        deletedCount++;
  4709	      }
  4710	      if (deletedCount % 10 == 0) esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4711	    }
  4712	  }
  4713	  multiDeleteMode = false; memset(multiDeleteSelected, 0, sizeof(multiDeleteSelected)); multiDeleteCount = 0;
  4714	  scanGalleryFiles(); scanPhotoCount(); gallerySelIdx = 0;
  4715	  char msg[32]; snprintf(msg, sizeof(msg), "%d file dihapus", deletedCount);
  4716	  islandPush(NOTIF_OK, msg); neoBurst(0, 180, 0, 2); neoOff();
  4717	  resetAllButtons(); appMode = MODE_GALLERY; if (galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();
  4718	}

  4720	void handleModeDialogMultiDelete(ButtonEvent evt) {
  4721	  unsigned long elapsed = millis() - deleteDialogOpenMs;
  4722	  if (elapsed < DELETE_TIMEOUT_MS) {
  4723	    int dw=200, dh=88, dx=(DISP_W-dw)/2, dy=(DISP_H-dh)/2;
  4724	    int prog = (int)((dw-20) * (1.0 - (double)elapsed/DELETE_TIMEOUT_MS));
  4725	    lcd.fillRect(dx+10, dy+36, dw-20, 4, COL_GRAY_2);
  4726	    lcd.fillRect(dx+10, dy+36, constrain(prog,0,dw-20), 4, 0xF800);
  4727	  } else {
  4728	    neoOff(); multiDeleteMode = false; resetAllButtons();
  4729	    appMode = MODE_GALLERY; if (galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery(); return;
  4730	  }
  4731	  if (!evt.valid) return;
  4732	  if (evt.pin == BTN_BOOT) { neoOff(); executeMultiDelete(); }
  4733	  else { neoOff(); multiDeleteMode = false; resetAllButtons(); appMode = MODE_GALLERY; if (galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery(); }
  4734	}

  4736	void handleModeGallery(ButtonEvent evt){
  4737	  bool cHeld=btnC.isHeld(),dHeld=btnD.isHeld();
  4738	  if(cHeld&&galleryHoldDir!=-1){ galleryHoldDir=-1;galleryHoldStart=millis(); galleryLastStep=millis();galleryStep(-1);return; }
  4739	  if(dHeld&&galleryHoldDir!=1){ galleryHoldDir=1;galleryHoldStart=millis(); galleryLastStep=millis();galleryStep(1);return; }
  4740	  if(!cHeld&&!dHeld) galleryHoldDir=0;
  4741	  if(galleryHoldDir!=0){
  4742	    uint32_t held=millis()-galleryHoldStart; uint32_t interval=(held<500)?200:(held<1500)?100:50;
  4743	    if(millis()-galleryLastStep>=interval){galleryStep(galleryHoldDir);galleryLastStep=millis();} return;
  4744	  }
  4745	  if(!evt.valid) return;
  4746	  if(multiDeleteMode) {
  4747	    if(evt.pin==BTN_BOOT && evt.isShort) {
  4748	      if(galleryCount>0 && gallerySelIdx>=0) {
  4749	        multiDeleteSelected[gallerySelIdx] = !multiDeleteSelected[gallerySelIdx];
  4750	        multiDeleteCount += multiDeleteSelected[gallerySelIdx] ? 1 : -1;
  4751	        if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();
  4752	      }
  4753	    } else if(evt.pin==BTN_B) {
  4754	      if(evt.isLong) { if(multiDeleteCount > 0) openMultiDeleteConfirmDialog(); }
  4755	      else { multiDeleteMode = false; memset(multiDeleteSelected, 0, sizeof(multiDeleteSelected)); multiDeleteCount = 0; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery(); }
  4756	    }
  4757	    return;
  4758	  }
  4759	  if(evt.pin==BTN_BOOT){
  4760	    if(evt.isLong){ if(galleryCount>0) openJumpDialog(); else islandPush(NOTIF_WARN,"GALLERY KOSONG"); }
  4761	    else if(evt.isShort){
  4762	      if(galleryCount>0&&gallerySelIdx>=0&&gallerySelIdx<galleryCount){
  4763	        GalleryFileType ft=galleryFileType[gallerySelIdx];
  4764	        if(ft==GFILE_VIDEO) openMjpegPlayer(galleryFiles[gallerySelIdx]); else showPhotoView(gallerySelIdx);
  4765	      }
  4766	    }
  4767	  } else if(evt.pin==BTN_B){
  4768	    if(evt.isLong){ galleryGridMode=!galleryGridMode; galleryScroll=(gallerySelIdx / (galleryGridMode ? 16 : GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE)) * (galleryGridMode ? 16 : GALLERY_ITEMS_PAGE); if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery(); }
  4769	    else { if(photoPixelBuf){free(photoPixelBuf);photoPixelBuf=nullptr;} galleryHoldDir=0;islandForceHide();resetAllButtons(); islandNoClear=true; appMode=MODE_VIEWFINDER;lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK); fpsLastTime=millis();fpsFrameCount=0; }
  4770	  } else if(evt.pin==BTN_C&&evt.isLong){
  4771	    if(galleryCount==0){islandPush(NOTIF_WARN,"Gallery kosong");return;}
  4772	    multiDeleteMode = true; multiDeleteCount = 0; memset(multiDeleteSelected, 0, sizeof(multiDeleteSelected));
  4773	    if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();
  4774	  } else if(evt.pin==BTN_D&&evt.isLong){
  4775	    if(galleryCount==0){islandPush(NOTIF_WARN,"Gallery kosong");return;}
  4776	    if(galleryFileType[gallerySelIdx]==GFILE_VIDEO){islandPush(NOTIF_WARN,"Pilih foto, bukan video");return;}
  4777	    photoViewIndex=gallerySelIdx; strncpy(photoViewPath,galleryFiles[gallerySelIdx],sizeof(photoViewPath)-1); openAIFeatureMenu(false);
  4778	  }
  4779	}

  4781	void handleModePhotoView(ButtonEvent evt){
  4782	  static unsigned long lastPanTime=0; bool cHeld=btnC.isHeld(),dHeld=btnD.isHeld();
  4783	  if(photoZoomLevel>0&&(cHeld||dHeld)&&millis()-lastPanTime>120){
  4784	    float zf=photoZoomFactors[photoZoomLevel]; int maxOffX=max(0,(int)photoBufW-(int)(DISP_W/zf)), maxOffY=max(0,(int)photoBufH-(int)(DISP_H/zf));
  4785	    if(cHeld) photoZoomOffX=constrain(photoZoomOffX-PAN_STEP,0,maxOffX); if(dHeld) photoZoomOffX=constrain(photoZoomOffX+PAN_STEP,0,maxOffX);
  4786	    photoViewRender();lastPanTime=millis();return;
  4787	  }
  4788	  if(!evt.valid) return;
  4789	  static unsigned long lastActionTime=0; if(millis()-lastActionTime<150) return; lastActionTime=millis();
  4790	  if(evt.pin==BTN_BOOT&&evt.isShort){
  4791	    photoViewCaptionVisible=false;photoViewCaptionUntilMs=0; photoZoomLevel=0;photoZoomOffX=0;photoZoomOffY=0;
  4792	    if(photoPixelBuf){free(photoPixelBuf);photoPixelBuf=nullptr;}
  4793	    lcd.setRotation(3); resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_GALLERY; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();return;
  4794	  }
  4795	  if(evt.pin==BTN_B){
  4796	    if(evt.isLong){photoViewClearCaption();openDeleteDialog();}
  4797	    else{
  4798	      photoZoomLevel=(photoZoomLevel+1)%ZOOM_LEVELS; photoZoomOffX=0;photoZoomOffY=0;
  4799	      if(photoZoomLevel>0){
  4800	        float zf=photoZoomFactors[photoZoomLevel]; int vpW=(int)(DISP_W/zf),vpH=(int)(DISP_H/zf);
  4801	        photoZoomOffX=max(0,(int)photoBufW/2-vpW/2); photoZoomOffY=max(0,(int)photoBufH/2-vpH/2);
  4802	      }
  4803	      photoViewRender();
  4804	      }
  4805	  }
  4806	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_C && !cHeld){ photoZoomLevel=0;photoZoomOffX=0;photoZoomOffY=0; photoViewPrev(); }
  4807	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_D && evt.isLong){
  4808	    openAIFeatureMenu(false);
  4809	  }
  4810	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_D && !dHeld){ photoZoomLevel=0;photoZoomOffX=0;photoZoomOffY=0; photoViewNext(); }
  4811	}
  4812	void handleModeMjpegPlayer(ButtonEvent evtBoot,ButtonEvent evtB,
  4813	                            ButtonEvent evtC,ButtonEvent evtD){
  4814	  static unsigned long lastToggleTime=0;
  4815	  if(evtBoot.valid){mjpegClose();lcd.setRotation(3); resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_GALLERY; if(galleryGridMode) drawGalleryGrid(); else drawGallery();return;}
  4816	  if(evtB.valid&&(millis()-lastToggleTime)>=200){
  4817	    mjpegPaused=!mjpegPaused;mjpegShowNotif(mjpegPaused?"PAUSE":"PLAY");lastToggleTime=millis();
  4818	  }
  4819	  if(evtC.valid&&(millis()-lastToggleTime)>=200){
  4820	    mjpegLoop=!mjpegLoop;mjpegShowNotif(mjpegLoop?"LOOP ON":"LOOP OFF");lastToggleTime=millis();
  4821	  }
  4822	  if(evtD.valid&&(millis()-lastToggleTime)>=200){
  4823	    mjpegSpeedIdx=(mjpegSpeedIdx+1)%3;
  4824	    char spBuf[12]; snprintf(spBuf,sizeof(spBuf),"%.1f\xd7",(double)mjpegSpeeds[mjpegSpeedIdx]);
  4825	    mjpegShowNotif(spBuf);lastToggleTime=millis();
  4826	  }
  4827	  loopMjpegPlayer();
  4828	}
  4829
  4830	void handleModeMenuLed(ButtonEvent evt){
  4831	  if(!evt.valid) return;
  4832	  if(evt.pin==BTN_BOOT){
  4833	    if(menuLedSel==0){ ledFlashEnabled=false; neoFlashEnabled=false; islandPush(NOTIF_FLASH,"FLASH OFF"); }
  4834	    else if(menuLedSel==1){ ledFlashEnabled=true; neoFlashEnabled=false; islandPush(NOTIF_FLASH,"FLASH ON"); }
  4835	    else if(menuLedSel==2){ ledFlashEnabled=true; neoFlashEnabled=true; islandPush(NOTIF_FLASH,"FLASH+NEO ON"); }
  4836	    saveSettings();lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);resetAllButtons();
  4837	    islandNoClear=true;appMode=MODE_VIEWFINDER;
  4838	  }
  4839	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_B){lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);resetAllButtons();islandNoClear=true;appMode=MODE_VIEWFINDER;}
  4840	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_C||evt.pin==BTN_D){menuLedSel=(menuLedSel+1)%3;drawLedMenu(menuLedSel);}
  4841	}
  4842
  4843	void handleModeMenuFormat(ButtonEvent evt){
  4844	  if(!evt.valid) return;
  4845	  if(evt.pin==BTN_BOOT){
  4846	    gc2145CaptureFormat=(menuFormatSel==0)?GFMT_BMP:GFMT_JPG;
  4847	    char fbBuf[28];
  4848	    snprintf(fbBuf,sizeof(fbBuf),"FORMAT: %s",gc2145CaptureFormat==GFMT_BMP?"BMP+stego":"JPG+EXIF+stego");
  4849	    islandPush(NOTIF_INFO,fbBuf);saveSettings();
  4850	    lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);resetAllButtons();
  4851	    drawExpMenu(menuExpSel);
  4852	    appMode=MODE_MENU_EXP;
  4853	  }
  4854	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_B){
  4855	    drawExpMenu(menuExpSel);resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_MENU_EXP;
  4856	  }
  4857	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_C||evt.pin==BTN_D){menuFormatSel=(menuFormatSel==0)?1:0;drawFormatMenu(menuFormatSel);}
  4858	}
  4859
  4860	void handleModeMenuExp(ButtonEvent evt){
  4861	  if(!evt.valid) return;
  4862	  if(evt.pin==BTN_BOOT){
  4863	    applyExpPreset((uint8_t)menuExpSel);
  4864	    if(menuExpSel==5){
  4865	      lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);resetAllButtons();appMode=MODE_MENU_EXP_ADJ;return;
  4866	    }
  4867	    char fbBuf[24]; snprintf(fbBuf,sizeof(fbBuf),"MODE: %s",expPresetNames[menuExpSel]);
  4868	    islandPush(NOTIF_INFO,fbBuf);saveSettings();
  4869	    lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);resetAllButtons();
  4870	    islandNoClear=true;appMode=MODE_VIEWFINDER;
  4871	  }
  4872	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_B){lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);resetAllButtons();islandNoClear=true;appMode=MODE_VIEWFINDER;}
  4873	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_C){menuExpSel=(menuExpSel+5)%6;drawExpMenu(menuExpSel);}
  4874	  else if(evt.pin==BTN_D){
  4875	    if(evt.isShort){menuExpSel=(menuExpSel+1)%6;drawExpMenu(menuExpSel);}
  4876	    else if(evt.isLong&&detectedSensor==PID_GC2145){
  4877	      openFormatMenu();
  4878	    }
  4879	  }
  4880	}
  4881
  4882	void handleModeMenuExpAdj(ButtonEvent evt){
  4883	  camera_fb_t *fb=esp_camera_fb_get();
  4884	  if(fb){
  4885	    if(fb->format==PIXFORMAT_RGB565&&fb->width==DISP_W&&fb->height==DISP_H){
  4886	      int visH=DISP_H-38;
  4887	      for(int row=0;row<visH;row++)
  4888	        lcd.pushImage(0,row,DISP_W,1,(uint16_t*)fb->buf+row*DISP_W);
  4889	    }
  4890	    esp_camera_fb_return(fb);
  4891	  }
  4892	  drawExpAdjOverlay();esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4893	  if(!evt.valid) return;
  4894	  if(evt.pin==BTN_BOOT){
  4895	    char fbBuf[24]; snprintf(fbBuf,sizeof(fbBuf),"MODE: %s",expPresetNames[5]);
  4896	    islandPush(NOTIF_INFO,fbBuf);saveSettings();
  4897	    lcd.fillScreen(COL_BLACK);resetAllButtons();
  4898	    islandNoClear=true;appMode=MODE_VIEWFINDER;return;
  4899	  }
  4900	  bool changed=false;
  4901	  int step=(expManualVal<100)?10:(expManualVal<400)?25:50;
  4902	  if(evt.pin==BTN_C){expManualVal=constrain(expManualVal-step,0,1200);changed=true;}
  4903	  if(evt.pin==BTN_D){expManualVal=constrain(expManualVal+step,0,1200);changed=true;}
  4904	  if(evt.pin==BTN_B){expManualGain=(expManualGain+1)%31;changed=true;}
  4905	  if(changed) applyExpPreset(5);
  4906	}

  4908	// UI UPDATE v6.0
  4909	void handleModeDialogDelete(ButtonEvent evt){
  4910	  unsigned long elapsed=millis()-deleteDialogOpenMs;
  4911	  if(elapsed<DELETE_TIMEOUT_MS){
  4912	    int dw=200, dh=88, dx=(DISP_W-dw)/2, dy=(DISP_H-dh)/2;
  4913	    int barW=dw-20;
  4914	    int prog = (int)(barW * (1.0 - (double)elapsed/DELETE_TIMEOUT_MS));
  4915	    prog = constrain(prog, 0, barW);
  4916	    lcd.fillRect(dx+10, dy+36, barW, 4, COL_GRAY_2);
  4917	    lcd.fillRect(dx+10, dy+36, prog,  4, 0xF800);
  4918	  } else {
  4919	    neoOff();
  4920	    resetAllButtons();showPhotoView(photoViewIndex);return;
  4921	  }
  4922	  if(!evt.valid) return;
  4923	  if(evt.pin==BTN_BOOT) { neoOff(); photoViewDeleteCurrent(); }
  4924	  else { neoOff(); resetAllButtons(); showPhotoView(photoViewIndex); }
  4925	}
  4926	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  4927	//  LOOP
  4928	// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  4929	void loop(){
  4930	  neoTick();
  4931	  esp_task_wdt_reset();
  4932
  4933	  if (g_hdrWaiting && (millis() - g_hdrFirstMs >= HDR_DOUBLE_TAP_MS)) {
  4934	    g_hdrWaiting = false;
  4935	    if (appMode == MODE_VIEWFINDER) runHDRFlow();
  4936	  }
  4937
  4938	  btPlayerTick();
  4939	  tickAllButtons();
  4940
  4941	  ButtonEvent evtBoot={},evtB={},evtC={},evtD={};
  4942	  btnBoot.pollEvent(evtBoot);
  4943	  btnB.pollEvent(evtB);
  4944	  btnC.pollEvent(evtC);
  4945	  btnD.pollEvent(evtD);
  4946
  4947	  ButtonEvent singleEvt={};
  4948	  if     (evtBoot.valid) singleEvt=evtBoot;
  4949	  else if(evtB.valid)    singleEvt=evtB;
  4950	  else if(evtC.valid)    singleEvt=evtC;
  4951	  else if(evtD.valid)    singleEvt=evtD;
  4952
  4953	  if(appMode==MODE_PHOTO_VIEW&&photoViewCaptionVisible&&millis()>photoViewCaptionUntilMs)
  4954	    photoViewClearCaption();
  4955
  4956	  // [PORTED v6.1] MPU tick
  4957	  mpuTick();
  4958	  if(usbModeActive){if(evtBoot.valid) exitUSBMode();return;}
  4959	  if(recActive){if(evtB.valid) stopRecording();else recordFrame();return;}
  4960
  4961	  switch(appMode){
  4962	    case MODE_VIEWFINDER:      handleModeViewfinder(singleEvt);                break;
  4963	    case MODE_GALLERY:         handleModeGallery(singleEvt);                   break;
  4964	    case MODE_PHOTO_VIEW:      handleModePhotoView(singleEvt);                 break;
  4965	    case MODE_MJPEG_PLAYER:    handleModeMjpegPlayer(evtBoot,evtB,evtC,evtD); break;
  4966	    case MODE_MENU_LED:        handleModeMenuLed(singleEvt);                   break;
  4967	    case MODE_MENU_EXP:        handleModeMenuExp(singleEvt);                   break;
  4968	    case MODE_MENU_EXP_ADJ:    handleModeMenuExpAdj(singleEvt);                break;
  4969	    case MODE_DIALOG_DELETE:   handleModeDialogDelete(singleEvt);              break;
  4970	    case MODE_DIALOG_MULTI_DELETE: handleModeDialogMultiDelete(singleEvt);        break;
  4971	    case MODE_MENU_FORMAT:     handleModeMenuFormat(singleEvt);                break;
  4972	    case MODE_JUMP_INPUT:      handleModeJumpInput(evtBoot,evtB,evtC,evtD);    break;
  4973	    case MODE_FEATURES:        handleModeFeatures(singleEvt);                  break;
  4974	    case MODE_AI_FEATURE_MENU: handleModeAIFeatureMenu(evtBoot,evtB,evtC,evtD);break;
  4975	    case MODE_AI_DESCRIBE:     handleModeAIDescribe(evtB,evtC,evtD);           break;
  4976	    case MODE_AI_NO_CONFIG:    handleModeAINoConfig(evtB,evtD);                break;
  4977	    case MODE_KEY_MANAGER:     handleModeKeyManager(evtBoot,evtB,evtC,evtD);  break;
  4978	    case MODE_BT_SCAN:         handleModeBTScan(singleEvt);                    break;
  4979	    case MODE_BT_MP3_LIST:     handleModeBTMP3List(singleEvt);                break;
  4980	    case MODE_BT_MP3_PLAYER:   handleModeBTMP3Player(singleEvt);              break;
  4981	  }
  4982	  if (appMode != MODE_VIEWFINDER) {
  4983	    bool isMenu = (appMode == MODE_JUMP_INPUT || appMode == MODE_AI_DESCRIBE || appMode == MODE_AI_NO_CONFIG || appMode == MODE_AI_FEATURE_MENU || appMode == MODE_KEY_MANAGER || appMode == MODE_FEATURES || appMode == MODE_MENU_EXP || appMode == MODE_MENU_LED || appMode == MODE_MENU_FORMAT || appMode == MODE_MENU_EXP_ADJ || appMode == MODE_DIALOG_MULTI_DELETE || appMode == MODE_BT_SCAN || appMode == MODE_BT_MP3_LIST || appMode == MODE_BT_MP3_PLAYER);
  4984	    if (!isMenu) islandNoClear = false;
  4985	    islandTick();
  4986	  }
  4987
  4988	}
